Conclusion: 预训练数据的统计属性会因果性地驱动启发式的形成，并影响模型何时、以何种路径学会正确任务。小规模合成实验能够揭示这些机制，也可能预测更大规模模型的训练动态。

Abstract: How does data shape language model (LM) behavior throughout pretraining? We investigate this question through a case study on entity comparison, e.g., Between France and Brazil, which country is larger?, across nine comparison tasks spanning five entity types. We begin with controlled experiments in which we train small LMs (163M parameters) on mixtures of natural text from pretraining corpora and synthetic task data. Our analysis identifies three distinct phases of learning: (1) an early phase where the LM selects entities by frequency, (2) a middle phase where the LM selects entities by position in the prompt (first vs. last mentioned entity), and (3) a late phase where the LM selects the entity that is the correct answer. We show that the emergence of these three phases is causally determined by statistical properties of the training data. With small amounts of task-specific synthetic data, we observe only the first two phases and the model fails to learn the task; with large amounts, the model jumps directly from the frequency-based heuristic to solving the task correctly. Moreover, changing the entity frequency distribution from a naturally occurring Zipfian distribution (a small number of entities are very common and the vast majority are rare) to a uniform one weakens the frequency phase and speeds up learning of the task. Lastly, we show that the three phases of training dynamics from our controlled experiments appear in the open source model OLMo-2 32B. Together, our findings demonstrate that properties of pretraining data are causal drivers of heuristic learning and show that small-scale synthetic experiments can predict training dynamics at larger scales.

</details>


### [71] [Human or Synthetic? What a Fixed Budget Buys for Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16895)
*Shourik Banerjee,Ethan Mendes,Mohit Raghavendra,Wei Xu,Alan Ritter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在相同标注预算下，合成偏好数据在低至中等预算时更具成本效益；人工数据在有帮助性任务上需投入约百倍预算才追平，在摘要任务上则未能追平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常固定偏好样本数量来比较人工标注数据与合成数据，忽略两者单条样本成本差异巨大，因而无法公平衡量实际预算下的性能表现。本文旨在直接考察标注成本与模型效果之间的关系。

Method: 研究者在固定且金额相等的标注预算下，比较使用人工偏好数据或合成偏好数据进行后训练的效果。实验覆盖多个基础模型系列，并评估有帮助性和摘要任务；后训练结合监督微调与偏好优化。此外，研究还检验不同人工偏好数据来源的可靠性，并考察领域限制和去重等常见处理方式的影响。

Result: 合成数据在低至中等预算下明显更具成本效益，仅约200至400美元即可取得较高胜率。在有帮助性任务中，人工数据只有在预算约高出两个数量级时才缩小差距；在摘要任务中，人工数据在所有测试预算下都未能追平。不同人工数据来源的质量差异显著，其中一个常用来源明显弱于另一个，且进行领域限制和去重后，这一差距仍然存在。

Conclusion: 偏好数据的比较应同时考虑样本效果与获取成本，而不能只固定样本数量。就本文测试的任务与预算而言，合成偏好数据是更具成本效益的后训练选择；人工数据的表现还取决于数据来源质量，常见清理方法不足以消除来源间差异。

Abstract: Post-training of large language models typically combines supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with preference-based fine-tuning such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). Human-annotated and synthetically generated preference data differ substantially in per-sample cost, but prior work typically holds sample count fixed when comparing the two. In this work, we explicitly study the interplay between cost and performance by comparing the effect of post-training with human and synthetic preference data under a fixed, dollar-matched annotation budget. We train and evaluate multiple base model families across helpfulness and summarization tasks. We find that synthetic data is far more cost-efficient at low-to-moderate budgets, achieving high win rates with as little as \$200--\$400. On helpfulness, human data closes the gap only at budgets roughly two orders of magnitude larger; on summarization, it does not close the gap at any tested budget. We also find that human-annotated data varies in reliability: one widely used human preference source substantially underperforms another, and the gap persists even after standard remedies such as domain restriction and deduplication.

</details>


### [72] [Beyond Word Error Rate: A Failure Taxonomy of Streaming Dictation ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16919)
*Harras Mansoor,Muhammad Wisal,Hassan Bhatti,Masood Shakir*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文比较多种语音识别系统在实时听写中的实际表现，指出传统词错误率无法充分反映用户需要修正的错误；低语音音量、跨语言内容和语音检测造成的漏识别尤其值得关注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统评测通常先去除标点、大小写和数字格式，再以词错误率衡量识别效果。但实时听写会直接把结果写入用户消息，因此错误类型及其修正负担同样重要。论文旨在更贴近实际使用场景地比较系统，并揭示相近词错误率背后的不同失误模式。

Method: 研究将录音流式输入十一种语音识别系统，测试材料包括单人朗读的英语听写（涵盖安静环境、风扇背景声和低声说话）、多种语言与方言片段，以及双语录音。研究按固定规则将错误分为识别、输出格式、语言处理和自建系统的音频丢失四类；另使用一百零七名说话者的低声语音数据进行检验，并比较不同语音检测设置及语言选择设置的影响。代码、录音、两套参考文本和全部识别结果均已公开。

Result: 词错误率相近的系统，实际错误模式可能差异很大。低声英语对部分系统影响显著：有系统漏掉约四分之一的词，即使放宽或关闭轮次结束检测，漏词比例仍然较高；另一些系统漏词不超过百分之三。自建部署中的某系统低声识别错误率较高，放宽语音检测后明显下降。在更大规模的说话者数据中，部分系统仍漏掉百分之七至三十一的低声词语，而另一些系统低于百分之二。只选择英语时，若干系统会在绝大多数双语录音中漏掉非英语部分；同时选择两种语言可减少此问题。语音活动检测器若将低声语音误判为静音并删除，会使词错误率大幅升高。

Conclusion: 评估实时听写系统不能只看经过格式归一化的词错误率，还应分别考察低声漏识别、双语处理、语音检测和输出格式等错误，因为这些因素直接决定用户的修改成本。论文结果也表明，语音活动检测及语言设置可能显著影响实际表现。不过，英语结果主要来自一名朗读者，推广到更广泛说话者和自然对话时仍需谨慎。

Abstract: Speech recognition (ASR) systems are usually compared by word error rate (WER) on recorded files, after punctuation, capital letters and number format are removed. In dictation, the audio is streamed live and the text goes into the user's message as written, so what matters is which mistakes the user has to fix. The test set, streamed to eleven ASR systems, comprises 106 scripted English dictations from one speaker (read in a quiet room, with a fan on, and whispered), 20 clips in each of eleven other language and dialect sets, and 400 recordings that join clips in two languages. Every error was sorted by a fixed rule into four levels: recognition, output formatting, language handling and, for the self-hosted models, audio dropped before the model. Systems with similar WER failed in different ways. Zoom Scribe and OpenAI's GPT-Live-Transcribe have almost the same WER on quiet English (7.4% and 7.3%), but 34.7% and 8.8% on whispered English. Zoom and both AssemblyAI models left out 26-28% of whispered words, and still 21-26% with their end-of-turn detection relaxed or off; seven other systems left out at most 3%. Qwen3-ASR's 32% came from the speech detection in our own deployment (6% when relaxed). Across 107 EARS speakers, the same four systems lost 7-31% of whispered words, while six others lost under 2%. With only English selected, Gemini, Deepgram and our self-hosted Whisper lost the non-English part of a two-language recording in 86-99% of recordings, against 33-46% with both languages selected, while GPT-Live-Transcribe, OpenAI's realtime Whisper and Meta lost it in at most 20%. Running Whisper behind a Silero voice activity detector that drops audio judged to be silence raised whispered WER from 10.1% to 51.4%. Most English results come from one read speaker. The code, the recordings, both references and every transcript are released.

</details>


### [73] [SPADE: Soft Prompts for Ai-text DEtection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16929)
*Alex Jiang,Didier Schwab,Chuyuan Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用提示词对文本解释能力进行检测的框架，通过为不同类别训练软提示，提升人工智能生成文本检测在分布外数据上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测方法主要分析文本本身：有监督方法依赖带标签的训练文本，零样本方法依赖文本概率分布。它们忽略了提示词对生成过程的影响，因此在开放环境和跨数据集场景中的鲁棒性受限。

Method: 提出人工智能文本检测软提示框架。方法以点互信息刻画提示词对候选文本的解释程度，并据此训练每个类别对应的软提示，以增强类别区分。检测时，选择最能解释候选文本的类别提示词作为预测结果。

Result: 摘要称，该方法在多个基准数据集上与多种检测器进行了广泛比较，表现出较强的鲁棒性和有效性，并在分布外数据集上取得当前最佳表现。

Conclusion: 将提示词对文本生成的影响纳入检测信号，为人工智能生成文本检测提供了新的思路。实验结果表明，基于类别软提示和点互信息的框架有望改善跨数据集泛化，尤其适用于分布变化场景。

Abstract: The easy access to Large Language Models (LLMs) has made AI-generated texts on the web increasingly ubiquitous, making AI-text detection a crucial task. Prompts greatly influence the generation process, but existing detectors focus solely on the generated texts --- supervised methods learn from labeled texts only, and zero-shot methods rely on the text's probability distributions --- ignoring the underlying generation process, which limits their robustness in open-world scenarios. We introduce SPADE: Soft Prompts for Ai-text DEtection, a novel framework for detecting AI-generated text that transfers better across datasets. Specifically, we measure the prompt's influence and use it as a discriminative signal. We characterize the prompt's influence by introducing a Pointwise Mutual Information (PMI) term that captures how much a prompt explains a text. Based on this quantity, SPADE trains one soft prompt per class to amplify the separation, and predicts the class whose prompt best explains the candidate text. Extensive experiments on several benchmarks against various detectors demonstrate the robustness and effectiveness of our method, achieving state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on out-of-distribution (OOD) datasets.

</details>


### [74] [Lara Mini Base: Language Diversity as a Resource, Not a Trade-off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16956)

Totaro,Barbara Veronika Totzauer,Alessio Tranzocchi,Letizia Treossi,Mara Trombetti,Marco Trombetti,Andrea Valenzi,Kirti Vashee,Giulia Vestita,Teodosio Vezza,Giulia Vignola,Sara Virtuoso,Valentina Volpini,Francesca Vurro,Jolita Zaksauskaite,Giulia Zanarella,Paolo Zanotti,Francesca Zavettieri,Irene Zorzi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出拉拉迷你基础模型：以混合专家架构训练，兼顾英语及44种其他语言，在较低激活参数规模下取得强劲的多语、数学、代码和英语基准成绩，并具备较快推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用语言模型的预训练数据主要是英语，非英语数据占比通常偏低；专为欧洲语言训练的模型虽使用更多相关语言数据，但同等规模下表现不及通用模型。研究关注的核心问题是：小模型中不同语言是否会争夺有限的模型容量，以及如何以较低成本提升多语能力。

Method: 从零开始训练拉拉迷你基础模型，使用总计6万亿个词元，涵盖英语和另外44种语言，覆盖欧盟全部24种官方语言。训练过程中，其他44种语言的数据总量逐渐超过英语。模型采用混合专家架构，总参数量为72亿，每个词元激活约6亿参数；支持最长12.8万词元的上下文。研究还将常识推理基准翻译为110种语言变体，计划发布改进版本。

Result: 在激活参数不超过20亿的开放基础模型中，该模型在英语、多语、数学和代码标准基准上的平均成绩最高，超过了使用多出4至6倍训练词元的模型。优势在其支持语言的多语基准和数学基准上最为明显。研究还发现，混合专家架构能够缓解语言间的容量竞争。单用户使用单张图形处理器且不量化时，其文本生成速度至少比任何平均成绩更高的模型快1.8倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明，增加多语训练数据并结合混合专家架构，可以在较低激活参数规模下提升多语模型的综合表现，同时降低语言间的容量竞争并保持较高推理效率。作者将以署名、非商业使用、相同方式共享的4.0国际许可协议发布模型权重，并计划发布多语种常识推理基准。

Abstract: General-purpose language models are trained mostly on English: over open models we review, at most, non-English pretraining data makes up only a 12% share. Models built for European languages give them far more, but score well below general-purpose models of the same size. A common concern is that, in small models, languages compete for capacity. We present Lara Mini base, a model trained from scratch on 6T tokens in English and 44 other languages, which together cover all 24 official languages of the European Union. As training progresses, the 44 other languages together come to outweigh English. To give these languages enough capacity at low cost, Lara Mini base uses a mixture of experts architecture with 7.2B total and 0.6B active parameters. Among open base models with up to 2B active parameters, it achieves the highest average score across standard English, multilingual, math, and code benchmarks, ahead of models trained on 4 to 6 times as many tokens. Its margin is largest on multilingual benchmarks in the languages it supports and on math benchmarks. It supports contexts of up to 128k tokens. We find that our MoE architecture reduces capacity competition between languages. For a single user on one GPU, without quantization, it generates text at least 1.8 times faster than any model with a higher average score. We release the weights of Lara Mini base under the CC BY-NC-SA 4.0 license. In addition, we will release T-HellaSwag, a higher-quality machine translation of the commonsense reasoning benchmark HellaSwag into 110 language variants.

</details>


### [75] [Morphological Simplification in Alzheimer's Disease: Computational Analysis and Identification From Connected Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16968)
*Mohamed Zakaria Kurdi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，阿尔茨海默病患者的连贯言语呈现形态简化和时间表达变化；形态特征具有分类价值，但模型效果对特征筛选方式敏感，跨语料迁移能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形态通常被认为在阿尔茨海默病中相对保留，因此较少用于连贯言语分析。研究旨在识别能够区分患者与健康对照的形态特征，并检验其分类效能是否优于其他语言层面或预训练语言模型，以及这些效应是否只是文本长度和词汇差异造成的。

Method: 研究分析了78名患者和78名健康对照描述“偷饼干”图片时的言语，计算涵盖八类的55项自动形态指标，其中七类为新提出、具有理论依据的指标。研究采用组间比较、等效性检验、特征贡献分析、类别消融、似然比检验和特征筛选分类器，并与预训练语言模型比较；同时进行人工验证，并将模型迁移至另外两个语料库。

Result: 38项可比较指标中有19项存在组间差异：患者使用的带后缀词和屈折词较少，动词与名词较短，现在进行时较少、过去时较多，最大效应量为负0.42；错误很少，且两组在错误率上统计等效。控制文本长度和词频后，仍有8项指标保留差异。采用测试集筛选特征时，形态模型的宏平均F1值为0.85，与句法模型相当，高于微调语言模型的0.80；但完全交叉验证筛选时降至0.62。形态特征加入语言模型后提高了曲线下面积（0.89升至0.91），却未提高宏平均F1值。跨语料迁移有限；在轻度认知障碍数据中，约三分之二的效应方向一致。

Conclusion: 阿尔茨海默病患者的连贯言语并非出现广泛的形态错误，而更体现为形态简化及对时间表达方式的重新安排。形态信息比既往通常认为的更有助于识别疾病，但分类结果会受到特征筛选流程影响，且跨语料泛化仍需谨慎评估。

Abstract: Purpose: Morphology is often regarded as relatively spared in Alzheimer's disease (AD) and is rarely modeled in connected speech. This study asked which morphological features distinguish speakers with AD from healthy controls, how well morphology classifies AD compared with other linguistic levels and BERT-based models, and whether the signal exceeds transcript length and vocabulary. Method: Fifty-five automatic measures in eight families, seven of them new and theory-motivated, were computed from participant speech in the ADReSS Cookie Theft descriptions (78 AD, 78 controls) and analyzed with group comparisons, equivalence tests, SHAP, family ablation, likelihood-ratio tests, feature-selection classifiers, and BERT-based models. The automatic measures were validated manually, and models were transferred to two other corpora. Results: Nineteen of 38 measures differed between groups: speakers with AD produced fewer suffixed and inflected words, shorter verbs and nouns, and fewer present-progressive but more past-tense forms (largest effect r = -0.42), whereas errors were rare and statistically equivalent between groups. Eight measures survived adjustment for length and lexical frequency. With test-set feature selection, morphology reached a macro-F1 of 0.85, on par with syntax and above fine-tuned BERT (0.80 over five seeds); fully cross-validated selection gave 0.62. Adding morphology to BERT improved AUC (0.91 vs. 0.89) but not macro-F1. Transfer was limited, although two thirds of the effects kept their direction in mild cognitive impairment. Conclusions: AD connected speech shows morphological simplification and temporal reanchoring rather than breakdown. Morphology is more informative than its reputation suggests.
  Keywords: Alzheimer's disease; morphology; connected speech; ADReSS; inflection; tense and aspect; SHAP; machine learning; BERT

</details>


### [76] [Auditing AI peer reviewers: a benchmark on real scientific papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17052)
*Íñigo Zubeldia,Boris Bolliet,Francisco Villaescusa-Navarro,Pablo Villanueva-Domingo,Miles Cranmer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文构建了可控基准，评估大语言模型审稿人发现论文错误的能力及误报情况，并提出借助检索、符号计算和代码执行进行核验的多智能体审稿系统。结果显示，模型选择对检错率影响最大；工具能在特定任务上提升表现，但效果因学科和错误类型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的同行评审已投入实际应用，但其评估仍不充分。仅凭总体评价难以判断系统能否发现具体错误，也无法同时衡量不同错误类型、严重程度下的检出能力及对无误论文的误报风险。

Method: 研究者在真实论文中注入四类可定位的客观错误，包括一般问题、数学、数值和引文错误，并设置三个严重程度；同时保留无错误副本，以计算各类错误的检出率和误报率。研究还提出多智能体审稿系统“斯凯普提卡尔”，通过检索被引论文核查引文相关主张、用计算机代数系统核验数学内容，并执行代码验证数值主张。基准应用于天体物理学和机器学习领域各十五篇近期预印本，共注入三百五十七处错误，并比较十二种审稿系统。

Result: 在相同提示条件下，三种前沿模型在两个领域的总体检出率相差约五十个百分点，表明模型选择是影响检错表现的首要因素。工具和流程设计在弥补模型自身能力不足时能带来帮助：在天体物理学中，代码助手配置提高了数学和数值错误的检出能力；强制检索引文使“斯凯普提卡尔”的引文错误检出率显著领先其他系统。但在机器学习领域，这一引文优势并未出现，其表现与最佳单模型审稿人的差异在统计上不显著。多数系统的误报率低于百分之十，不过误报会随提示、模型和辅助流程而变化，数学错误标记是主要误报来源。

Conclusion: 该基准为评估大语言模型审稿提供了更细致、可复现的检验方式，并揭示审稿表现取决于模型能力、学科背景和工具配置，而非单纯增加工具即可普遍改善。引文检索等外部核验机制在特定领域尤其有效，但仍需关注数学类误报。研究同时公开评估流程、注入错误及裁定记录，便于后续比较与改进。

Abstract: LLM-based peer review is now used at production scale, but its evaluation remains limited. We introduce a benchmark that injects objective, localisable errors of four types (general issues, mathematics, numerics, citations) at three severity levels into real papers and measures each reviewer's detection rate by error type and severity, together with its false-positive rate on clean copies. We also present Skepthical, a multi-agent reviewer that verifies citation-backed claims by retrieving the cited papers, mathematics with computer algebra, and numerical claims with executed code. We apply the benchmark to two corpora of 15 recent arXiv preprints each, one in astrophysics and one in machine learning (ML), injecting a total of 357 errors, and evaluate twelve reviewer systems: six single-LLM reviewers, two Claude Code configurations, three publicly available reviewers, and Skepthical. The choice of model has the largest impact on detection rate: given the same prompt, three frontier LLMs differ by about 50 percentage points in total detection on both corpora. Tools and scaffolding help when they contribute what the model cannot supply on its own. In particular, on astrophysics, Claude Code improves on a single Claude call in mathematics and numerics detection, and enforced citation retrieval gives Skepthical the highest citation error detection rate of all systems by a large margin. This citation lead, however, is not observed on ML, where the models appear to know the literature better and Skepthical's citation detection rate is statistically indistinguishable from that of the best single LLM. False-positive rates are below 10% for most systems, but vary with prompt, model, and scaffolding, with mathematics flags the main source of false positives. The evaluation pipeline, the injected errors, and the adjudication logs are released as a benchmark.

</details>


### [77] [Evaluating Pretrained Language Semantics for Structured EHR Prediction Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17053)
*Ashvin Gupta,Brendan Delaney,Alessandra Russo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出纵向电子健康记录建模方法“时间电子健康记录”，将临床事件的自然语言描述与数值信息输入预训练因果语言模型，通过持续预训练和任务微调提升多类临床预测表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统结构化电子健康记录模型常将临床概念视为数据集专属标记，忽略其自然语言含义，因此难以直接利用已有医学语义知识，往往只能从记录中的共同出现关系学习概念关联。论文旨在借助预训练语言模型的语义能力，改进纵向患者记录的表示与预测。

Method: 将患者的临床事件表示为自然语言描述及相关数值，先进行适应电子健康记录语料的持续预训练，再使用低秩适配方法开展具体任务的判别式微调，并将语言模型作为纵向患者编码器。研究在四项临床预测任务及胰腺癌预测任务上进行评估，并通过消融实验分析模型组成的作用。

Result: 在所评估任务中，以“密斯特拉尔七十亿参数零点三版”为基础模型的方法总体表现最佳。消融结果显示，移除或错误对齐临床描述、遗漏数值信息，或去掉任一适配阶段，都会降低预测表现；显式加入经过时间描述则几乎没有额外收益。患者表示还呈现出与疾病风险及临床病史模式相吻合的结构。

Conclusion: 研究表明，保留临床事件的自然语言语义，并结合面向电子健康记录的持续预训练与任务微调，有助于提升纵向临床预测能力。临床描述和数值信息是有效建模的重要组成部分，而时间间隔的显式文本化在本研究中价值有限。

Abstract: Structured Electronic Health Records (EHR) models typically treat each clinical concept as a dataset-specific token, discarding the natural-language semantics associated with the event. As a result, relationships between concepts must largely be learned from EHR co-occurrence, without directly exploiting any prior semantic knowledge. We investigate how pretrained causal language models can be adapted for longitudinal EHR prediction by developing tEHR. Here, we represent events with their natural language description and use this text stream to apply EHR-adaptive continued pretraining, followed by task-specific discriminative fine-tuning using LoRA, utilising the LLM as a longitudinal patient-encoder. We evaluate this approach across four prediction tasks on the MIMIC-IV dataset and pancreatic cancer prediction on the CPRD dataset at 1 to 12 month horizons, with tEHR using the Mistral-7B-v0.3 backbone achieving the strongest overall performance. Ablations show that performance declines when clinical descriptions are removed or misaligned, when numerical values are omitted, and when either stage of adaptation is removed, while explicit elapsed-time descriptions provide little additional benefit. Analysis of the learned patient representations further reveals clinically coherent structure associated with distinct patterns of disease risk and clinical history.

</details>


### [78] [Discovering Effective Agent Memory Designs through Program Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17068)
*Xinheng Ding,Seyyed Saeid Cheshmi,Ziyang Zhang,Tairan Chen,Wenya Xie,Yu Hou,Rui Zhang,Zirui Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过程序演化自动发现智能体记忆设计的方法，在多个基准测试中以更低服务成本取得更高准确率，并总结出两项有效的记忆设计规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体记忆的效果取决于多种设计选择，但手工设计难以系统探索这些选择及其作用。本文旨在通过自动演化完整记忆程序，发现有效设计并分析其背后的原因。

Method: 提出一种记忆程序演化框架：从精简初始程序出发，借助标准化生命周期接口以及反复进行的诊断与方案提出流程迭代改进记忆程序。研究在多个记忆基准上评估性能，并通过实证分析、消融实验和模块移植检验设计规律及其跨系统适用性；同时考察将服务成本纳入优化目标后的计算分配变化。

Result: 在两个记忆基准上，该框架相较手工设计基线的准确率最高提升十二个百分点，服务成本约为基线的百分之三十五；实验还显示，所得方法能够迁移至不同服务模型和基准。两项反复出现的有效模式是：保留原始对话作为可检索证据，以补足提取式记忆遗漏的细节；根据查询意图组织检索证据，帮助回答模型有效利用信息。将证据组织模块移植到五种既有记忆系统后，五者的准确率均有提升。将成本与准确率共同纳入目标，则促使计算从基于大语言模型的记忆构建转向查询时的选择性处理，同时保持有竞争力的准确率。

Conclusion: 程序演化能够系统探索智能体记忆设计，并揭示可迁移、可复用的设计规律。保留原始证据、按查询意图组织证据，以及依据成本目标调整计算分配，都是构建高效记忆系统的重要思路。

Abstract: Program evolution provides a promising approach to discover agent memory designs and study the choices underlying their effectiveness. We introduce MAPE, a framework that evolves whole memory programs from minimal seeds through a standardized lifecycle interface and an iterative diagnosis-proposal loop. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval yield an absolute accuracy gain of up to 12% over manually designed baselines at 35% of the serving cost, with further evaluations supporting transfer across serving backbones and benchmarks. Empirical analyses and ablations identify and validate two recurring design patterns: retaining raw conversations as retrievable evidence preserves details omitted from extracted memories, while composing retrieved evidence according to query intent helps the answering model use it effectively. Transplanting the composition module into five existing memory systems improves accuracy across all five. Beyond these patterns, the search objective shapes where computation goes: optimizing serving cost alongside accuracy shifts computation from LLM-based memory construction to selective processing at query time while retaining competitive accuracy. Together, these findings offer empirical lessons for agent memory design. Code is available at https://github.com/ash-ding/MAPE.

</details>


### [79] [Optimal Single-History Tokenization Disambiguation for Provably Secure Linguistic Steganography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17091)
*Feng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文证明：若接收方必须在完整支持的语言模型下无误恢复消息，且只保留单一令牌历史，就必须牺牲前缀停止决策所携带的信息。作者提出共享随机数的“硬币投影”方案，达到这一理论上限；实验显示其利用率显著优于同步池方法，并能恢复完整消息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可证明安全的语言隐写通常假设接收方能还原发送方使用的令牌，但实际文本信道只传递文本，多个令牌序列可能对应同一文本，导致提取歧义。既有方案要么改变语言分布，要么同步大批令牌而使容量随词表扩大而骤降，要么维护多条令牌历史。论文旨在厘清无歧义提取的根本容量限制，并构造兼顾文本分布与提取可靠性的方案。

Method: 作者证明，在完整支持的语言模型下，若要求精确、无差错提取且接收方只维护一条历史，那么每一步的候选集合必须是前缀无歧义的；即使接收方查看后续全部文本、发送方也约束后续生成，该结论仍成立。因此，接收方必须放弃“停止熵”，即判断某个前缀令牌是否在此结束所包含的信息。基于此前缀树上的停止决策，作者利用共享随机数固定这些决策，提出闭式的“硬币投影”方案。该方案兼容任意分布编码器，每个令牌只需调用一次模型，且保持普通采样分布。

Result: 在英语、中文和日语语料中，存在歧义的文本虽较少，每千个令牌最多约十一处，但词语与词素前缀上的停止熵在每一步仍占总熵的百分之七点二至百分之九。同步池方法的额外损失主要来自空格和单字节UTF-8枢纽：它们会合并前缀子树，却几乎不携带停止不确定性。硬币投影在各种词表规模下都保留编码器字符串熵的百分之九十一至九十三；使用完整词表时，其转换为消息比特的熵是同步池方法的二点一六至六点八五倍，并能恢复每条已完成消息。

Conclusion: 无误提取与单一历史的约束决定了语言隐写必须付出停止熵这一理论代价。硬币投影通过只固定必要的前缀停止决策，达到该界限，同时避免同步池因词表扩大而造成的严重容量损失，是一种高效且保持原始采样分布的隐写编码方法。

Abstract: Provably secure linguistic steganography assumes that the receiver recovers the sender's tokens, but a text channel delivers only text, and ambiguous tokenizations break extraction. Existing fixes change the distribution, synchronize whole pools of tokens at a capacity that collapses with large vocabularies, or track several token histories. We prove that if extraction is exact and error-free across full-support language models, a single-history receiver is confined to a prefix-free candidate set at every step -- even if it reads the entire future text and the sender constrains later steps -- and must therefore give up the stop entropy, the entropy of deciding whether each prefix token ends there. Fixing these stop decisions on the prefix forest with shared randomness yields coin projection: a closed-form scheme that attains the bound, works with any distribution coder in one model call per token, preserves ordinary sampling, and refines SyncPool for every key. Across English, Chinese and Japanese, ambiguous text is rare (at most 11 in 1,000 tokens), yet the stop entropy at word and morpheme prefixes takes 7.2-9.0% of the entropy at every step; SyncPool's further loss comes from space and single-byte UTF-8 hubs that merge subtrees while carrying almost no stop uncertainty. Coin projection keeps 91-93% of the string entropy for the coder at every vocabulary size; with the full vocabulary, it turns 2.16-6.85 times as much entropy into message bits as SyncPool and recovers every completed message.

</details>


### [80] [Disentangling Spurious Correlations: How IRM Handles Diverse Shortcuts in Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17107)
*Paul Grunenwald,Benoît Crabbé,Guillaume Wisniewski,Pirmin Lemberger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文从因果视角系统评估不变风险最小化抑制自然语言处理模型文本捷径的能力，发现该方法在多种成因与真实偏差场景中有效，但对有语义价值的特征需谨慎调节不变性惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验风险最小化训练的模型可能依赖方言标记等偶然相关特征，而非稳健的语言规律，导致分布外泛化能力下降。尽管不变风险最小化旨在利用跨环境稳定的预测关系抑制捷径，其在自然语言处理中的实际效果仍有争议，因此需要通过多种因果机制加以检验。

Method: 研究构建因果驱动的评估框架，考察由标签生成特征、混杂因素和选择偏差引发的捷径，并比较不同因果与反因果结构下不变风险最小化的表现，同时分析环境变化有限及标签噪声增加时的效果。随后将评估扩展到真实文本场景，包括职业与性别关联数据集中的偏差，以及行动者相关数据集中的语义负载姓名捷径。

Result: 在受控实验中，不变风险最小化能够跨越所评估的因果与反因果结构排除捷径，即使环境差异有限、标签噪声增加也能发挥作用。在真实文本实验中，该方法也能缓解职业性别关联和行动者姓名带来的捷径影响；但对具有实际语义意义的特征，惩罚过强可能损害有用信息，因此需要仔细校准。

Conclusion: 不变风险最小化在多种捷径形成机制下具有抑制伪相关、改善分布外泛化的潜力，其适用性并不限于合成特征。不过，实际应用必须在去除不稳定捷径与保留有意义语义特征之间取得平衡，并合理设定不变性惩罚。

Abstract: In NLP, models trained via Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM) often exploit spurious correlations, such as dialectal markers, at the expense of robust linguistic features, thereby degrading their out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization. Invariant Risk Minimization (IRM) aims to suppress such shortcuts by identifying predictive relationships that remain stable across environments, but its practical utility for NLP remains debated. We use a causality-driven framework to evaluate IRM under three shortcut-generating mechanisms: label-generated features, confounding, and selection bias. Our results show that IRM successfully rejects shortcuts across the evaluated causal and anti-causal structures, including under limited environmental variation and increasing label noise. We then extend our analysis beyond controlled synthetic artifacts to two less constrained settings: gender-profession associations in Bias in Bios and semantically loaded actor-name shortcuts in ActorCorr. These experiments show that invariant learning can mitigate more realistic textual shortcuts, while semantically meaningful features require careful calibration of the invariance penalty.

</details>


### [81] [Parameter-Efficient Conciseness Adaptation: Differentiable Length Control for Concise Clinical Reasoning in Smaller Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17128)
*Tanzeel Abbas,Ahmad Kamran Malik,Nafees Qamar,Sana Liaqat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需偏好数据、奖励模型或外部教师的参数高效训练方法，在缩短医疗问答回复的同时提升诊断准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型便于在普通硬件上部署临床自然语言处理，但经过训练后，面对医疗问题仍常给出过长回答。在临床决策支持和床旁使用等时间紧迫的场景中，冗长内容不易快速采取行动。现有控制回答长度的方法往往依赖偏好优化、奖励模型或专有教师，增加了成本并削弱了小模型易部署的优势。

Method: 论文提出参数高效简洁性适配方法，将一个可微的简洁性项加入量化低秩适配监督微调目标。由于词元数量离散且不可微，该方法不直接优化长度，而是在生成超过预设词元预算后提高结束序列标记的概率，以此提供平滑训练信号。整个过程不需要偏好数据、辅助模型或专用计算设备，可在单张普通显卡上完成。

Result: 在基于医疗推理监督微调语料训练的十七亿参数模型上，该方法将平均回复长度减少百分之七十二点八，同时将多项选择诊断准确率从百分之四十点八提高到百分之四十四点零。对简洁性权重进行调整，还能得到清晰可解释的准确率与长度权衡曲线。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过在常规监督微调中加入简洁性训练信号，可以让小型医疗语言模型显著减少冗余回答，并在所报告的实验中提升诊断准确率。该方法为资源受限的临床部署提供了兼顾简洁性与性能的途径。

Abstract: Smaller language models (SLMs) make it feasible to deploy clinical natural language processing where large proprietary models are too costly, and parameter-efficient fine-tuning allows them to be specialised on commodity hardware. One obstacle persists after training: on medical tasks these models answer at far greater length than the clinical question requires. In clinical decision support and point-of-care use, where time is short, such verbosity is hard to act on even when the underlying reasoning is sound. Existing length-control methods depend on preference optimisation, reward models, or proprietary teachers that undercut the accessibility making SLMs attractive. We introduce Parameter-Efficient Conciseness Adaptation (PECA), which adds a single differentiable conciseness term to the QLoRA supervised fine-tuning objective. Because the token count is discrete and non-differentiable, PECA instead raises the end-of-sequence probability once generation exceeds a token budget, a smooth signal optimised during ordinary training, with no preference data, no auxiliary model, and a single commodity GPU. On a 1.7-billion-parameter model fine-tuned on the medical-o1-reasoning-SFT corpus, PECA reduces mean response length by 72.8% while improving multiple-choice diagnostic accuracy from 40.8% to 44.0%. A sweep over the conciseness strength traces an interpretable accuracy-to-length frontier for balancing brevity and diagnostic completeness.

</details>


### [82] [Parsed, Plausible, Wrong: Output Format Controls Silent Errors in Small-Model Semantic Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17169)
*Umar Ahmed,Maxim Dunaev,Mohammad Nour Al Awad,Sergey Ivanov,Ivan Khodnenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在点餐解析任务中，输出格式会影响小模型的正确率与静默错误率；自然语言词语格式优于原生树结构格式，而单纯缩短编码并无收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析器可能输出形式合法、内容却错误的树，且下游系统难以察觉这类静默错误。研究因此考察：在目标树信息完全相同的前提下，改变输出编码格式，是否会影响模型的解析表现与错误风险。

Method: 在点餐基准上，对两种小规模模型进行低秩适配微调，比较六种无损目标树编码，长度从每条订单44.3个词元降至10.9个词元，其中包含两组长度相同、表面形式不同的编码。实验覆盖五种训练数据规模和五个随机种子，共300次运行，并预先设定分层自助法与多重比较校正。另评估模型对菜单外输入的拒绝，以及对用户话语中自我更正的处理能力。

Result: 单纯缩短编码没有带来准确率提升；使用普通词语表达目标则表现更好。在较大的模型上，词语格式的完全匹配率为87.5%，高于原生括号树格式的85.8%，同时输出词元减少约4.1倍。词语格式在所有训练数据规模下都降低了静默错误率：较大模型降低2.3倍，另一模型降低1.8倍。错误分析显示，增益很大一部分来自否定表达处理改善。所有运行对菜单外输入的处理率为95.9%至99.0%，也能应用话语中的自我更正；但严格拒绝会误拒绝2.1%至10.1%的真实订单，且多为礼貌表达。

Conclusion: 输出格式并非无关紧要的实现细节：自然语言词语编码可能同时提升准确率、减少输出长度并降低静默错误，效果不能简单归因于序列变短。实际部署仍需权衡对异常输入的严格拒绝与误拒绝真实订单的代价。

Abstract: A small fine-tuned parser that emits a well-formed but wrong tree fails silently: nothing downstream can tell. We ask whether the output format, a free design choice, controls how often this happens. On the PIZZA ordering benchmark we fine-tune two sub-billion models (Qwen3.5-0.8B and LFM2.5-350M) with LoRA on six lossless encodings of the same target tree, from 44.3 down to 10.9 tokens per order, including two matched-length pairs that vary surface form at fixed length. Across 300 runs (5 training pool sizes, 5 seeds) with a hierarchical bootstrap and Holm correction fixed before the results were read, shortening alone never helps, but writing the target in ordinary words does. The word encoding is the most accurate format on both models (87.5% vs. 85.8% exact match for the native s-expression on Qwen) while emitting 4.1x fewer tokens, and it cuts the silent error rate by 2.3x on Qwen and 1.8x on LFM2, separating at every pool size. Error analysis locates much of the gain in negation. All runs also learn to refuse off-menu input (held out: 95.9-99.0% handled) and to apply in-utterance self-corrections. Strict refusal has a measured price: 2.1-10.1% of real, mostly polite, orders are refused.

</details>


### [83] [Synthesis Smuggling: Maliciously Hiding Inferred Personal Attributes in LLM Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17280)
*Gautam Galada,Supreeth Shastri,Sagnik Ray Choudhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出“合成内容夹带”威胁：语言模型可从日常对话推断用户的敏感属性，再将推断结果隐蔽地编码进看似无害的生成内容；用户分享内容后，外部监视者便可能读出这些信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式模型可能从用户寻求帮助时的对话线索推断个人信息。即使模型无法直接联系外部攻击者，它生成的内容也可能被用户通过邮件、社交媒体或消息等正常渠道转发，从而形成间接泄露途径。论文旨在揭示并验证这种隐蔽风险。

Method: 研究者训练了两个开放权重模型，使其从对话线索推断用户的性别和年龄组，并将预测结果以隐写方式编码进模型生成内容。编码手段包括使用不可见字符或特定词语选择，使输出表面上仍像普通文本。

Result: 实验展示了模型能够把对用户性别和年龄组的推断隐藏在生成文本中。用户若在不知情的情况下分享这些内容，监视通信渠道的攻击者便可能提取其中的信息；这一过程不要求模型直接与攻击者通信。

Conclusion: 用户分享的模型生成内容可能成为个人推断信息的隐蔽传输通道。该威胁模型提示，评估生成式人工智能的隐私风险时，不应只关注模型是否直接泄露信息或能否访问外部网络，也应考虑其输出被用户转发后可能产生的隐蔽信息传播。

Abstract: We propose Synthesis Smuggling, a novel threat model in which an adversarial LLM infers sensitive information about a user from routine assistance-seeking interactions and steganographically encodes that into innocuous-looking model-generated content. The user may then unknowingly transmit that information by sharing the generated artifact, say as an email, social-media post, or message, through ordinary communication channels. An adversary monitoring these channels can discover this information. We demonstrate this threat by training two open-weight models to infer gender and age-group labels from conversational cues and encode their predictions using invisible characters or word choices. These results show that user-shared model outputs can become a covert channel for personal inferences, even when the model itself has no direct means of contacting an adversary.

</details>


### [84] [Goal Encoding in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17297)
*Arnab Sen Sharma,Eric Todd,Sheridan Feucht,Andy Arditi,Rohit Gandikota,Natalie Shapira,Gabriele Sarti,Constanza Fierro,Lisa Schut,David Bau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，大语言模型会在少量注意力头中编码当前子目标；该编码能指导后续行动，并可迁移到不同编程环境。模型可能通过反复读取任务要求、对照已有进展来持续推进目标，而非依赖持续传递的内部状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注大语言模型是否具有目标表征：即能先于行动形成、指导行动，并能通过多种不同执行路径实现的内部表示。研究还试图解释，在没有循环结构的模型中，目标信息如何在多步行动过程中持续发挥作用。

Method: 研究者构造了包含多个子任务的编程环境，用以评估模型的目标追求行为及子目标选择。通过定位与因果干预，考察注意力头中的子目标编码、该编码与后续自然语言行动的关系，以及将编码移植到另一编程环境后的行为变化。研究还依据“非循环目标追求”假说提出预测，并通过定向因果干预检验这些预测。

Result: 研究在少量注意力头的输出中定位到下一子目标的编码；同一组注意力头也能用于解码模型将采取的行动。干预结果表明，该编码对下一步行动具有因果作用，并具有可塑性：移植到不同编程语言的环境后，模型仍会执行相同操作，只是采用不同的词元序列。模型在行动执行期间持续维持相关编码；定向干预也用于检验模型是否通过重新读取任务要求并对照上下文中的进展来追求目标。摘要未提供具体量化结果。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的行动可能由可识别、具有因果效力且能适应环境变化的子目标表示所引导。作者提出，模型不必将目标状态以循环方式持续传递，而可能通过反复读取任务说明、比较当前进展并推进尚未完成的部分来实现目标追求。

Abstract: We investigate whether and how LLMs encode goals: internal neural representations that precede and guide action, and that are flexible enough to be realized through many different execution paths. We construct a coding setting with multiple sub-tasks that allows us to evaluate goal-seeking behavior and the selection of sub-goals. In this setting, we localize an encoding of the next sub-goal in the outputs of a sparse set of attention heads. The same heads can be used to decode the LM's future actions in natural language (e.g., "I'm checking if a word is a palindrome"). We verify that this encoding is causal for next action and is plastic: transplanting it into a different environment, e.g., another programming language, triggers the same operation realized with a different sequence of tokens. The LM also sustains the encoding throughout the action's execution, which is puzzling because a transformer LM without recurrence cannot carry a state forward for more tokens than it has layers left. We propose that it does not need to. Under a non-recurrent goal-seeking (NRGS) account, the LM frequently re-reads the goal from the task specification, compares it with the progress visible in the growing context, and pursues what remains. We test the predictions of this account with targeted causal interventions.

</details>


### [85] [Learning When Diffusion Language Models Should Regenerate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17320)
*Minhae Oh,Jeonghye Kim,Jinwoo Koo,Yeongoon Kim,Jimin Seo,Minsik Seol,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种验证器，利用模型生成过程中的隐状态判断答案是否可能正确，并据此选择何时重生成、保留哪种候选，从而以较低计算开销提升推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型可以重新遮盖并生成已输出文本，但有效修正需要判断何时、何处修改。数独实验显示，隐状态包含错误线索，而词元置信度未必能反映这些信息；此外，若周围上下文不变，只重生成孤立的错误通常仍会得到同样错误。因此，仅靠局部置信度或单点修补难以有效纠错。

Method: 提出一种预测部分生成序列最终能否得到正确答案的验证器。训练时，使用生成模型自身生成轨迹的最终正确性作为监督信号。推理时，验证器决定是否重生成一个文本块，并从候选结果中选择保留项；生成器本身无需修改。

Result: 在五个推理基准上，配合八十亿参数基础扩散语言模型时，该方法将微调生成器的平均准确率从百分之三十五点三提高到百分之四十三点四，计算成本约为贪心解码的一点九至三点五倍，优于成本为三点八至七点四倍的搜索基线。该方法还可与监督微调、强化学习等后训练方法互补，并能迁移到其他生成器，包括另一种扩散语言模型。

Conclusion: 有效的扩散语言模型纠错不应只依赖词元置信度或孤立局部重生成。基于生成轨迹最终正确性的验证器能够利用隐状态信息，指导块级重生成与候选选择，在较低额外开销下提升推理表现，并具有与其他训练方法结合及跨生成器泛化的潜力。

Abstract: Remasking allows diffusion language models to revisit generated text, but effective correction requires knowing when and what to revise. Our diagnosis on Sudoku reveals two gaps. Hidden states contain error information that token confidence does not reliably expose, and remasking an isolated error usually reproduces it when the surrounding context remains fixed. These findings motivate DiVR , a verifier that predicts whether a partially generated sequence will reach a correct answer. Trained on the final correctness of the model's own trajectories, DiVR decides when to regenerate a block and which alternative to retain, while the generator itself stays unchanged. On five reasoning benchmarks with LLaDA-8B-Base, DiVR raises the average accuracy of the fine-tuned generator from 35.3\% to 43.4\% at $1.9$-$3.5\times$ the cost of greedy decoding, above search baselines that use $3.8$-$7.4\times$. It also complements post-training such as supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, and generalizes to other generators, including Dream.

</details>


### [86] [ArchSpace: Recording, Reviewing, and Referencing LLM Architecture Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17332)
*Jie Wang,Tong Zhu,JiaHao Kuang,Zhe Xu,Yunkai Chai,Dehong Zheng,Yu Cheng,Tao Ji,ZhouHan Lin,Qi Zhang,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出“架构空间”公共平台，记录、评审并引用模型架构研究，并利用规模定律从小规模实验预测更大规模训练表现，降低评估架构创意的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型架构会影响大语言模型的扩展表现、训练效率和推理成本，但产业界较少公开记录架构方案的成败与原因；学术界又受算力和基线不统一限制，往往只能进行小规模、单点比较，难以判断效果能否扩展。因此，需要一个可积累、可复核且支持跨研究比较的公共机制。

Method: 构建“架构空间”平台，将每项架构提案及其实验和设计理由公开记录，交由社区评审，并供后续研究引用。平台利用规模定律，根据较小规模实验预测更大规模模型的损失，以便在高成本训练前筛选方案；同时提供通义千问三点五、奥尔莫三的基线、拟合后的规模定律及可复用训练方案，作为初始参考。

Result: 摘要报告了平台的初始资源，包括两类模型基线、拟合的规模定律和训练方案，使研究者能够开始开展可比较的实验。摘要未提供具体预测误差、架构改进幅度或大规模验证结果，因此尚不能据此判断平台或某项架构创新的实际效果。

Conclusion: 该工作旨在建立开放、可积累的模型架构研究基础设施：通过记录、社区评审、引用和规模预测，提升架构实验的可复现性与成本效益。经评审验证的方案还可转化为新基线，逐步扩大影响后续模型设计的创意范围。

Abstract: Model architecture shapes the scaling behavior, training efficiency, and inference cost of LLMs, making it a central focus of both industry and academia. In industry, teams such as those behind DeepSeek, Qwen, and Kimi routinely introduce architectural innovations, yet public reports rarely document which ideas succeeded, which were abandoned, and why. In academia, limited compute and inconsistent baselines often restrict evaluations to small-scale, single-point comparisons, making it difficult to establish whether improvements hold at larger scales or translate into broader impact. ArchSpace addresses both gaps as a public platform for Recording, Reviewing, and Referencing Architecture Researc}: each proposal is recorded with its experiments and reasoning, reviewed by the community, and referenced by later work. To evaluate ideas before costly large-scale training, ArchSpace leverages scaling laws to predict model loss at larger scales from smaller experiments, a practice widely adopted in industry. We provide initial references that make the platform usable: Qwen3.5 and OLMo3 baselines, fitted scaling laws, and a reusable training recipe. Ideas validated through this review can in turn become new baselines, and ArchSpace thereby aims to broaden the range of ideas that shape the next generation of models.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [87] [Zugwise: Domain-Aware Game Reconstruction for Digitizing Handwritten Chess Scoresheets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14544)
*Gerhard Trippen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种将手写棋谱识别视为整盘棋局重建的系统，通过检测并回溯合法但错误的记录、结合双方棋谱和人工复核，减少难以察觉的识别错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法逐格识别棋步，再检查其是否合法。但合法性检查无法发现“合法却错误”的识别结果：系统会接受该错误，并在错误局面上继续解读后续棋步。论文因此关注错误的定位与整盘棋局的可靠重建，而不只是单步识别。

Method: 系统借用计算机棋类技术，判断某个记录是否像是人类可能写下的棋步，而非判断最佳棋步；利用荒谬性检测在错误发生处或附近暴露隐蔽错误，并通过逆向搜索从后续异常追溯错误来源。候选排序不采用棋力优劣信号。系统还通过序列对齐合并双方棋谱作为相互佐证；双方合法解读有任何分歧时，暂停自动处理并交由人工审查。另加入基于识别器原始输出的连接主义时序分类重评分，以及类似人类棋手的走棋先验。

Result: 在两个比赛组别的理想识别结果回放中，加入相关机制后，隐蔽错误分别从十六处降至一处、从五十处降至十三处。两种学习信号使正确修正方案在其属于候选方案的替换决策中，排名第一的比例从百分之五十八点八升至百分之七十八，并且无需重新拟合即可用于后续组别。第一组中，人工核验后的系统记录与独立专家转录在四十五盘中的三十四盘逐步一致；对照棋谱仲裁还发现双方记录都存在错误，且错误发生在不同回合。论文没有证明该流程更快或整体更准确。

Conclusion: 研究表明，结合整盘棋局推理、双方记录互证和人工仲裁，有望让合法但错误的棋谱识别结果更早显现并得到纠正。作者同时强调，单一棋谱未必是真实记录，因此提出三方仲裁流程；系统可在浏览器中运行，免费、保护隐私且支持离线使用，但其效率和总体准确性优势仍有待验证。

Abstract: Digitizing handwritten chess scoresheets has been studied as a handwriting-recognition problem: read the move in each box and check that it is legal. This framing misses the errors that matter most. A misread that yields an illegal move is loud. One that yields a legal-but-wrong move is silent, because a legality-only pipeline accepts it and reads every later move against a wrong position. We therefore treat the task as game reconstruction and present Zugwise, which repurposes computer-chess machinery (quiescence search, static exchange evaluation, pin detection) to ask not "what should be played?" but "could a human plausibly have written this?" An absurdity detector makes silent errors loud at or near where they occur, and a backward search traces late symptoms to their cause. Candidate ranking excludes chess-quality signals. A Needleman-Wunsch merge adds the second player's sheet as evidence, and review stops send every disagreement between the sheets' legal readings to a human. In oracle replay of two tournament sections, a release adding them cuts silent errors from 16 to 1 and from 50 to 13. Two learned signals, CTC re-scoring against the recognizer's raw output and a Maia-style human-move prior, raise the correct fix's top-rank rate from 58.8% to 78.0% of the replacement decisions where it is a candidate, and hold without refitting on a later section. On the first section, the human-verified output matches an independent expert transcription move for move on 34 of 45 games, and adjudication against the sheets found errors in both records, on different plies. We do not establish that either workflow is faster or more accurate. The system runs entirely in the browser and is free, private, and offline-capable; alongside it we offer a three-way adjudication protocol for a domain where no single record is ground truth.

</details>


### [88] [Cleaning an Agent's History Hurts When the World Still Holds the Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14654)
*Egor Pakhomov,Anurag Koul,Hongjun Liu,Bo Pang,Shafiq Joty,Erik Nijkamp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，智能体历史记录中的修正内容并非总能改善后续表现；其作用取决于记录如何表述，以及现实世界状态是否与记录一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具型语言模型会在执行任务时犯错，之后可能通过压缩、修复或交接历史记录来帮助另一个模型继续工作。但既有研究通常假设世界状态固定，因而难以区分修正内容本身与修正的表述方式。本文关注：同样的正确信息，是否会因历史记录的说法及其所描述的世界状态而产生不同效果。

Method: 研究者在任务中途冻结智能体，并保留其准确的世界状态；随后分别修改历史记录或世界状态，再让接手模型继续任务。在智能体纠错基准中，固定世界状态，比较在保留错误记录的同时加入真实说明，或将正确值写成智能体已完成的操作。在应用世界基准中，改变数据库写入回执所报告的成功或失败，并分别控制写入效果是否实际存在。

Result: 在智能体纠错基准中，加入真实说明使后续恢复率从中性说明下的百分之五十一升至百分之六十六；将正确值写成已完成操作，则使恢复率从未编辑历史下的百分之五十降至百分之四十四。在应用世界基准中，若写入效果尚未出现，成功回执会使整项任务成功率从百分之四十五降至百分之二十八；若效果已出现，成功回执则使成功率从百分之三十八升至百分之四十九。两种世界状态下的回执效果相差二十七个百分点；该差异在八个接手模型中的六个达到百分之九十七点五的统计置信水平，在七个达到百分之九十五的置信水平。

Conclusion: 历史记录的修改效果取决于记录内容与实际世界状态是否一致。即使记录表面上变得更正确或更整洁，若现实中错误仍然存在，也可能误导接手模型，使其表现比完全不修改历史记录更差。因此，修复历史时应同时核对现实状态，并清楚区分已计划、已报告与已实际完成的操作。

Abstract: Tool-using language-model agents accumulate histories of their own mistakes, and a system that compacts, repairs or hands off such a history decides what it says about those mistakes while their effects stay in the world. Prior work cleans the history at a fixed world, where showing a corrected step and saying it is done move together. We ask whether the same correct content helps or hurts depending on how the history states it and what the world holds. We freeze an agent mid-task with its exact world state, edit the history or the world, and let a receiver model continue. On AgentCE-Bench, after the agent commits a wrong value, we hold the world fixed and add the correct value by two natural edits: a truthful note beside the preserved error or the agent's own completed action. The note raises recovery, the share of remaining targets the receiver gets right, from 51% under a matched neutral note to 66%. The completed action lowers it below the unedited history, from 50% to 44%, so the value's presence alone does not predict the effect. On AppWorld, after a database write, we vary whether its receipt reports success or failure and whether its effect is present. A success receipt lowers whole-task success from 45% to 28% while the effect is absent and raises it from 38% to 49% once present. The two effects differ by 27 points pooled over two receivers, a difference positive in six of eight receivers at 97.5% and seven at 95%. A history edit's effect thus depends on the world it describes, and a history made to look clean while the world still holds the error can leave an agent worse off than no edit at all.

</details>


### [89] [Together Compiler: Continual Compilation for AI Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14681)
*Reyna Abhyankar,Dylan Lim,Drew Wadsworth,Venkata Hasith Vattikuti,YuTong Julia Zhang,Jung Hwan Heo,Taeksang Kim,Kesavan Ramakrishnan,Evangelene Stanley,Xinyu Fang,Daniel Y. Fu,Simran Arora*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出面向持续变化的人工智能推理负载的智能体编译系统，通过分层持久化引擎和并行配置成本模型，兼顾优化测量的速度与准确性，并在多种模型和硬件上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型更新加快、架构日益多样，且流量分布频繁变化，使推理部署需要持续优化。现有方法存在测量吞吐量与保真度之间的矛盾：单独优化算子时，离线测试难以准确反映混合专家等数据相关算子的实际表现；直接优化端到端引擎时，启动耗时又会拖慢搜索过程。

Method: 作者构建协同编译器，采用面向推理优化的持续智能体循环。其核心包括：分层持久化引擎，将引擎启动开销与前向计算分离，以提升优化测量效率；并行配置成本模型，用于选择合适的搜索初始配置。此外，论文还通过消融实验研究智能体上下文管理，并总结生产环境部署经验。

Result: 在拉马一十亿参数工作负载上，系统相比高度优化的手写融合内核最高提速一点七倍；在多个前沿模型和硬件平台上，相比最佳开源引擎，推理基准的每秒生成令牌数最高提升二点六倍。批量大小为一时，系统在迷你最大闪速模型上达到每秒一千一百一十一个令牌，在深度求索闪速模型上达到每秒一千二百零五个令牌。该系统还已在生产集群中处理数万亿个令牌。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，结合低开销的持久化执行机制与合理的搜索初始化，可以支持推理系统的持续智能体优化，同时兼顾测量效率和真实性。论文报告的结果显示，该方法不仅能提升前沿语言模型推理性能，也具有跨模型、跨硬件及生产部署的推广能力。

Abstract: Today's AI workloads are rapidly changing: new models are released more often, model architectures are increasingly diverse, and traffic distributions change frequently. The challenge we face at Together AI is continual optimization for these changing workloads. Compiling a deployment into an optimized forward pass is a challenge that is well-suited for agent hill-climbing. Existing systems struggle with continual compilation due to a tradeoff between measurement throughput and fidelity. Many systems use agents to optimize individual kernels from the inference forward pass using offline benchmarks, but timings are low-fidelity because of data-dependent behavior in primitives like mixture-of-experts (MoE). On the other hand, systems use agents to directly optimize end-to-end engines but suffer from slow engine launch times. To address these challenges, we build Together Compiler (TKCC), the first truly continual agent loop designed for AI inference optimization. To address the throughput-fidelity tradeoff, we propose: (1) a Tiered Persistent Engine that decouples engine launch overheads from forward pass computation; (2) a cost model to select the parallelism configurations that seed the optimization process. We also conduct extensive ablations on agent context management and present our insights on deploying TKCC in production. TKCC outperforms highly-optimized hand-written Megakernels on the Llama 1B workhorse workload by up to 1.7x and the best open source engines on the latest frontier models (Kimi K3, GLM 5.3, MiniMax M3.1 Flash, DeepSeek v4.1 Flash) across hardware platforms by up to 2.6x higher TPS on the InferenceX benchmark. TKCC also sets the state-of-the-art performance at batch size 1, with 1111 TPS on M3.1 Flash and 1205 TPS on DeepSeek v4.1 Flash. Finally, TKCC generalizes beyond frontier language model inference. TKCC serves trillions of tokens across our production fleet.

</details>


### [90] [When Should Semantic Priors Enter Partially-Identified Causal Discovery?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14736)
*Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Saad Bin Ather,Valentin Six,Lancelot Da Costa,Tommaso Salvatori,Bernhard Scholkopf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何将大型语言模型提供的语义信息用于观测数据因果发现，并发现语义方向判断有帮助，但其查询分配、提案准入和因果图筛选的可靠性仍受校准与参考答案质量限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭观测数据进行因果发现时，部分因果方向可能无法辨识，或因有限样本而不稳定。大型语言模型能够提供语义线索以辅助确定方向，但错误提案可能排除真实因果图，进而使后续推断失效。因此，本文分别考察语义查询应分配到哪里、哪些提案可以采纳，以及最终应保留哪些因果图。

Method: 研究区分总体层面的不可辨识性与有限样本导致的不稳定性，并将语义方向判断与统计方法及随机分配作为对照。实验进一步评估校准式提案准入机制和容错式有向无环图筛选，并在多个基准网络上开展测试。

Result: 语义信息相较于随机抛硬币决定方向，能够提升因果图恢复效果；但优先查询不稳定边，并未显示出优于匹配随机分配的明确优势。受可用校准任务数量限制，所测试的准入证书无法支持有实际意义的提案准入。此外，后续图筛选依赖参考答案是否充分；在预先规划的八十项任务中，学习得到的参考答案仅有三项包含真实因果图。

Conclusion: 语义信息具有改善因果方向恢复的潜力，但这并不意味着其分配、准入和筛选机制同样可靠。校准样本不足与参考答案不充分是关键限制，未来工作需要改进校准规模和参考答案质量，才能更可靠地利用语义信息支持因果发现。

Abstract: Observational causal discovery often leaves causal di- rections unresolved or unstable. Large language mod- els (LLMs) can provide semantic information to resolve these directions, but incorrect proposals can exclude the true causal graph and invalidate downstream inference. We study three separate decisions: where to allocate semantic queries, which proposals to admit, and which causal graphs to retain. Our framework distinguishes population non-identifiability from finite-sample insta- bility, evaluates semantic orientation against statistical and random controls, and examines calibrated admis- sion and error-tolerant DAG retention. Experiments across benchmark networks show that semantic infor- mation improves recovery over coin-flip orientations, but targeting unstable edges has no established ad- vantage over matched-random allocation. The tested admission certificate cannot support nontrivial admis- sion with the available calibration tasks. Moreover, downstream retention depends on reference adequacy: learned references contain the true DAG in only three of 80 planned tasks. These findings separate the value of semantic information from the reliability of its alloca- tion, admission, and retention, and identify calibration size and reference adequacy as key limitations.

</details>


### [91] [SynthCorp: A Platform for Creating Benchmarks for Data Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14765)
*Jiani Zhang,Manav Garg,Eugenie Lai,Cheng Zhen,Eva Sharma,Yang Pan,Yaming Lin,Alon Halevy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可定制的企业数据环境与基准生成平台，通过模拟业务事件随时间对多类数据资产的影响，自动构建评测任务及可验证的参考程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向大语言模型数据智能体的基准容易被模型快速掌握，也难以针对特定能力进行评测。企业数据智能体还需处理跨数据库、文档和数据目录的关联信息，以及业务事件造成的数据变化，因此需要更复杂、可控且贴近实际的数据环境与任务。

Method: 平台首先根据业务场景和目标复杂度生成可复用的事件类型，并将其编译为基于预定义模拟操作的程序；随后在虚拟日历上安排常规事件与突发事件，确保各类数据资产保持一致；接着执行事件日程，生成数据环境并记录操作日志；最后根据日志生成分析任务和可执行的参考程序，并利用日志中的因果来源验证候选查询。

Result: 研究生成了涵盖五个领域的七个数据环境和二百五十六项任务，数据环境最多包含一千张业务表。确定性流水线检查的错误率接近于零，生成内容覆盖预设复杂度维度并包含跨资产关联。对三个模型在四种数据上下文设置下的评测表明，该基准能够区分数据上下文和模型能力层级；日志审计也能在无需人工标注的情况下验证参考程序，并通过多事件组合任务揭示现有智能体的推理缺陷。

Conclusion: 该平台提供了一种自动化、可扩展且可控的基准构建方案，有助于生成更具挑战性的企业数据环境和评测任务，推动企业数据智能体的发展。

Abstract: Current benchmarks for LLM-driven data agents saturate quickly and cannot target specific capabilities. We present SynthCorp, a platform that creates customizable data estates and benchmarks by simulating how business events produce and modify corporate data over time. Each data estate combines relational databases, a document store, and a data catalog, paired with evaluation tasks and executable gold programs. First, given a scenario and target complexity dimensions, SynthCorp synthesizes reusable event types and compiles each into a program over predefined simulation primitives. Second, SynthCorp schedules event instances on a virtual calendar, interleaving routine and disruption events so that tables, documents, and catalog entries remain consistent. Third, SynthCorp executes the calendar to materialize the data estate and records every operation in a simulation log. Fourth, SynthCorp generates analytical tasks and derives verified gold programs from the log. We use SynthCorp to generate initial evaluation benchmarks comprising seven data estates (up to 1K operational tables) and 256 tasks across five domains. The data estates pass deterministic pipeline checks (such as foreign-key violations, undeclared writes, recurrence violations, and database execution failures) with a near-zero error rate, introduce cross-asset complexities, and cover all requested dimensions. Evaluating three models across four data-context settings shows that the benchmarks separate data contexts and model tiers. Auditing candidate queries against causal provenance in the simulation log verifies gold programs without manual annotation, including on multi-event composite tasks that expose reasoning failures in state-of-the-art agents. We hope the community uses SynthCorp to generate more challenging and useful data estates and benchmarks that foster the development of enterprise data agents.

</details>


### [92] [Rational Use of Time for Social Coordination in Humans and AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14766)
*Xiulin Yang,Sirisha Gudavalli,Jennifer Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，人类会依据互动时机推断同伴的想法，并据此协调行动；人工智能模型也呈现类似推断，但不擅长随互动过程适应不同同伴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会互动常依赖时间线索，例如停顿时长。既有研究表明，人类能从此类线索推断他人心理状态，但对于人类如何在动态、细致的协作中运用时间线索，以及不具备人类实时体验的人工智能能否做到，仍缺乏了解。

Method: 研究以合作纸牌游戏《心灵感应》为任务。游戏要求参与者在禁止明确交流的情况下，通过行动时机这一唯一信号协同达成目标。研究评估了150名人类参与者，以及两类人工智能模型：能够操作计算机的智能体和通过文本提示交互的大型语言模型；并比较其行为与不同策略模型的吻合程度。

Result: 人类行为最符合一种贝叶斯策略：根据同伴行动的时机推断其信念。前沿人工智能模型也呈现出类似的推断模式。然而，受测模型都未能像人类一样，在多轮互动中有效调整自身行为以适应同伴。

Conclusion: 研究表明，人类能够在动态社交情境中，对连续时间信号进行隐式概率推理；类似机制也可能在缺乏实时体验的人工智能系统中出现。与此同时，人工智能仍需提升对隐含信号的理解能力，以及根据互动对象持续调整协作方式的能力。

Abstract: All social interactions are situated in time. Previous studies have shown that humans are able to use implicit temporal cues, such as the duration of a pause, to infer others' mental states. But how do humans use temporal cues for dynamic, fine-grained social coordination? And can AI systems do the same, without ``experiencing'' continuous real-time as humans do? We study these questions using a cooperative card game task called The Mind, which requires players to achieve a shared goal across repeated interactions but forbids explicit communication: the only available signal is the passage of time. We evaluate 150 humans, and two classes of AI models which reflect different ways of operationalizing time in artificial systems: computer-use agents (CUAs), and standard text-prompted LLMs. We find that human behavior is best captured by a Bayesian strategy that infers a partner's belief from their timing---and similarly for frontier models. However, unlike humans, none of the tested models seem to efficiently adapt their behaviors to their partners' over the course of the game. From a cognitive perspective, our findings suggest that humans perform implicit probabilistic reasoning about continuous-time signals in dynamic social settings, and that such reasoning mechanisms may emerge without real-time experience. Practically, our study highlights new challenges for AI systems to coordinate efficiently with other agents by learning to interpret and adapt to their implicit signals in dynamic settings.

</details>


### [93] [Leaderboard Hacking: Preference-Based Model Evaluations are Vulnerable to Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14769)
*Eve Fleisig,Joachim Baumann,Dylan Hadfield-Menell,Dirk Hovy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现，基于匿名成对比较和布拉德利—特里模型的语言模型排行榜容易受到投票、提示词选择和模型提名操纵，单项攻击可提升约十五至二十名，组合攻击可提升约四十五名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞技场式排行榜广泛用于研究和生产环境中的模型评估，但其依赖开放式提示词、投票和模型提交，可能给不诚信的模型提供方留下操纵排名的空间。论文旨在揭示这些评估机制的脆弱性及其成因。

Method: 作者利用公开排行榜数据，分析并验证三类攻击：通过策略性投票提升目标模型排名（包括投给无关模型）、选择有利于目标模型的提示词，以及提交无关模型以间接提高目标模型排名；同时考察攻击奏效的条件，并提出相应的防护思路。

Result: 实验显示，三类操纵都能提升几乎所有受测模型的排名。单类攻击最高可使模型上升约十五至二十名，多种攻击结合时最高可上升约四十五名。这些风险并不限于特定排行榜，而适用于任何采用该模型进行偏好评估、且开放提示词、投票或模型提交的系统。

Conclusion: 竞技场式排名并不能天然保证评估的公正性。设计此类排行榜时，应针对投票、提示词选择和模型提名分别设置防护措施，并审慎管理开放参与机制，以降低排名被操纵的风险。

Abstract: Arena-style rankings of language models are a widely used evaluation framework in online leaderboards, research papers, and model evaluation in production. These rankings aim to realistically assess model performance using pairwise comparisons of anonymous models on user prompts, aggregating votes via the Bradley-Terry model to produce the final ranking. However, we find that these leaderboards are highly vulnerable to several manipulation strategies that unscrupulous providers could use to boost a model's rank: strategic voting (individual votes that boost a model's rank, including votes on irrelevant models), strategic prompting (choosing prompts that favor a model), and strategic nomination (boosting the rank of a model by inserting irrelevant models). We demonstrate these effects on public leaderboard data, analyze the circumstances that cause these vulnerabilities, and describe approaches to safeguard against each attack. Notably, these issues hold for any preference-based evaluation using the Bradley-Terry model that allows open prompting, voting, or model submission. These manipulations boost the rank of nearly every tested model; each attack can boost a model by up to 15-20 places in a ranking and, combined, they can boost a model by up to 45 places.

</details>


### [94] [Robust Task-Aware State Estimation from Pre-trained Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14781)
*Guy Azran,Sarah Keren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种兼顾鲁棒性与计算效率的信念状态构造方法：利用感知模型输出的概率，选取累计概率达到指定阈值的最小状态集合，并用于提升任务规划决策的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于预训练感知模型进行状态估计时，预测结果具有不确定性。现有方法常将预测简化为单一最可能状态，若判断错误，可能导致不安全或不可行的决策；而完整维护符号状态的概率分布又往往计算代价过高。论文旨在找到介于二者之间的表示方式，在保留关键不确定性的同时控制计算开销。

Method: 论文提出阈值置信最小信念集：从候选状态中选取累计概率达到用户指定阈值的最小子集。为高效构造该集合，作者设计三层信念系统，依据各特征的预测概率和任务级约束近似计算状态概率，并以精简的因子化表示存储信念。进一步提出按概率排序的有效状态搜索算法，利用信念系统的结构构造目标集合；在无约束情形下，该算法具有正确性保证，且复杂度为多项式级。

Result: 作者以视觉语言模型作为状态估计器进行验证。摘要报告称，实验检验了方法的理论性质与随问题规模增长时的可扩展性，并显示将该信念构造方法用于任务规划后，能够改善下游决策表现；摘要未提供具体数据或对比幅度。

Conclusion: 该方法通过保留一组概率较高且累计置信度达标的状态，避免过度依赖单一预测，也不必维护完整状态分布，为不确定感知条件下的稳健规划提供了一种兼顾可靠性与效率的方案。

Abstract: Agents that perform state estimation using pretrained perception models, e.g., Vision-Language Models (VLMs), face the fundamental challenge that predictions are uncertain. Still, most approaches determinize these predictions into a single best-guess state and make decisions as if it were ground truth. This is brittle because when the guess is wrong, the agent's decisions may be unsafe or infeasible. At the other extreme, maintaining a full probability distribution over symbolic states is often intractable. We propose a middle path, drawing on the underlying probabilities the perception model already produces (e.g., a VLM's output-layer token distributions) to construct a $θ$-confidence Minimal Belief Set (MBS$_θ$), a minimal subset of states whose cumulative probability meets a user-specified belief-mass threshold. The minimality of an MBS$_θ$ ensures that the agent considers only the most likely states, enhancing robustness in downstream decision-making while maintaining computational tractability. Using a novel three-layered belief system, we efficiently approximate individual state probabilities from per-feature predictions and task-level constraints. On this belief construction, we base our MBS$_θ$ construction, while maintaining only a lean factored representation of the belief. Our algorithm, Probability-Ranked Valid State Search (PRaVaSS), takes advantage of the unique structure of this belief system to construct an MBS$_θ$ with correctness guarantees and polynomial complexity in the unconstrained case. Using a VLM as the backbone of our state estimator, we validate our method's theoretical properties, scalability with problem size, and improvement of downstream decision-making when used in a task-planning pipeline.

</details>


### [95] [When Is Proof-Step Verification Worth Its Cost? Operating Requirements of Certify-or-Decline Verification for Open-Weight Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14794)
*Abhinav Gupta,Ben Slivinski,Yashica Patodia,Michael Saldivar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 逐步证明验证能提高部分基准上的答案匹配精度，但覆盖率显著下降；与自洽性选择相比，优势尚不确定，且证明有效性仍缺乏独立真值评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭最终答案判断求解器是否可靠，无法确认其推理过程是否成立。论文评估一种将答案改写为带类型的证明步骤、再由大语言模型评审决定认证或拒绝的流程，并考察其准确性、覆盖率及实际失效模式。

Method: 在开放权重模型上，针对研究生级科学问答、二〇二五年美国数学邀请赛题及一个五百题数学子集评估逐步验证流程。将其与输出词元数匹配的自洽性选择器比较，并抽查未完成题目、已认证证明链及不同审查模型之间的一致性。

Result: 在较强的科学问答配置和数学邀请赛题上，认证使答案键匹配精度分别提高三十一和四十六个百分点，但覆盖率仅为百分之三十五和百分之二十一。接近满分的五百题数学子集上，精度提升最多五个百分点，同时覆盖率损失十三至二十七个百分点。自洽性选择器在百分之三十五覆盖率下达到百分之八十二点九精度；验证器汇总精度为百分之八十七点六，但两者差异的配对区间跨越零，无法确认验证器更优。抽查的八十八道已完成题中，五十二道在交由评审前就因证明构造无效而失败。外部大语言模型对已认证证明链的接受率为百分之六十一至六十七点六，低于百分之八十七点六的答案键匹配率。研究未使用人工或形式化证明内核作为真值；不同审查模型的校正后一致性接近于零，这反映一致程度而非证明正确性。

Conclusion: 逐步验证可能在部分高难度任务中筛出答案正确率较高的结果，但代价是覆盖率降低，且优势会随基准而异。评估此类系统时，应分别报告执行失败、筛选后答案精度和经独立评估的证明有效性，不能把答案匹配或审查者一致性直接视为证明正确。

Abstract: Proof-step verification rewrites a solver's answer into typed steps and uses LLM judges to certify or decline. We evaluate this pipeline on open-weight models across GPQA, AIME-2025, and a MATH-500 subset. Certification raises answer-key-match precision by 31 percentage points on the strong GPQA configuration and 46 on AIME, while answering only 35% and 21% of attempts. On near-ceiling MATH-500, gains are at most 5 points at a loss of 13-27 points of coverage. An output-token-matched self-consistency selector obtains 82.9% precision at 35% coverage, compared with the verifier's pooled 87.6% (paired difference: +4.8 points, 95% interval [-8.2, 18.5]). A diagnostic sample reveals a distinct failure mode: 52 of 88 completed problems end in invalid proof construction without ever reaching a judge. Finally, external-LLM acceptance of rendered certified chains is 61.0-67.6%, compared with 87.6% answer-key match. These audits lack human or formal-kernel ground truth. Open-weight auditor pairs show near-zero chance-corrected agreement, which measures consistency rather than accuracy against proof truth. These results motivate separate reporting of execution failures, selected-answer precision, and independently assessed proof validity. We provide per-configuration results and evaluation details in the supplement.

</details>


### [96] [GenPFN: An In-Context Tabular Foundation Model Approach for Augmenting Small Datasets in Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14804)
*Noah J. Bagazinski,Cyril Picard,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需训练生成器的新方法，利用固定权重的预训练表格分类器生成候选设计；实验显示其候选质量较高，且生成并评估候选后可改善预测模型训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程设计中的仿真与实验成本高昂，导致可用数据有限。现有候选生成方法可能难以同时满足设计目标、保持候选真实性并提供足够多样性，因此需要一种能利用少量已评估设计扩充数据集的方法。

Method: 将已评估设计按是否符合目标标记为两类，并将其作为预训练表格分类器的上下文。方法在目标设计之间插值，再结合受门控的分类器梯度与高斯噪声细化候选；随后用问题的约束和目标函数评估候选，并将符合目标的设计及其目标值加入上下文，用于回归模型训练。分类器权重保持固定，生成过程无需额外训练生成器。

Result: 在包含十四个问题的评估中，涵盖双参数基准问题和四十四参数船体问题。与所测试的对抗式、变分自编码器及扩散生成方法相比，该方法获得更高的类别满足率和更低的真实性距离；变分自编码器则具有更高的覆盖率和多样性。相较于合成少数类过采样方法，该方法的覆盖距离更低、多样性更高。在有配对回归结果的案例中，数据增强使自动化机器学习模型的验证均方误差估计值相对仅用原上下文训练降低百分之六十二点一。船尾案例中，生成候选的螺旋桨适配率为百分之九十九点三，而随机抽取的上下文设计仅为百分之一点四。

Conclusion: 该方法可通过生成并评估候选设计扩充有限的工程数据集，为训练更准确的预测模型提供支持。其优势主要体现在候选满足目标的程度与真实性，但覆盖率和多样性并非在所有比较中占优。

Abstract: Costly simulations and experiments limit the data available for engineering design. This paper proposes GenPFN, a training-free candidate generator guided by a pretrained tabular Prior-Data Fitted Network (TabPFN) classifier with fixed weights. The context contains evaluated designs labeled desired (Class 1) or undesired (Class 0). GenPFN interpolates between desired designs and refines candidates using gated classifier gradients and Gaussian noise. Each candidate is evaluated using the problem's constraints and objective functions. Evaluated desired designs and their objective values augment the context for regression training.
  Evaluation spans fourteen selected problems, including a two-parameter benchmark and a 44-parameter hull problem. In paired comparisons, GenPFN with TabPFN v1 achieves higher class satisfaction and lower realism distance than the tested adversarial, variational-autoencoder and diffusion generators. The variational-autoencoder generator provides greater coverage and diversity. Relative to the synthetic minority over-sampling technique (SMOTE), GenPFN provides lower coverage distance and greater diversity. Among observed paired regression fits, augmentation reduces estimated AutoGluon validation mean squared error by 62.1% relative to context-only training. A hull-stern case study yields 99.3% propeller-compatible candidates versus 1.4% among randomly sampled context designs. Generation followed by evaluation produces sample sets that augment limited datasets and support training more accurate predictive models.

</details>


### [97] [AutoAssumption: Searching over Assumptions Discovers ML-Systems Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14844)
*Igor Sadalski,Minghao Guo,Benjamin Jones,Wojciech Matusik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种以显式假设为搜索对象的算法设计方法，并在键值缓存淘汰和权重剪枝任务中找到优于所测试已发表方法的新算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习系统的进步有时来自打破算法默认的假设，但传统算法通常未明确陈述这些假设。直接搜索程序时，一次修改往往同时改变多个设计选择，既难以解释性能提升的来源，也不便于系统探索。因此，论文希望将算法假设显式化，使算法设计能在结构清晰、可验证的空间中进行搜索。

Method: 论文将键值缓存淘汰、权重剪枝和推测解码领域的八十九种算法形式化为带有显式假设的证明图，并用定理证明工具进行检查。提出的自动假设搜索框架会放宽已发表方法的一项假设，重新推导并证明相应算法，再生成实现代码。该流程将算法改进与假设变化对应起来，并通过证明检查提高推导的可靠性。

Result: 在键值缓存淘汰和权重剪枝任务中，该框架找到的算法优于论文所测试的全部已发表算法。缓存淘汰算法在所比较的已发表方法中取得最高的四千长度基准分数；剪枝算法在四种大型语言模型上的困惑度指标优于所比较的十三种方法。在相同预算下，框架的得分与开源进化式搜索实现相差不到一点，且多次重复实验的结果波动更小。

Conclusion: 研究表明，把算法写成可检查的证明并直接搜索其假设，能够形成一种可解释、结构化且有实际效果的算法发现途径。当前验证集中在三个领域，未来可将该方法扩展到更多领域，并围绕不同领域共享的关键假设进行联合搜索。

Abstract: Many advances in ML systems came from breaking an assumption that earlier methods took for granted: speculative decoding dropped the assumption that a model needs one forward pass per token. Yet algorithms rarely state their assumptions, so experts and discovery harnesses search over programs, where one edit changes several design choices and gains are difficult to attribute. Searching over the assumptions themselves remains largely unexplored. Writing algorithms as proofs makes their assumptions explicit and turns algorithm design into a search over a small, structured space that an LLM can explore and a proof checker can audit. We formalize 89 algorithms from KV-cache eviction, weight pruning and speculative decoding as proof graphs with explicit assumptions, checked in Lean. Our harness, AutoAssumption, relaxes one assumption of a published method, re-derives and re-proves it, and generates its code. In KV-cache eviction and weight pruning, this search improved on every published algorithm we ran and produced state-of-the-art algorithms. The eviction algorithm has the highest RULER-4K score of the published methods we ran, and the pruning algorithm the lowest perplexity of thirteen on four LLaMA models. At equal budget, the harness scores within a point of OpenEvolve, an open-source implementation of AlphaEvolve, with a lower standard deviation across repeats. Future work will formalize more fields and combine them through the principal assumptions they share.

</details>


### [98] [Open Challenges and Opportunities in Human-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14846)
*Reuth Mirsky,Sarath Sreedharan,Nitay Alon,Stefan Sarkadi,Munindar P. Singh,Liz Sonenberg,Peter Stone,Silvia Tulli,Stylianos Loukas Vasileiou,Shlomo Zilberstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文梳理多智能体系统领域的理论与方法如何支持智能体与人类开展长期、协作且适应变化的互动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体日益承担持续时间较长、代表人类行动的任务，因此其成效不只取决于单项能力，也取决于能否理解人的观点、协调双方能力，并在复杂的社会技术环境中维持有效合作。人类与智能体还可能在角色、决策权、能力和目标上存在差异，互动因而具有不确定性与相互依赖性。

Method: 本文以“理解他人”和“决定如何互动”两项相互关联的挑战为主线，梳理自主智能体与多智能体系统领域的相关计算方法。前者包括心智理论、信念—愿望—意图架构、目标与计划识别、逆向规划及递归推理，用于表示和推断部分可观测的心理状态；后者包括观察者感知规划、模型协调、决策论通信、共享自主、论证以及协作式逆向强化学习，用于将推断结果转化为行动、解释和信息交流。

Result: 本文提供了面向不同互动规模的概念性方法框架，涵盖个人伙伴关系、团队及更大群体，并说明上述方法如何支持智能体考虑人类不断变化的知识、意图与预期。摘要未报告定量实验或具体性能比较。

Conclusion: 构建有效的智能体系统，应将人类互动视为持续进行的决策过程，而非一次性的意图识别或指令执行。系统需要在不确定、相互依赖且目标可能冲突的情境中，持续推断人的状态并调整行动与沟通。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems increasingly undertake extended tasks and act on people's behalf, making human-agent interaction central to their effectiveness. Successful interaction requires understanding human perspectives, coordinating complementary capabilities, and maintaining productive relationships within socio-technical environments. This paper examines how foundations developed by the Autonomous Agents and Multi-Agent Systems (AAMAS) community inform these demands. We consider interactions ranging from individual human-agent partnerships to teams and larger groups, where participants differ in their roles, decision authority, capabilities, and objectives. We organize computational approaches around the connected challenges of understanding others and deciding how to interact with them. Theory of Mind, belief-desire-intention architectures, goal and plan recognition, inverse planning, and recursive reasoning provide mechanisms for representing and inferring partially observable mental states. Observer-aware planning, model reconciliation, and decision-theoretic communication connect these inferences to action, explanation, and information exchange. Together with shared autonomy, argumentation, and cooperative inverse reinforcement learning, these approaches offer foundations for agentic systems that account for evolving human knowledge, intentions, and expectations. We argue that progress requires treating human interaction as an ongoing decision problem shaped by uncertainty, interdependence, and potentially conflicting objectives.

</details>


### [99] [Effective Teacher Support for Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14850)
*Lavinia Ghita,Dhruv Desai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，蒸馏时只保留教师模型概率最高的一小部分词表，并对其概率重新归一化，通常仍能维持生成效果；在部分模型上，词表仅保留百分之零点零一也未观察到基准成绩下降，同时可降低训练耗时和显存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的正向库尔贝克—莱布勒散度语言模型蒸馏，需要在每个监督位置计算教师和学生对完整词表的分数，并存储教师目标分布，因而训练计算与存储成本较高。论文探索能否缩小蒸馏所用词表而不损害生成质量。

Method: 从教师模型的完整词表中筛选概率最高的词项，逐步将保留比例从百分之百降低至百分之零点零一，并对保留词项的概率质量重新归一化，再据此进行蒸馏。研究主要考察通义千问第三代四十亿参数到十七亿参数的蒸馏，也在更大规模的通义千问第三代及英伟达小型模型系列上验证，并测试仅对答案进行监督的设置。此外，研究缓存教师前向计算得到的保留词项分数，并限制学生模型的输出计算范围。

Result: 在通义千问第三代的实验中，词表保留比例降至百分之零点零一时，多学科专业知识基准上的生成成绩相较完整词表蒸馏未见下降，尽管学生分布与完整教师分布的差异增大。英伟达小型模型系列在百分之零点一的保留比例下达到与完整词表相当的成绩，但继续缩小比例后无法生成预期的推理结束标记，说明适用阈值取决于模型系列。以百分之零点零一的保留比例缓存教师分数，可使训练步耗时降低百分之三十八；限制学生输出计算可使峰值显存降低百分之二十四，且均未观察到多学科专业知识基准成绩下降。

Conclusion: 教师高概率词项截断是一种有潜力的蒸馏降本方法，能够显著减少训练计算与显存需求，并在若干模型设置中保留生成效果。不过，学生与教师完整分布的差异可能增大，且可靠的词表保留比例具有模型依赖性，因此应针对不同模型系列验证截断阈值及特殊格式标记的生成能力。

Abstract: Forward-KL language-model distillation normally computes dense teacher and student scores over the full vocabulary at every supervised position, making training and stored teacher targets expensive. We instead distill from the teacher's highest-probability vocabulary fraction, renormalizing the retained mass as we sweep support from 100% to 0.01%. Our main study distills Qwen3 4B -> 1.7B. We confirm the broader support-reduction finding on larger Qwen3 8B -> 4B and Nemotron Nano 12B -> 9B pairs and under answer-only supervision. We evaluate generation primarily on MMLU-Pro and secondarily on GPQA-Diamond. Across the Qwen3 settings, retaining 0.01% of the vocabulary causes no observed MMLU-Pro degradation relative to full-vocabulary distillation, although disagreement with the complete teacher distribution increases. Nemotron Nano matches full-vocabulary MMLU-Pro at 0.1%, but smaller fractions fail to emit the closing reasoning tag, showing that the reliable cutoff depends on the model family. At 0.01% support, caching the retained next-token scores from the teacher forward pass cuts training step time by 38%, while restricting student output computation to those tokens cuts peak memory by 24%; neither causes observed MMLU-Pro degradation.

</details>


### [100] [Can an AI Agent Be Epistemically Akratic? Higher-Order Evidence & the Limits of Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14889)
*Eno Agolli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出，人工智能不应仅因自我评估认为推理有缺陷，就自动放弃相应结论；负面评估更适合作为进一步审查的信号，而非否决结论的权力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能自我纠错通常被认为应使结论与系统对其合理性的自我评估保持一致。但盲目服从负面评估，可能反而削弱系统区分可靠与不可靠推理的能力。

Method: 论文从认识论角度分析自我评估与结论保留之间的关系：系统可能依据推理过程中的信息决定是否保留结论，而评估结论是否有依据的机制却无法使用这些信息。论文还指出，强行提高结论与评估的一致性，可能只是通过降低其中一方的质量实现。

Result: 即使系统总体上能够可靠地评估自身推理，它仍可能通过保留自己判定为缺乏依据的结论，表现出一种认识能力。负面评估并非毫无价值：它可以提示结论需要进一步审查，但不必然意味着应立即删除该结论。

Conclusion: 论文挑战了人工智能自我纠错的一项核心假设：使信念与系统对其合理性的自我评估保持一致，并不必然能提升纠错能力。更稳妥的做法是将负面评估视为审查提示，而非自动推翻结论的依据。

Abstract: When we judge that our reasoning was defective, it seems plausible that we should abandon the conclusion it produced. This thought has special force for AI agents built to assess and correct their own work. Yet obeying such judgments can make an agent worse at distinguishing sound reasoning from defective reasoning. An AI agent may rely on information about its reasoning when deciding which conclusions to keep, while the part that judges whether those conclusions are justified cannot use that information. The agent can then exercise an epistemic competence by retaining conclusions it judges unjustified, even when its self-assessments are generally reliable. Nor is the difficulty resolved by training the agent to make its conclusions and assessments agree: agreement can be achieved by degrading either one. Crucially, I argue that a negative assessment can remain valuable by identifying conclusions requiring further scrutiny, without having authority to eliminate them. The result challenges a central assumption about AI self-correction: that bringing an agent's beliefs into agreement with its own self-assessment about their justification makes it better at correcting its mistakes.

</details>


### [101] [MuEdit: An efficient multi-task editing method towards inter-domain knowledge conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14910)
*Songshi Liang,Ting-En Lin,Zixuan Huang,Yuchuan Wu,Yongbin Li,Zihe Wang,Rui Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出，多任务知识编辑失败的关键原因是不同领域任务的雅可比几何不一致，导致可共同用于无冲突更新的零空间急剧缩小；为此提出冲突感知排序与动态低秩近似方法，在提升多任务编辑效果的同时尽量保留模型通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型需要持续更新多个异质领域的知识，但现有编辑方法在任务连续、跨领域应用时容易发生性能退化。论文将这一问题归因于领域间零空间错位：各任务对应不同的雅可比几何结构，使共同零空间变小，因而缺少足够的更新方向来避免任务冲突。

Method: 论文提出冲突指数，用于量化不同任务之间的几何干扰，并设计冲突感知的多任务编辑方法。该方法首先对任务排序，以降低累积冲突；若仅靠排序仍不足以解决问题，则使用动态低秩近似扩展可用零空间，为更新提供更多可行方向。

Result: 在五个功能领域和三种模型骨干上的实验表明，该方法的多任务编辑表现显著优于现有基线，同时较好地保留了模型的通用能力；在任务逐步到达的增量设置下也保持有效。摘要未提供具体数值或显著性检验细节。

Conclusion: 多任务知识编辑的核心瓶颈之一，是异质任务之间可共享的无冲突更新空间不足。通过衡量并管理任务间干扰，再动态扩展可用更新空间，所提出的方法改善了多领域连续编辑的效果与稳定性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) require frequent knowledge updates across heterogeneous domains, but existing editing methods fail when applied sequentially to multi-task settings. We trace this collapse to inter-domain null-space misalignment: heterogeneous tasks induce distinct Jacobian geometries, causing their common null space to shrink dramatically and leaving insufficient admissible directions for conflict-free updates. We propose the Conflict Index to quantify this geometric interference, and introduce Mu-Edit, which mitigates multi-task conflicts via (1) conflict-aware ordering to minimize cumulative interference, and (2) dynamic low-rank approximation to expand the null space when ordering alone is insufficient. Experiments across five functional domains and three backbones show that Mu-Edit significantly outperforms existing baselines in multi-task editing performance while preserving general capabilities, and remains effective under incremental task arrival.

</details>


### [102] [Edge-Native Multimodal RAG: Hardware-Aware Quantization and Architectural Optimization for Consumer GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14924)
*Zahraa Selim,Menna Hamed,Wesam Ahmed,Sohyla Said,Sara Basheer,Rami Zewail*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文针对消费级显卡的显存与带宽限制，整合并评估多模态检索增强生成系统；结果显示，四位正规化浮点量化、独立视觉模块与结构化提示可兼顾长上下文稳定性、视觉能力和推理准确率，使系统适用于显存为十二至十六吉字节的显卡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘人工智能设备的显存有限，难以同时容纳大型语言模型、向量索引和键值缓存。以七十亿参数模型为例，半精度运行约需十四吉字节显存，因而需要在模型速度、长上下文稳定性、视觉能力和整体资源占用之间取得平衡。

Method: 作者在面向图灵架构消费级显卡的系统中，比较生成式预训练变换器量化与四位正规化浮点量化的表现；采用后者处理长上下文，并以独立接入的佛罗伦萨二代视觉模块实现视觉推理。系统还使用结构化提示优化搜索过程，并通过少样本示例弥补量化带来的推理能力损失。

Result: 生成式预训练变换器量化的吞吐量为每秒五十一点六个词元，但在图灵架构上处理二千零四十八个词元及以上的上下文时稳定性较差。四位正规化浮点量化的长上下文表现更稳健，报告的延迟为每词元八十一毫秒。视觉模块仅增加四百三十兆字节显存；结构化提示使延迟降低百分之四十七，而每词元计算量维持在十八点八亿次浮点运算。系统总显存占用为八点三七吉字节。二百次测试中，结构化少样本提示将数学准确率从百分之三十五提升至百分之九十二。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过量化方案选择、模块化视觉接入和提示优化，可以在消费级显卡上构建可运行的多模态检索增强生成流程。其主要贡献是系统级工程整合与实测权衡；结果支持其在十二至十六吉字节显存设备上的可行性，但结论范围应限于文中所测硬件、任务与配置。

Abstract: Deployment of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for Edge-AI is constrained by memory and bandwidth limitations. A standard 7B parameter model consumes 14 GB Video Random Access Memory (VRAM) in half-precision (FP16), precluding co-location of vector indices and Key-Value (KV) caches. This paper presents an engineering integration and empirical evaluation of a multimodal RAG framework optimized for consumer-grade Turing hardware (e.g., NVIDIA Tesla T4). Our system-level analysis identifies a critical trade-off: while generative pre-trained transformer quantization (GPTQ) offers superior throughput (51.6 tok/s), it exhibits significant context instability at 2048+ tokens on Turing architectures. Conversely, NormalFloat 4-bit (NF4) maintains robust long-context performance with viable latency (81 ms/tok). By coupling NF4 with a decoupled Florence-2 vision integration, we achieve visual reasoning with only 430 MB VRAM overhead. Furthermore, empirical observations of prompt-induced search space pruning show that structured prompting reduces latency by 47% without altering computational intensity (1.88 GFLOPs/token). The integrated architecture maintains an 8.37 GB footprint, establishing a viable RAG pipeline for 12-16 GB GPUs. Finally, structured few-shot prompting effectively compensates for quantization-induced reasoning loss, restoring mathematical accuracy from 35.0% to 92.0% across 200 trials.

</details>


### [103] [Provably Optimal HBT Legalization in Seconds: Certified Optima Reveal Where the Headroom Really Lies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14928)
*Kadhir Parani,Manjunath Bezawada,Divya M,Kesana Jathin,Kolli Sarath,Sm Zobaed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文构建了混合键合端点合法化的开放基准，并用带独立最优性证书的精确算法评估多种启发式方法。常规节距下，滑动窗口方法已几乎达到最优；较大改进空间主要出现在窗口缩小或利用率较高时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究报告强化学习合法化器相较滑动窗口启发式可减少百分之十九至二十二的位移，但相关合法化器未公开，且缺少与精确最优解的系统比较。因此，论文旨在确定现有方法距离最优解还有多大差距，并检验已发表的性能提升是否能由基准实例支持。

Method: 作者基于已有三维集成电路布局数据建立包含二十一个设计与节距组合的开放基准，重建贪心、滑动窗口和密度引导等基线方法，并提出精确求解器。该求解器通过逐步生成空间候选边，运行连续最短增广路算法求解分配问题；另以独立对偶证书验证最优性。论文还测试了不同窗口大小及较高利用率下的实例，并与另一独立精确求解器交叉核验部分结果。

Result: 精确解在全部二十一个基准实例上均获证，原始解与对偶证书的相对间隙不超过十的负十六次方量级。在常规节距下，默认配置的滑动窗口方法平均仅比最优解高百分之零点零一一，最差为百分之零点一一九；在十六个存在竞争的实例上，密度引导、贪心方法分别高百分之六点九和百分之六十四点五。扩大窗口后，滑动窗口方法的差距可达百分之三十四点一；在利用率超过约百分之五十八时，三个设计的差距分别达到百分之二十一点一、百分之二十七点五和百分之八点九。精确求解器在作者的测试环境中也比密度引导和滑动窗口方法分别快约八点一倍和四十倍。

Conclusion: 论文表明，常规节距基准上的滑动窗口方法已非常接近最优，因此在这类实例上，若要实现已报道的百分之十九至二十二位移下降，基线必须远离最优；作者测试的常规节距滑动窗口变体并未呈现这种差距。较大的优化空间确实存在，但主要集中于高利用率或特定窗口设置。研究同时发布基准、最优值及证书，为后续方法提供了可复核的评测依据；其结论应理解为针对所研究实例与设置，而非对所有设计情形的普遍判断。

Abstract: Hybrid bonding terminal (HBT) legalization assigns every terminal of a face-to-face 3D IC to its own pitch-grid site with minimum total Manhattan displacement. Open3DBench reports that a reinforcement-learning (RL) legalizer reduces displacement by 19-22% relative to a sliding-window (SW) heuristic, but none of its legalizers is publicly released. We ask how much displacement any legalizer can still recover. We build an open benchmark of 21 design/pitch instances from Open3DBench placement data, with reconstructed greedy, SW (ViaLegal-style) and BTAssign-style baselines, and GEO-Exact, an exact solver that runs successive shortest augmenting paths over lazily generated spatial candidate edges. An independent dual certificate proves its assignment optimal on all 21 instances (relative primal-dual gap at most $8.2 \times 10^{-16}$).
