Conclusion: 概念特征是主动学习式系统综述筛选中值得考虑的补充表示，但其收益并非普遍一致。实际应用应结合具体案例、基础文本表示和本体选择进行评估，而不宜假设单一组合始终最优。

Abstract: The paper examines the choice of text representation for semi-automated systematic literature review (SLR) systems based on active learning. Motivated by the insufficient attention that the bag of concepts representation has received so far, the paper discusses the implementation and experimental evaluation of several variations thereof. The experimental study was performed on medical SLR case studies, with results reported for random forest classification, cluster initialization and uncertainty sampling. The utility of bag of concepts features obtained using different ontologies is evaluated by applying them within the active learning framework to sequentially create classification models based on training data iteratively expanded with expert annotations. Systematic experiments compare BoW, BoC, PubMedBERT, Jina, Gemma and Qwen3 representations, together with combinations of BoC and each of the other representations. The evaluation uses workload savings and performance profiles adapted from optimization benchmarking. The results suggest that concept-based features can provide complementary information, with the BoC-augmented representations achieving higher mean workload savings on the DERP cases than their corresponding base representations. The Qwen3+BoC representation gives the best average performance in this comparison. The effects of augmentation vary across case studies and base representations. The paper also concentrates on the impact of ontology choice on document classification outcomes. In particular, the paper compares the predictive value of bag of concepts representations based on chosen ontologies with that of representations obtained using MedCAT with a UMLS-based dictionary.

</details>


### [205] [From Tokens to Behaviors: Rethinking Generative Recommendation for User Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16159)
*Jiaqi Wang,Hanchen Yang,Hengyu Zhang,Hongxu Ma,Wenqi Rao,Hongyang Wang,Zhongbo Sun,Renliang Qian,Weixin Chen,Wengen Li,Jihong Guan,Shuigeng Zhou,Qingpeng Cai,Peng Jiang,Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向用户行为轨迹预测的生成式推荐框架，通过行为级并行生成、概率反馈和轨迹学习，提升不同预测跨度下的预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐通常逐个自回归解码语义标识，导致同一次交互中的生成步骤相互依赖，并将标识生成与行为状态转移耦合；离散物品反馈难以表达预测不确定性，而教师强制训练与实际生成轨迹之间存在差距。

Method: 框架以单次行为为单位并行预测语义标识，并通过循环状态更新建模行为转移，同时使用语义标识级和物品级监督。其概率反馈机制结合已选行为与对其他候选行为的概率加权摘要，引导后续预测；关系感知的轨迹学习则利用协同转移信号，并在模型生成的轨迹上进行后续行为的信用分配训练。

Result: 在四个公开数据集上的实验表明，该方法在不同预测跨度下均稳定优于当前先进基线。摘要未提供具体指标或提升幅度。

Conclusion: 研究通过重新协调行为生成、反馈和学习过程，缓解了串行解码、反馈不确定性缺失及训练与推理不一致等问题。实验结果支持该框架用于用户未来交互序列预测的有效性。

Abstract: User behavior trajectory prediction (UBTP) aims to capture how a user's needs evolve over time by predicting the sequence of future interactions from that user's interaction history. Recent generative recommendation (GR) methods address this task through autoregressive generation of semantic IDs (SIDs). However, serial SID decoding couples token generation with behavior state transitions and requires multiple dependent steps per interaction. Discrete item feedback overlooks predictive uncertainty, and teacher-forced training leaves a training-inference gap in trajectory generation. To address these limitations, we propose TraGR, a behavior-aware generative recommendation framework that aligns generation, feedback, and learning with successive user behaviors. Its behavior-level architecture predicts SID tokens in parallel within each interaction and models behavioral transitions through recurrent state updates, with joint SID and item-level supervision. Probability feedback combines the selected behavior with a probability-weighted summary of alternatives to guide subsequent predictions. Relation-aware trajectory learning incorporates collaborative transition signals and later credit assignment into training on model-generated trajectories. Experiments on four public datasets demonstrate that TraGR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across different prediction horizons. Code: https://github.com/CalmaQi/GR4UBT.

</details>


### [206] [GA-HEL: A Knowledge-Driven Expert System for Open-Set, Granularity-Aware Hierarchical Event Linking in Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16194)
*Shengqi Zhou,Zhigang Liu,Wenxiang Zhu,Zhentao Cang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向社交媒体事件识别的开放集分层链接方法，先判断帖子是否属于已登记事件，再确定有证据支持的事件层级，并通过规则校验保证输出路径合法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体帖子可能不属于任何已登记事件；即使相关，也未必能可靠地细分到最具体的事件层级。若系统过度深入推断，或输出不合法的层级路径，会损害事件情报的可靠性与可用性。因此，研究需要同时处理开放集拒识、分层判断以及证据不足时及时停止。

Method: 研究将任务建模为基于固定事件卡片的开放集分层事件链接。所提方法采用三阶段、知识驱动的专家系统流程：首先控制候选范围，限制后续推理所接触的候选事件；随后使用考虑层级结构的解码器，选择证据支持的事件路径与深度；最后以确定性规则校验记录，确保路径符合预定义结构并可用于数据库。实验先用二百条帖子开展任务与机制开发，再以独立标注的四百九十七条扩展集评估迁移能力，其中包括四个未参与开发的故事线及困难负例。扩展集金标准标注前，方法实现、提示、事件卡片、候选顺序和六个系统的预测均已冻结。

Result: 评估涵盖端到端分层匹配、开放集拒识、结构校验、消融分析、跨故事线分析，以及对固定审计子集的独立专家复标。所提方法整体上与强大的全卡片推理方案具有竞争力，同时提供明确的候选范围控制和层级合法的结构化记录。误差分析显示，主要遗留问题是证据仅支持集级事件时，系统仍错误地下探至原子事件层级。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了将范围控制、层级推理与确定性结构校验结合起来处理开放集事件链接的可行性。其固定资源、冻结预测和可审计评估流程为后续研究提供了可复现基础；进一步改进的重点是避免证据不足时过度细化事件层级。

Abstract: Social-media event intelligence requires a system to determine whether a post belongs to a registered event space, identify the supported hierarchy path, and stop at the deepest level warranted by the evidence. We formulate this requirement as open-set hierarchical event linking over frozen Storyline--Episode--Atomic Event Cards. GA-HEL implements a three-stage, knowledge-driven expert-system funnel: open-set scope control limits candidate exposure before hierarchical reasoning, hierarchy-aware decoders select a supported path and depth, and deterministic legal-path validation checks each record before database-compatible use. A 200-post Pilot supports task and mechanism development, while a separately annotated 497-post Expansion set evaluates transfer across four held-out Storylines and a hard-negative pool. Method implementations, prompts, Event Cards, candidate order, and six systems' predictions were frozen before Expansion Gold annotation. The resulting evaluation combines end-to-end hierarchical matching, open-set rejection, structural validation, ablation, cross-Storyline analysis, and an independent expert reannotation of a fixed audited subset. GA-HEL remains competitive with strong global full-card reasoning while providing explicit scope control and hierarchy-valid structured records. Error analysis identifies unsupported descent from Episode-level evidence to Atomic Events as the dominant remaining challenge. The task definition, frozen resource, and auditable protocol provide a reproducible foundation for scope-aware event intelligence. Code, Event Cards, labels, frozen predictions, and evaluation scripts are available at https://github.com/SHbiao/GAHEL_release.

</details>


### [207] [CURATOR: Scalable Fine-grained Evidence Selection for Retrieval-Augmented Generation using Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16366)
*Shib Dasgupta,Bandish Parikh,Rashmi Gangadharaiah*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种经过专门训练的证据筛选决策模型，能够从大量候选文本片段中高效、可靠地挑选支持答案的证据，并提升检索增强生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成通常将整段检索文本交给语言模型，但真正与问题相关的证据往往只占少数。逐段判断可能忽略片段之间的关联；结合完整上下文判断虽更可靠，却会反复处理相同文本，计算开销较高。与此同时，现有预训练决策模型在一次处理更多候选片段时，识别相关证据的能力会下降。

Method: 提出并微调一种证据筛选决策模型，通过训练其从规模不同、包含大量候选片段的集合中选出不同数量的支持性片段，使模型在联合评估候选片段及其上下文时，仍能高效且准确地完成证据选择。

Result: 实验表明，该模型在候选片段数量增加时仍保持较强的证据区分能力，并能迁移到不同任务和文档领域。将其用于检索增强生成，可改善下游任务表现，尤其是在可用上下文受限时。

Conclusion: 通过针对大规模候选集合训练决策模型，可以缓解联合筛选中的准确率下降问题，在减少重复计算的同时提升证据选择质量，从而更有效地利用检索到的上下文。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) provides entire retrieved passages to the LLM, even though only a handful of spans within them contain the evidence pertaining to the question. While precise selection of these spans can improve context utilization, identifying them in isolation is challenging. A span may appear irrelevant by itself, and deciding whether to retain it may require examining other retrieved information. One can evaluate each span's candidacy with the full retrieved context, but this is computationally inefficient as the same text must be processed repeatedly. Pretrained decision model can be used to efficiently evaluate multiple spans simultaneously, allowing each decision to consider the surrounding spans as well. However, our experiments show that their ability to identify relevant evidence deteriorates as the number of simultaneous decisions increases.
  To address this, we introduce CURATOR, a fine-tuned decision model trained to make reliable evidence-selection decisions over large candidate sets. By training a decision model to select varying numbers of supporting spans from large candidate pools, CURATOR develops the capability to select evidence for RAG while retaining the computational efficiency of joint decision-making. We observe that CURATOR maintains a strong evidence discrimination capability as the number of candidates increases, generalizes across different tasks and document domains, and improves downstream RAG performance, particularly when the available context is limited.

</details>


### [208] [TriGen: A Generative Paradigm for Trigger-Induced Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16395)
*Muyu Zou,Haibo Xing,Shunye Tang,Liyan Tie,Lingyu Mu,Yandong Wang,Jinxin Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出面向触发诱导推荐的生成式框架，通过融合用户长期偏好与即时触发意图，提升推荐效果；离线评测和六个国家市场的线上实验均显示收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐主要针对用户历史驱动场景，将目标物品视为长期行为序列的后续生成结果，未充分覆盖用户点击某个内容后立即触发后续信息流的场景。在此类场景中，推荐既要迅速响应即时意图，又要维持与长期偏好的连贯性。传统触发推荐方法依赖判别式打分，难以直接指导逐词生成；已有生成模型则缺少协调这两类意图的机制。

Method: 作者提出面向触发诱导推荐的生成式框架“三生成”。该方法通过校准式尾部注入重新编码局部历史；使用动态门控，在稳定偏好与即时意图之间自适应权衡；并对不同生成位置施加特定变换，使触发信号能够在解码的各个步骤中提供分层引导。

Result: 离线评测显示，该方法相较于有竞争力的基线取得了稳定提升。覆盖六个国家市场的线上对照实验进一步表明，该方法显著提升了营收和商品交易总额，并已全面部署至生产环境。摘要未提供具体提升幅度或统计显著性数值。

Conclusion: 该研究针对触发诱导推荐中即时意图与长期偏好的协调难题，构建了相应的生成式推荐范式。实验结果表明，该方法不仅在离线评测中有效，也具备线上业务价值。不过，摘要未说明具体模型细节、评测指标及各项收益幅度。

Abstract: Generative recommendation typically operates under the User-Induced Recommendation (UIR) setting, predicting target items by autoregressively generating semantic IDs as a passive continuation of long-term user history. However, this paradigm overlooks Trigger-Induced Recommendation (TIR), an essential industrial scenario where a user click triggers an immediate follow-up feed. In TIR, recommendations must dynamically align with the instant trigger intent without compromising historical preference continuity. Traditional TIR methods rely on discriminative scoring and cannot guide token-level generation, while existing generative models lack mechanisms to reconcile this structural tension. To address this challenge, we propose TriGen, establishing a tailored generative paradigm for trigger-induced recommendation. TriGen introduces calibrated tail injection to re-encode local history, adaptively arbitrates between persistent tastes and immediate impulses via dynamic gating, and applies position-specific transformations to sustain level-aware trigger guidance across decoding steps. Extensive offline evaluations confirm consistent gains over competitive baselines. Furthermore, online A/B tests across six country markets demonstrate significant revenue and GMV improvements, leading to the full production deployment of TriGen.

</details>


### [209] [ReSID: Rethinking Long-Tail Retrieval in Generative Recommendation through Item Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16404)
*Muyu Zou,Shunye Tang,Yi Xu,Haibo Xing,Yandong Wang,Lingyu Mu,Jinxin Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文研究生成式推荐中的长尾物品召回，提出兼顾候选保留与结果恢复的方法，在提升长尾召回的同时保持整体准确率，并改善推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐将物品编码为离散语义标识符，并通过序列生成完成召回。但交互分布偏斜与线上服务预算有限，使长尾物品容易在标识符构建、搜索解码或匹配环节中被遗漏。论文旨在系统分析完整流程中的长尾可达性，并解决长尾候选难以进入最终召回结果的问题。

Method: 研究发现，在编码长度相同的条件下，并行构建语义标识符比顺序构建更有利于长尾物品；即使未能完整生成标识符，部分生成序列仍包含局部一致信息，可通过部分匹配找回相关物品。不过，这种恢复受上游束搜索限制：若搜索剪除了所有处于允许偏差范围内、仍可补全的前缀，后续匹配便无法恢复候选。基于此，论文提出一种联合保留与恢复长尾候选的方法：采用较长的并行语义标识符，结合降低热门度偏置且感知用户语义的束搜索保留潜在路径，再利用部分标识符匹配，从搜索保留下来的词元中恢复物品。

Result: 该方法在长尾物品召回方面达到当前最佳水平，同时保持有竞争力的整体准确率；在束宽较窄时，还能实现更低延迟与更高吞吐量。论文称该方法已部署于生产系统并服务主要用户流量，带来了显著业务收益；作者同时公开代码与模型参数以支持复现和实际部署。

Conclusion: 长尾召回不仅取决于最终匹配，也受到标识符构建与搜索解码的共同制约。论文通过并行标识符构建、偏置校正的搜索和部分匹配恢复，形成端到端的长尾候选保留与找回方案，并展示了其在效果、效率和实际部署方面的价值。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) represents items with discrete Semantic IDs (SIDs) and casts retrieval as sequence generation. Under skewed interaction distributions and tight serving budgets, GR struggles to retrieve long-tail items. In this work, we are the first to study long-tail reachability across the complete pipeline of SID construction, decoding, and matching. We find that under equal code lengths, parallel SID construction consistently outperforms sequential SIDs on tail items. Moreover, even when full generation fails, decoded sequences carry locally consistent information that retrieves relevant tail items via partial matching. Yet this recovery is bounded by upstream search, failing once beam search prunes all prefixes completable within a fixed mismatch tolerance. Motivated by these findings, we propose ReSID to jointly preserve and recover tail candidates. Built on long parallel SIDs, ReSID pairs popularity-debiased and user-semantic-aware beam search to keep promising paths alive with partial SID matching to recover items from surviving tokens. ReSID achieves state-of-the-art long-tail retrieval while maintaining competitive overall accuracy, with lower latency and higher throughput under narrow beams. ReSID is deployed in our production system, serving main user traffic with substantial business gains. We release our complete code and model checkpoints to support full reproducibility and real-world deployment at https://github.com/Gabriel-reina/ReSID.

</details>


### [210] [AntEmbed: Dual-Mode Multimodal Embedding Model for Efficient and Fine-Grained Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16405)
*Kehang Jia,Teng Liu,Juntao Li,Hua Chen,Jinxiong Chang,Xiaobo Liang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种兼顾高效检索与细粒度语义匹配的多模态嵌入模型，通过统一嵌入空间和跨模式自蒸馏提升检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判别式嵌入计算高效，但难以捕捉细粒度语义；生成式嵌入能够借助推理理解查询意图和相关性证据，却需要更多计算。本文旨在结合两者优势，同时避免在线推理带来的额外开销。

Method: 首先构造面向检索的推理内容，刻画查询意图及易混淆候选之间的语义证据，并将原始候选与推理增强候选视为语义等价的不同视图，通过多正例对比学习，以单一嵌入标记对齐判别式与生成式嵌入。其次，采用跨模式自蒸馏，将推理增强表示中的细粒度语义迁移至原始表示，具体包括候选相似度分布蒸馏和查询表示对齐。两种检索模式共用同一候选索引。

Result: 在多模态嵌入基准第二版上，该模型的八十亿参数版本使用推理增强嵌入取得八十一点五分，较最强基线高三点一分。

Conclusion: 该方法在统一嵌入空间中整合高效判别式检索与推理增强的细粒度匹配，并通过离线蒸馏提升原始表示，使模型无需在线额外推理也能增强语义检索能力。

Abstract: Discriminative embeddings encode original multimodal inputs efficiently but struggle with fine-grained semantics. Generative embeddings use reasoning to capture query intent and relevance evidence, but incur higher computational costs. We propose AntEmbed, a dual-mode multimodal embedding model that supports efficient retrieval and reasoning-enhanced fine-grained matching in a unified embedding space. First, we construct retrieval-oriented reasoning content that captures query intent and confusion-aware semantic evidence. We treat original and reasoning-enhanced candidate inputs as semantically equivalent views, and use multi-positive contrastive learning with a single embedding token to align discriminative and generative embeddings. Second, cross-mode self-distillation transfers fine-grained semantic information from reasoning-enhanced to original representations through candidate similarity-distribution distillation and query representation alignment. This strengthens discriminative embeddings without requiring additional online reasoning. Both retrieval modes share a single candidate index. On MMEB-v2, AntEmbed-8B achieves an overall score of 81.5 with reasoning-enhanced embeddings, outperforming the strongest baseline by 3.1 points.

</details>


### [211] [RMIT-ADM+S at TREC RAG 2026: Research Agents, Retriever Ablations, and Open-Weight Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16425)
*Oleg Zendel,Kun Ran,Chenglong Ma,Nguyen Khoi Vo,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该团队在检索增强生成评测中比较了多种检索、写作模型与智能体流程；六项提交的综合表现高于赛道中位数，最佳方案在覆盖率方面表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索如何通过检索策略、答案写作模型及智能体工作流程的组合，提升检索增强生成系统的回答质量，并考察自动化覆盖率指标与人工偏好是否一致。

Method: 团队使用克莱姆混合语料提交了七组研究智能体实验，采用专有模型和开放权重模型，并比较不同检索策略、写作模型和智能体流程。提交前的消融实验重点考察写作模型选择与相邻文本块扩展；正式评估则结合自动化评分和分批次的人工偏好比较。

Result: 正式评测中，七组方案有六组在三个评分与要点集合上的表现均超过赛道中位数。简洁混合方案在团队提交中实现了最高的全项覆盖率，并以零点八九二的批次一关键覆盖率并列赛道最高。人工偏好因批次而异：开放权重模型方案在批次一领先，研究优先方案在批次二领先，尽管前者的自动化覆盖率较低。

Conclusion: 结果表明，写作模型选择和相邻文本块扩展是影响回答质量的重要因素；自动化覆盖率并不能完全代表人工偏好。开放权重模型也展现出构建低成本研究智能体系统的潜力。

Abstract: We describe the RMIT-ADM+S team's participation in the TREC 2026 Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) track using the ClimbMix corpus. We submitted seven research-agent runs using proprietary OpenAI and open-weight models, exploring different retrieval strategies, writer models, and agent workflows. Pre-submission ablations identified writer selection and adjacent-chunk expansion as the main factors affecting answer quality. In the official NIST evaluation, six of seven runs exceeded the track median across all three rubric/nugget collections. Our best-performing run, brief-hybrid, achieved the highest all-item coverage among our submissions and tied the track maximum (0.892) for Batch-1 vital coverage. Human side-by-side preferences differed across evaluation batches: the open-weight owa-glm5 led Batch 1 despite lower automated coverage, while sol-research-first led Batch 2. These findings highlight differences between automated coverage measures and human preferences, as well as the potential of lower-cost open-weight models for research-agent systems.

</details>


### [212] [Active Learning of Approximate LLM Ranking for Energy-Efficient Retrieval of Long Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16488)
*Ohad Porat,Elad Yom-Tov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种结合轻量级预测器与主动学习的混合检索框架，通过让大语言模型只评估少量高信息量文档，在大幅降低能耗的同时保留较强的检索效果，并提供可按预算调节的准确率—能耗权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词项匹配检索速度快、能耗低，但对长篇、语境丰富的查询理解有限；大语言模型能捕捉语义并处理复杂查询，却因推理成本和能耗过高而难以大规模使用。论文旨在弥合两者之间的差距，使检索质量与计算预算能够灵活权衡。

Method: 提出大语言模型主动近似模型：在语义检索与大语言模型重排之间加入轻量级学习预测器，并通过主动学习迭代改进。大语言模型只需为少量最具信息量的文档提供相关性标注，预测器据此近似其判断，从而减少大语言模型推理次数。研究使用词项检索系统与深度求索第三代模型，在不同查询详尽程度下进行评估。

Result: 在稳健检索二〇〇四测试集的详尽查询条件下，该方法的前十位平均准确率达到大语言模型重排的百分之八十七，数值分别为零点四五九和零点五二六；但每条查询能耗仅为其百分之三点三。统计检验显示，该方法显著优于词项检索基线；在平均准确率上与大语言模型无显著差异，但在平均精度均值上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 通过主动学习选择性地近似大语言模型的相关性判断，可以用评估预算直接控制检索质量及相应能耗。该框架不局限于低成本但较不准确的词项检索，或高成本的大语言模型全面重排，而是提供一系列可调节的能耗—准确率工作点，便于实践者依据计算预算选择方案。

Abstract: Search engines and Large Language Models (LLMs) offer complementary strengths for document retrieval: lexical retrievers which rely on keyword matching are fast and energy-efficient, whereas LLMs capture semantic meaning and excel at interpreting long, context-rich queries. However, this comes at a cost that is computationally and energetically prohibitive.
  We propose the LLM Active Approximation Model (LAAM), a hybrid retrieval framework that introduces a lightweight learned predictor which serves as an intermediary between semantic retrieval and LLM retrieval. LAAM is refined through an active learning loop where an LLM labels a small number of documents. By concentrating LLM evaluations on the most informative documents, the framework substantially reduces inference cost while preserving retrieval effectiveness.
  We evaluate the proposed framework on TREC Robust 2004 using Lucene with BM25 retrieval and DeepSeek v3 as the LLM, under three levels of query verbosity. For verbose queries, LAAM achieves 87% of average P@10 achieved by DeepSeek's reranking (0.459 vs. 0.526), while requiring only 3.3% of the per-query energy consumption. LAAM is statistically significantly better than BM25 and is indistinguishable from DeepSeek on average precision, though not on MAP (Friedman test, P<0.05 with post-hoc testing).
  Overall, our results demonstrate that selectively approximating LLM relevance judgments through active learning enables retrieval accuracy to be controlled directly through the evaluation (and consequently energy) budget. Rather than committing to either inexpensive (and less accurate) lexical retrieval or exhaustive (and expensive) LLM reranking, our framework exposes a continuous spectrum of operating points along the energy--accuracy curve, allowing practitioners to select how to trade retrieval quality with computational budget.

</details>


### [213] [KuaiRecV2: Benchmarking Large-Scale Continual Learning for Diversified and Multi-task Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16795)
*Chenxu Li,Shuchang Liu,Hantao Shu,Wei Yuan,Zhe Xu,Guoqing Hu,Yan Wang,Biao Yang,Xiang Li,Cheng Ling,Fan Yang,Yongqi Liu,Lantao Hu,Han Li,Kaiqiao Zhan,Kun Gai,An Zhang,Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出面向真实工业推荐场景的快手推荐第二版基准，重点评估持续学习、推荐多样性与多任务表现，弥合离线评测和线上效果之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统虽受益于语义表示和模型、数据规模的提升，但简单离线评测仍常与复杂线上结果不一致。作者认为，现有评测未充分覆盖动态候选池与分布变化下的长期效果、多层次准确性与多样性权衡，以及生成式模型的多任务可控性。

Method: 构建工业级推荐基准，提供包含百万规模多模态物品信息、连续三十天长期交互记录及动态视频候选池的数据集；同时建立统一评测体系，综合衡量召回准确性、多样性和多任务表现，并规定只有所有指标均得到改善，才视为有效进步。

Result: 摘要报告了基准的数据与评测设计，但未提供具体模型实验结果或性能提升数值。因此，不能据此判断某种方法已在各项指标上取得提升。

Conclusion: 该工作通过构造更贴近工业实际的数据集和综合评测标准，推动推荐研究从单一离线准确率转向兼顾长期适应性、结果多样性与多任务效果的评估。其价值主要在于提供研究基准，而非提出并验证一种新的推荐模型。

Abstract: Recommender systems, which retrieve items of interest to users based on their interaction histories, have recently witnessed several key technological advancements, benefiting from the semantic representation space and model-data scaling. Nevertheless, practitioners and researchers have frequently encountered critical inconsistencies between simple offline evaluations and complex online results, and this gap still exists in the era of large models. In this work, we address several key factors that contribute to this inconsistency: 1) the \textit{large-scale continual learning challenge} that assumes a practical demand to maintain long-term model effectiveness under dynamic item pools and distributional shifts; 2) the \textit{combinatorial diversity challenge} that requires the generation strategy to solve the accuracy-diversity trade-off in both the finer token level and the holistic item level; and 3) the \textit{multi-task reward balancing challenge}, which is particularly critical for modern generative models that can accurately model the sequence distribution but are less controllable for the multi-task demands.
  To address these challenges and further pave the way for the development of more realistic recommendation solutions, we introduce KuaiRecV2, a deliberately constructed benchmark with industrial-level datasets from Kuaishou. Specifically, we provide a large-scale interaction dataset with million-scale multi-modal item information and long-term interaction records across 30 days, with a dynamic video candidate pool. Additionally, we provide a rigorous benchmark that evaluates retrieval accuracy, diversity, and multi-task performance in one rubric system, where an effective advancement occurs if and only if the new method improves all metrics. The code is available at https://github.com/cxli2333/KuaiRecV2

</details>


### [214] [SignalAudit-QD: A Reproducible Audit Resource for External Signals in AI-Query Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16947)
*Amir Rafe,Iman Jamshidi,Gourab Sil,Subasish Das*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文发布一套可复现的审计资源，用于检验专利信号能否在搜索历史之外提升人工智能相关趋势预测；指定实验未发现明确收益，也不能据此证明两者等效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部信号常被用于提升时间检索与趋势预测，但其是否确实提供超出既有查询历史的信息，容易受到数据时点、测量口径及实验可复现性影响。本文旨在建立可审计的评估框架，并审慎检验这一主张。

Method: 研究构建固定的美国人工智能二十个术语面板，对齐两份归档的谷歌趋势数据提取结果与八类美国专利商标局人工智能专利公开数据流，发布按预测起点索引的预测、配对损失、数据来源记录、可执行测试及防篡改运行清单。实验在冻结的数据测量版本中进行伪起点评估，而非模拟当时实际可用的信息；主要比较是在一个月预测期内，检验向水平岭回归模型加入由两个分量构成的专利特征块的效果，并辅以替代模型、时间匹配控制、植入信号测试及高关注度警报示例。

Result: 主要比较中，加入专利特征后，宏观平均绝对误差增加0.0012个趋势点；条件式百分之九十五移动区块区间为负0.0363至正0.0387。因此，结果既未确立整体收益，也未证明效果等同。以持续性为基准的模型和非线性模型出现依赖模型设定的误差增加，其中持续性锚定模型的恶化很小。时间匹配控制揭示了数据可用性假设；植入信号测试仅能检出测试中幅度最大的注入。高关注度警报展示了发布接口，但不能证明其在线应用价值。其他特征维度、语义映射、专利定义、第二份数据提取结果及三个月预测期仍未解决。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献不是证明专利信号有效，而是提供一个范围明确、可复现且可扩展的负面案例与证据记录规范。由于输入数据是在所模拟的二〇二〇至二〇二三年预测起点之后回溯重建，结论仅适用于冻结测量版本下的伪起点评估，不能视为历史部署效果或普遍无效的证据。

Abstract: SignalAudit-QD is a reproducible audit resource for testing whether a dated public signal adds information beyond query history in a fixed 20-term U.S. AI panel. It aligns two archived Google Trends extractions with eight USPTO AI-patent publication streams and releases origin-indexed predictions, paired losses, provenance, executable tests, and tamper-evident run manifests. Both inputs are retrospective reconstructions obtained after the modeled 2020--2023 origins; the task is therefore a pseudo-origin assay within frozen measurement vintages, not a historical deployment simulation. In the designated one-month comparison, adding a two-component patent block to a level ridge changes macro mean absolute error by +0.0012 Trends points, with conditional 95% moving-block interval [-0.0363,+0.0387]. This establishes neither aggregate benefit nor equivalence. Alternative dimensions, semantic mapping, patent definitions, a second extraction, and a three-month horizon remain unresolved; persistence-anchored and nonlinear hosts instead show model-specific worsening, tiny for the anchored fit. Matched timing controls expose availability assumptions, while a planted-signal assay detects only the largest tested injection. A within-query high-interest alert demonstrates the released interface but not online utility. The contribution is a bounded negative case study and an extensible evidence schema for auditing external-signal claims in temporal information retrieval.

</details>


### [215] [Evaluation of Music Version Retrieval Methods for Setlist Identification in Live Concerts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17070)
*Andres Ferraro,Albin Correya*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究比较多种音乐版本检索方法在现场演出曲目单识别中的表现，发现紧凑型神经音频嵌入在准确率和存储效率上均具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现场演出录音常伴有噪声、音质变化和编曲差异，且需要高效处理长时间连续音频。传统音乐版本识别方法未必适用于这些真实场景，因此有必要评估现有检索方法及其规模化应用能力。

Method: 研究使用阿西德基准，对比既有的再移动系统、克留斯方法和内部研发的神经音频版本嵌入模型。另评估音乐与非音乐片段筛选对识别效果及时间覆盖范围的影响。

Result: 神经音频嵌入模型与克留斯方法均显著优于再移动系统。神经音频嵌入模型使用二百五十六维表示，维度仅为克留斯方法的四分之一，却取得更好的检索表现，因而可大幅降低嵌入存储需求。音乐片段筛选带来小幅检测和时间覆盖改善；定性分析还显示，它可能使识别边界更准确，而标准评测指标未能充分体现这一点。

Conclusion: 基于片段级音频嵌入的方法适合构建可扩展的现场曲目单识别系统。紧凑表示能够在真实演出条件下保持较强准确性，同时显著降低存储与服务成本。

Abstract: Automatic setlist identification aims to identify the songs performed in live concert recordings by matching noisy and variable-quality audio against a database of reference tracks. This task presents challenges beyond conventional music version identification, including substantial arrangement differences, background noise, and the need to process continuous recordings efficiently at large scale. In this work, we evaluate modern music version retrieval methods for setlist identification using the ASID benchmark, comparing the established Re-MOVE system with CLEWS and NAVE, an internal neural audio version embedding model. Our results show that NAVE and CLEWS substantially outperform Re-MOVE. Notably, NAVE uses 256-dimensional embeddings, compared with 1024 dimensions for CLEWS, providing a four-fold reduction in embedding storage while achieving better retrieval performance. We further evaluate music/non-music gating and find modest improvements in detection and temporal coverage, while qualitative analysis suggests that such preprocessing can improve recognition boundaries beyond what the standard ASID metrics capture. Overall, the results demonstrate that segment-level audio embeddings provide a strong basis for scalable setlist identification and that compact representations can achieve strong accuracy under realistic live-concert conditions at much lower storage and serving cost.

</details>


### [216] [Sheaf-Inspired Structured Scoring Extensions of LightGCN for Collaborative Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17226)
*Ksenia Belkovskaya,Timur Lidzhiev,Maria Stebliy,Alexander Tarakanov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文在三层轻量图卷积推荐模型之上加入受层论启发的结构化评分器，并与多种评分器及参数量匹配的多层感知机基线比较。结果显示，排序效果变化幅度有限且因数据集而异，目录覆盖率有时提升较明显；实验并未证明层论几何本身带来收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图协同过滤通常通过用户—物品交互图传播嵌入，再用简单内积计算偏好分数。论文希望借鉴层论中局部向量空间由线性限制映射联系的思想，为用户与物品嵌入加入依赖具体配对的结构化变换，同时不改变原有图传播过程。

Method: 以三层轻量图卷积网络为骨干，比较内积评分器、对角残差评分器、秩一低秩残差评分器、完整矩阵残差评分器，以及参数量匹配的残差多层感知机基线。在亚马逊图书、戈瓦拉、电影镜头一百万和 Yelp 二〇一八数据集上，评估前十和前二十名的排序质量与目录覆盖率。

Result: 不同结构化评分器带来的排序质量变化较小，并随数据集而变化；目录覆盖率在部分设置下则有较明显变化。摘要未提供具体数值，也未指出某一种评分器在所有数据集上稳定占优。

Conclusion: 结构化的配对评分器可能影响推荐结果的覆盖范围，但其对排序质量的收益并不稳定。该方法仅受层论几何启发，既未构造胞腔层，也未实现层拉普拉斯扩散，因此实验不能据此证明层论几何本身具有因果性优势。

Abstract: Graph-based collaborative filtering learns user and item representations by propagating embeddings over a user--item interaction graph. We investigate a modular, sheaf-inspired scoring extension placed on top of a three-layer LightGCN backbone. The motivation comes from cellular sheaves, where local vector spaces are related by linear restriction maps; here, this idea motivates structured, pair-dependent transformations of user and item embeddings rather than a change to graph propagation. We compare the identity inner-product scorer, diagonal, rank-one low-rank, and full matrix residual scorers, and a parameter-matched residual MLP baseline (\texttt{mlp\_full}) on Amazon-Book, Gowalla, MovieLens-1M, and Yelp2018. We report ranking quality and catalog coverage at cutoffs 10 and 20. The selected structured variants show modest, dataset-dependent changes in ranking quality and sometimes substantial changes in coverage. Our method is inspired by sheaf geometry but does not construct a cellular sheaf or implement sheaf-Laplacian diffusion; the experiments therefore do not establish a causal benefit from sheaf geometry itself.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [217] [CAFIN: CUDA-accelerated Adaptive Fisher Information Matrix Network Optimization via GPU-Native Kernel Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14589)
*Maryam Arshad,Muhammad Khuram Shahzad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出面向图形处理器的内核融合框架，在不改变阿达费舍尔优化器数学形式的前提下，减少执行开销、内存访问和临时分配，并维持相近的收敛表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶优化器利用曲率信息改善深度神经网络训练，但实际效率常受图形处理器执行开销限制。阿达费舍尔优化器虽通过对角克罗内克结构和指数移动平均降低费舍尔信息矩阵的近似成本，其原有实现仍需依次启动多个计算内核、分配中间张量并重复访问内存，因而存在进一步提速空间。

Method: 提出内核融合框架，将费舍尔信息矩阵预条件器构造、指数移动平均更新、梯度预条件、动量累积、权重衰减和参数更新逐步整合进融合计算内核。进一步利用共享内存复用、寄存器驻留计算及原位操作，减少内存流量和中间存储分配，同时保持优化算法本身不变。

Result: 在多种图像分类数据集、两款图形处理器上使用残差网络进行实验。相较原实现，费舍尔信息矩阵构造的单独延迟最高降低百分之七十八点四，优化器单步延迟降低百分之二点一至百分之二十六点九；每个优化步骤约减少一千八百九十一万字节的中间显存分配。数值结果符合预设的单精度浮点误差容限，且不同数据集和随机种子下的收敛精度相近。

Conclusion: 实验表明，图形处理器计算内核融合能够显著提升阿达费舍尔二阶优化的执行效率，并减少显存开销，同时保留其数学形式及学习行为。

Abstract: Second-order optimizers improve deep neural network training using curvature information, but their practical efficiency is limited by GPU execution overhead. AdaFisher reduces Fisher Information Matrix (FIM) approximation costs through diagonal Kronecker structures and exponential moving averages, yet its PyTorch implementation requires sequential CUDA kernel launches, intermediate tensor allocations, and repeated memory accesses. We introduce CAFIN, a GPU-native CUDA kernel fusion framework that improves AdaFisher execution efficiency without changing its optimization formulation. The proposed approach progressively integrates FIM preconditioner construction, exponential moving average updates, gradient preconditioning, momentum accumulation, weight decay, and parameter updates into fused CUDA kernels. Shared-memory reuse, register-resident computation, and in-place operations further reduce memory traffic and intermediate allocations. Experiments using ResNet-18 on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny ImageNet across NVIDIA GTX 1660 Ti and RTX 3060 Laptop GPUs demonstrate up to 78.4% lower isolated FIM construction latency and 2.1-26.9% lower optimizer-step latency. The implementation also eliminates approximately 18.91 MB of intermediate GPU memory allocations per optimizer step. Numerical validation confirms consistency within the predefined FP32 tolerance, while convergence accuracy remains comparable across evaluated datasets and random seeds. These findings demonstrate that CUDA kernel fusion can substantially accelerate second-order optimization while preserving AdaFisher's underlying mathematical formulation and learning behavior.

</details>


### [218] [An Asynchronous Federated Learning Platform for Lung Adenocarcinoma Grading via Foundation Models and Structural Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14590)
*Moon-Il Joo,Md Ariful Islam Mozumder,Hee-Cheol Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究构建了面向肺腺癌病理切片的网页式联邦学习系统，在不交换患者数据的前提下进行跨机构训练。结果显示，联邦归一化算法结合百分之四十结构化剪枝，兼具较好的分类表现与通信效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺腺癌低分化与中高分化亚型的区分关系到患者预后，但病理医师之间存在判断差异，影响分类一致性。多家医院的数据联合使用有助于提升模型泛化能力，却受到患者隐私保护的限制，因此需要一种能够整合跨机构信息、又不直接共享病理数据的学习方案。

Method: 研究使用统一二号基础模型提取病理全切片图像特征，并采用单分支聚类注意力学习方法进行分类，目标为区分低分化与中高分化亚型。研究搭建了包含四个客户端的联邦学习系统，对比联邦平均、联邦近端、联邦批归一化和联邦归一化四种算法；另以北美肺癌筛查试验队列进行外部验证。为降低模型传输量，研究对模型实施百分之二十、百分之三十和百分之四十稀疏度的绝对值范数结构化剪枝，并开发支持切片上传、本地训练、人工智能分类及注意力热图展示的网页应用。

Result: 集中式评估中，联合使用临床蛋白质组肿瘤分析联盟与癌症基因组图谱数据，外部验证表现优于单机构训练，说明多机构数据整合具有价值。联邦算法的测试曲线下面积依次为：联邦批归一化、联邦近端、联邦归一化、联邦平均；其中联邦归一化对低分化和中高分化两类的分类表现最为均衡。剪枝后，联邦平均、联邦近端和联邦批归一化的测试曲线下面积总体呈下降趋势，而联邦归一化在所有测试稀疏度下均有所提升。联邦归一化结合百分之四十剪枝被验证为兼顾分类效果与通信效率的实用方案。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，联邦学习可在保护患者隐私的同时支持跨机构肺腺癌病理图像分类。联邦归一化算法配合百分之四十结构化剪枝，有望提升外部分类表现并减少模型传输负担；所开发的网页系统为此类方法的实际应用提供了流程基础。

Abstract: Classification of lung adenocarcinoma into poorly differentiated (PD) and well-to-moderately differentiated (WMD) subtypes plays an important role in patient survival; however, consistent classification is difficult due to inter-observer variability among pathologists. Collecting data from multiple hospitals is also challenging due to patient privacy concerns. This study proposes a web-based federated learning system that shares only locally trained model weights without sharing patient data between institutions. The UNI2 foundation model and CLAM-SB were used to classify pathological whole slide images. A four-client federated learning system was constructed, and the NLST cohort was reserved exclusively for external validation. In centralized evaluation, joint training with CPTAC and TCGA outperformed single-institution training on external validation, confirming the necessity of multi-institutional data integration. FedAvg, FedProx, FedBN, and FedNova were compared as federated learning algorithms under identical experimental conditions. Test AUC ranked as FedBN, FedProx, FedNova, and FedAvg. FedNova showed the most balanced classification performance between PD and WMD. L1-norm structural pruning at 20%, 30%, and 40% sparsity was applied to reduce model transmission size. FedAvg, FedProx, and FedBN showed a tendency of decreasing Test AUC under pruning, while FedNova achieved improved Test AUC across all sparsity levels. A web-based application was developed supporting slide upload, local training, AI classification, and attention heatmap-based visualization. The combination of FedNova and 40% pruning was confirmed as a practical approach that improves communication efficiency and classification performance while protecting patient privacy.

</details>


### [219] [Adversarial Risk in the Generative AI Era Necessitates Dropping the Small Epsilon Ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14594)
*Andrew C. Cullen,Neil G. Marchant,Paul Montague,Jiani Xie,Benjamin I. P. Rubinstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文批评对“微小且不可察觉扰动”的过度关注，认为以ℓₚ范数定义对抗威胁会造成错误的安全认知，并可能转移防御重点、助长实际风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对抗机器学习研究常将寻找最小扰动作为核心目标，尤其强调在ℓₚ范数约束下构造可欺骗模型、又不易被人察觉的输入。作者认为，这种研究取向与现实系统的脆弱性并不匹配，可能让防御者误判风险。

Method: 本文采用概念性论证，审视以ℓₚ范数球界定对抗威胁的研究范式及其对防御实践的影响。作者指出，该范式可能使防御资源集中于狭窄的数学鲁棒性指标，而忽略其他并不重合的攻击路径；摘要未说明具体实验或量化评估方法。

Result: 作者提出，ℓₚ范数主导的研究范式可能传递失真的风险图景，使防御者针对错误威胁优化，并给攻击者留下利用其他对抗路径的空间。因此，这种范式未必能减少危害，甚至可能因制造虚假的安全感而加剧危害。

Conclusion: 评估机器学习系统安全性不应局限于ℓₚ范数约束下的微小扰动。作者主张将关注点从狭义的数学鲁棒性扩展到系统层面的真实脆弱性与多样化攻击路径，以避免防御措施与现实风险脱节。

Abstract: Adversarial Machine Learning research is currently centered on the search for the epsilon-ball: minimal perturbations designed to fool models while remaining imperceptible to humans. We argue that this fixation on $\ell_p$ norms is a metrological trap that communicates a distorted perception of risk to defenders, one that is unaligned with real-world system vulnerabilities. This paradigm misleads defenders into optimizing for narrow, mathematical robustness at the expense of systemic security, while simultaneously incentivizing attackers to exploit non-overlapping alternative adversarial pathways. Ultimately, we argue that the dominance of the $\ell_p$ ball does not just fail to reduce harm---it may actively facilitate it by communicating a false sense of security against the wrong threats.

</details>


### [220] [StructRL: Model-Free Reinforcement Learning with Replay Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14599)
*Ivo Nowak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 结构强化学习将探索经验构造成可回放的状态转移图，并利用图上规划结果指导深度强化学习；在若干稀疏奖励任务中显著提升成功率，但效果受图覆盖、状态混淆及高维信息损失限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励任务中，智能体难以获得有效反馈，普通无模型强化学习可能长期无法发现通往目标的路径。论文希望把训练过程中已经获得的转移经验显式保存并加以规划，再将规划结果用于改善学习。

Method: 将强化学习期间收集的经验组织为显式回放图。当图中出现通往目标的路径时，在已观测到的状态与转移范围内使用动态规划选择动作，形成仅适用于图中已知区域的策略。随后将策略知识迁移给无模型学习器：对深度Q网络提供动作偏好，对软演员—评论家提供沿已观测最短路径前进的方向目标；也可将图策略蒸馏到新的Q网络中。

Result: 在三个稀疏奖励迷你网格任务中，深度Q网络的最终成功率分别提升至门钥任务的0.90、钥匙走廊任务的0.80和障碍迷宫任务的0.50；未使用图引导的基线即使训练预算增加至三倍也未能追上。在连续点迷宫任务中，图生成的方向标签使软演员—评论家的最终成功率在七次匹配实验中升至1.0，基线为0.17。将图策略蒸馏到新Q网络后，六乘六门钥环境中可从91%的可达状态到达目标，而普通深度Q网络为38%。

Conclusion: 显式经验图能够把已探索到的结构转化为规划信号，并有效加速稀疏奖励任务中的策略学习与迁移。不过，其性能依赖图是否覆盖关键状态和转移；状态结构混淆以及高维输入中图信息的损失，也构成扩展应用的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: StructRL turns the experience collected during reinforcement learning into an explicit replay graph. Once the graph contains a path to the task goal, dynamic programming selects actions on the observed part of the state space; these actions form a graph policy that is defined only where the required transitions have been observed. StructRL transfers these actions to model-free learners: as action preferences for a DQN Q-model and as target directions along shortest observed paths for a SAC actor. For DQN on three sparse-reward MiniGrid tasks, final success rises from 0.10 to 0.90 (DoorKey), 0.10 to 0.80 (KeyCorridor) and 0 to 0.50 (ObstructedMaze); plain DQN does not catch up with three times the budget. For SAC on continuous PointMaze, graph-derived direction labels raise final success from 0.17 to 1.0 on seven matched runs that reached the goal. Distilling the graph policy into a fresh Q-model gives a DoorKey 6x6 controller that reaches the goal from 91% of reachable states, against 38% for plain DQN. Scaling experiments identify three bottlenecks: incomplete graph support, structural aliasing, and loss of graph information in high-dimensional inputs.

</details>


### [221] [KernelMosaic: An Open Dataset and Agentic Framework for CUDA Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14604)
*Ningyuan Xi,Yuhang Lai,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KernelMosaic 是一个包含多样化 CUDA 任务、经验证实现及优化过程轨迹的数据集；使用其轨迹进行微调，能显著提升小型开源模型生成正确且高效 CUDA 内核的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练大语言模型生成高效 CUDA 内核，需要覆盖不同计算需求的监督数据，也需要呈现反复测试和优化的过程。但任务规格形式多样，可靠实现往往依赖复杂的迭代验证与修订，因此构建高质量监督数据具有挑战性。

Method: 研究者构建 KernelMosaic，结合人工审核与关注多样性的筛选，收录以自然语言描述、参考程序、数学规格及其组合形式表达的任务，并配对经过验证的解答和过程轨迹。其智能体框架引导强语言模型完成内核实现、正确性修复与性能优化，并利用可复用的 CUDA 指导和执行反馈迭代改进。随后以数据集对小型开源模型进行监督微调，并比较过程轨迹监督与直接回答监督的效果。

Result: 微调后，模型在 KernelBench 的第一级、第二级和第三级任务上的正确率，分别从 4%、0%、0% 提升至 88%、82%、16%；在各级任务中，生成速度快于 PyTorch 急切执行的内核比例也有所提高。进一步实验显示，经过整理的优化轨迹显著优于直接回答，并促使模型持续进行测试和修订。

Conclusion: KernelMosaic 为 CUDA 内核生成提供了兼具任务多样性、验证结果和迭代过程的监督资源。实验表明，利用优化轨迹训练能够使通用模型在正确性与性能方面取得显著进步。

Abstract: Training large language models to generate efficient CUDA kernels requires supervision spanning diverse computational requirements and iterative code optimization. Constructing this supervision is challenging because of heterogeneous specifications, and useful implementations emerge through iterative complicated testing and refinement. We introduce KernelMosaic, an open dataset pairing diverse CUDA tasks with verified solutions and trajectories. Human-guided review and diversity-aware selection curate tasks expressed as natural-language descriptions, reference programs, mathematical specifications, and their intersections. To build the solutions and trajectories, our agentic framework guides a strong LLM through implementation, correctness repair, and performance optimization with reusable CUDA guidance and execution feedback. Supervised fine-tuning of a small open-source model on KernelMosaic raises correctness from 4%, 0%, and 0% to 88%, 82%, and 16% on KernelBench L1, L2, and L3, respectively, while increasing the fraction of tasks with kernels faster than PyTorch eager at every level. Further training experiments show that supervision from the curated trajectories substantially outperforms direct responses and promotes sustained testing and revision. These results demonstrate that KernelMosaic enables a general-purpose model to achieve substantial gains in CUDA kernel generation.

</details>


### [222] [Thompson Sampling using Prior-fitted Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14656)
*Sihwa Park,Jingsen Zhu,Vinamr Jain,Sheng-Yen Chou,Alexander Terenin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种预训练扩散变换器，使其能在推理时通过上下文学习执行汤普森采样；在多项全局优化基准上，采用非高斯先验的方法优于传统高斯过程流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汤普森采样适合在黑箱条件下优化未知函数，但连续全局优化中的应用长期受限于高斯过程，因为实际可计算后验分布的先验类别较少。高斯先验也难以表达某些函数特征，例如单峰性，因此需要兼具计算可行性与先验灵活性的替代方法。

Method: 提出先验拟合扩散变换器：先依照先验拟合网络范式进行预训练，再于推理阶段利用上下文执行汤普森采样。研究还构造了适用于一般黑箱函数的多种非高斯先验，并考察模型规模与预训练迭代次数之间的权衡及其缩放规律。

Result: 在一组全局优化基准测试中，使用非高斯先验的先验拟合扩散变换器，相较于基于高斯过程的传统优化流程取得了更好的表现。实验还显示，其性能随模型规模和预训练投入呈现类似其他模态变换器的缩放规律。

Conclusion: 扩散模型与先验拟合预训练相结合，为连续黑箱全局优化中的汤普森采样提供了超越高斯过程的可行途径；更灵活的非高斯先验有助于纳入特定函数结构，并带来基准性能提升。

Abstract: Thompson sampling is natural randomized strategy for optimizing unknown functions in a black-box manner. In continuous global optimization, Thompson sampling has largely been restricted to Gaussian process priors, as these are essentially the only class for which computing the posterior distribution has been tractable in practice. In this work, we introduce \emph{prior-fitted diffusion transformers}, which are diffusion models that perform Thompson sampling in-context at inference time, after being pre-trained using the prior-fitted network paradigm. To apply these models, we develop a set of practical non-Gaussian priors for general black-box functions---including those that have characteristics that cannot be achieved under Gaussianity, such as priors over unimodal functions. We also quantify the tradeoff between model size and the number of pre-training iterations, and show that these models follow scaling laws akin to those observed in transformers defined over other modalities. Across a set of global optimization benchmarks, we show that prior-fitted diffusion transformers with non-Gaussian priors can achieve improved performance compared to traditional pipelines built on Gaussian processes.

</details>


### [223] [Closing the Loop: Co-Evolving EM for Irregular Time Series Generation in Lifted Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14659)
*Idan Arbiv,Gal Fadlon,Omri Azencot*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种生成器与缺失值插补器协同迭代的蒙特卡洛期望最大化框架，并设计适用于提升表示的后验采样方法，在多种不规则时间序列生成任务上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常先对不完整序列进行一次性插补，再固定插补结果训练生成器。这种开环流程存在结构性缺陷：确定性插补会将含糊的缺失片段压缩为条件均值，随机插补结果则会随着生成器更新而过时。因此，关键问题并非单独改进插补器，而是让插补与生成过程持续迭代、相互适应。

Method: 提出协同演化的蒙特卡洛期望最大化框架。在每轮期望步骤中，根据当前生成器的后验分布对缺失值进行采样；在最大化步骤中，利用这些补全样本重新训练生成器，同时保留无条件生成器训练方式。针对扩散先验使用二维提升表示、而观测位于时间序列空间这一差异，进一步提出适用于提升表示的后验采样方法，在期望步骤中确保观测算子一致性、不确定性保留和流形一致性。

Result: 在十个数据集和四种破坏设定上的实验显示，该方法取得最先进表现。相较最强基线，判别得分改善百分之五十九、预测得分改善百分之十五、上下文分布距离改善百分之七十三、特征相关误差改善百分之七十一。消融实验还显示，相较单步基线，平均判别得分降低百分之七十三，并缩小了与干净数据预言机之间的大部分差距。

Conclusion: 研究表明，不规则时间序列生成中的核心改进在于闭合插补器与生成器之间的反馈循环，而非仅依赖一次性插补。通过协同迭代和针对提升表示的后验采样，所提方法能更有效地利用缺失数据并提升生成质量。

Abstract: Regular time-series generation from irregular time series is typically reduced to one-shot imputation: complete each partial sequence once, freeze the completions, and train a generator on the resulting surrogate dataset. We show that this open-loop strategy is structurally flawed. Point imputers collapse ambiguous missing regions to conditional averages, while stochastic imputers become stale as the generator evolves; the binding constraint is therefore iteration, not imputer design. We introduce a co-evolving Monte Carlo EM framework that closes the imputer-generator loop. Each E-step samples missing values from the posterior of the current generator, and each M-step retrains the generator on these completions, preserving unconditional generator training. Since our diffusion prior operates in a lifted 2D representation while observations live in time-series space, we further introduce Posterior Sampling for Lifted Representations (PSLR), which enforces operator-consistent conditioning, uncertainty preservation, and manifold consistency during the E-step. Across ten datasets and four corruption regimes, our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving over the strongest baselines by 59% in discriminative score, 15% in predictive score, 73% in Context-FID, and 71% in feature-correlation error. Ablations show that our approach reduces average discriminative score by 73% over single-step baselines, closing most of the remaining gap to the clean-data oracle. Code is available at https://github.com/azencot-group/PSLR.

</details>


### [224] [Trait Hitchhiking in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14683)
*Adil Soubki,Elizabeth S. Z. Tan,Miles Cranmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“特质搭便车”机制，说明强化学习后训练在优化目标特质时，也可能意外放大或抑制未被奖励直接约束的特质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已成为语言模型后训练的重要环节，尤其能提升具有可验证奖励任务的能力。但训练会改变奖励未明确针对的行为与内部属性，因此需要刻画这些非目标特质如何随训练变化。

Method: 研究者建立描述强化学习后训练中特质演化的形式体系，并由此推导出源自进化生物学的普赖斯方程。进一步提出数学模型，用“特质搭便车”描述训练过程中，随着不同生成结果的概率重新分配，非目标特质被连带放大或抑制的现象；随后在采用组相对策略优化训练的语言模型上检验该方程的预测。

Result: 实验显示，普赖斯方程给出的特质变化估计与模型采样得到的实际特质动态高度吻合，支持该表达式用于刻画训练中的特质变化。

Conclusion: 强化学习后训练不仅改变被奖励直接优化的能力，也可能通过概率重新分配悄然改变其他特质。特质搭便车为理解这一现象提供了数学框架，并提示应关注训练过程中非目标行为可能出现的意外放大或抑制。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is now a standard stage of language model post-training, and the compute devoted to it is growing rapidly. While it has been shown to improve capabilities, particularly for tasks with verifiable rewards, it can also change behaviours and internal properties that the reward does not explicitly target. We present a formalism to describe the evolution of "traits" during RL post-training and, in doing so, recover the Price equation from evolutionary biology. We use this to develop a mathematical model for what we call "trait hitchhiking": the amplification or suppression of non-targeted traits as training redistributes probability across completions. We evaluate this Price update expression on language models trained with GRPO and show that the Price estimates closely track the sampled trait dynamics. These results identify trait hitchhiking as a mechanism by which RL post-training can silently amplify undesirable traits.

</details>


### [225] [Beyond Row-Level Prediction: Evaluating and Learning Composition for Reusable Table Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14697)
*Ali Younes,Saeed Ghoorchian,Maximilian Schambach,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Johannes Höhne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出直接评估表级嵌入的基准，并发现表格基础模型的局部状态若采用合适的学习式组合规则，可显著提升表级表示质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格信息分散在模式、单元格、行和列中，检索、去重等任务需要将这些局部信号整合成单一的表级向量。然而，现有方法多通过行级预测或有监督下游任务评估，无法直接衡量表级嵌入质量，也未充分厘清性能不足究竟来自编码器还是组合方式。

Method: 作者提出表级嵌入基准，从局部视图变化下的一致性、不同标签粒度间的区分能力、对无害扰动的鲁棒性及效率四方面进行评估，并比较十二种方法。随后固定一个表格基础模型的编码器，仅学习组合单元格状态的规则；为此提出自监督的支持集条件分布匹配损失，使嵌入相似度分布与表内及跨表的分区重叠分布相匹配。

Result: 在合成与真实语料的比较中，轻量级模式指纹和文本指纹表现突出。两种表格基础模型虽擅长行级预测，但其单元格状态经均值、最大值或其他无参数规则汇总后，仍逊于表现最强的指纹方法。采用所提学习式组合规则后，所有十五种头部配置在合成数据上的相似度与局部视图重叠度之间的斯皮尔曼相关系数，从零点零四提高至零点四三至零点五零；三元组排序、聚类和检索也有所改善。在真实数据上提升幅度较小，但趋势稳定；父表检索任务进一步验证了这些发现。

Conclusion: 表级嵌入的质量不仅取决于局部编码器，也显著受局部状态如何组合及其训练目标影响。直接针对表级结构关系设计组合规则和评估基准，能够更准确地衡量并改善表格表示。

Abstract: Tables are compositional, with content spread across a schema, cell values, rows, and columns. Large-scale operations such as retrieval, overlap estimation, deduplication, and data curation need these local signals composed into one reusable embedding vector per table. Table representation methods, however, are mostly evaluated through row-level prediction or supervised downstream tasks, which do not measure table-level embedding quality directly. We introduce a benchmark that evaluates table-level embeddings through four desiderata: consistency under partial views, discriminability across label granularities, robustness to benign perturbations, and efficiency. Across twelve methods on synthetic and real corpora, lightweight schema- and text-based fingerprints are among the strongest. The sharpest contrast comes from two tabular foundation models, TabPFN and ConTextTab: although both are strong row-level predictors, their states, composed by mean, max, or three further parameter-free pooling rules, trail the strongest fingerprints. We therefore ask whether the gap lies in the encoder or in the compositional rule. Keeping ConTextTab frozen, we learn only the compositional rule over its cell states, using support-conditioned distribution matching (SCDM), a self-supervised loss we propose that matches the distribution of embedding similarities to the distribution of partition overlaps within and across tables. Across all fifteen head configurations, the learned rule raises the Spearman correlation between embedding similarity and partial-view overlap from 0.04 to 0.43-0.50 on synthetic data and improves triplet ranking, clustering, and retrieval, with smaller but systematic gains on real data. A downstream parent-table retrieval task confirms these findings. How local states are composed, and with what objective, substantially affects the quality of table-level embeddings.

</details>


### [226] [Code as Explanations: Executable and Verifiable LLM Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14704)
*Nhi Nguyen,Shauli Ravfogel,Rajesh Ranganath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出用可执行程序解释大语言模型在一组输入上的行为，并以程序复现模型输出的比例衡量解释一致性。程序解释更短、可自动验证，但在语言复杂任务上的效果有限；一致性反馈与代码执行有助于改进解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的思维链通常针对单个输入生成，难以在新输入上验证，也不易说明模型跨多种输入时的整体行为。论文希望找到一种能够概括多输入行为、并可通过执行自动检验的解释形式。

Method: 提出“代码解释”：生成一个程序，用它描述模型在一组输入上的输出行为。定义一致性指标，即程序能够复现模型输出的输入所占比例，并据此评估和筛选候选程序。研究在三种开源代码生成模型和八项任务上开展实验，另测试向模型提供一致性反馈和代码执行结果的效果。

Result: 在部分任务中，代码解释与模型行为高度一致，长度比思维链短约六至一百倍，并在二十四种设置中的九种设置里比思维链更贴合模型行为。但语言成分更复杂的任务上一致性显著下降，程序的表现不优于缺乏信息量的解释基线，且常依赖较长的硬编码模式。向模型提供一致性反馈及代码执行结果，能够提升多项任务上的解释一致性，但未消除复杂任务上的差距。

Conclusion: 可执行代码是一种新颖、简洁且可验证的大语言模型行为解释方式，在适用任务上具有潜力。自动验证反馈也可能改善解释质量；然而，对语言复杂行为的概括仍是主要挑战，后续方法需要减少对硬编码模式的依赖，并提升复杂任务上的一致性。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) increasingly make consequential decisions and solve problems alongside humans, we must understand how models arrive at their conclusions. Understanding how an LLM behaves requires observing it across multiple inputs, yet free-text explanations such as chain-of-thought (CoT) are generated per instance and cannot be easily verified on new inputs. We propose {code explanations to address the mismatch in how LLM explanations are generated versus how they are evaluated: a program explains LLM behavior across a dataset of inputs, and its execution enables automatic verification. We define Consistency metric that measures the fraction of inputs on which the program reproduces the LLM's outputs, and use it to evaluate and select candidate programs. Across three open-source code-capable LLMs and eight tasks, we find that LLMs can produce highly consistent code explanations in several settings. Code explanations are also 6$\times$ to 100$\times$ shorter than CoT, and better align with model behavior in 9 out of 24 settings. However, consistency is significantly lower on tasks with more complex linguistic components, where generated programs reproduce model outputs no more frequently than an uninformative explanation baseline, and these programs often rely on longer hard-coded patterns. In experiments with Claude Opus 5.5, providing the model with consistency feedback and code execution improves explanation consistency across tasks, but consistency remains significantly lower on more complex tasks. Our findings establish executable code as a novel, verifiable, and compact approach to explaining LLM behavior and highlight verification feedback as a promising direction for improving explanation consistency. The code for this paper is at https://github.com/rajesh-lab/code-as-explanations.

</details>


### [227] [UniPipe: Unified Inference Serving and Fine-Tuning for Deadline-Constrained DNN Surrogate Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14748)
*Jaiaid Mobin,Avinash Maurya,M. Mustafa Rafique,Bogdan Nicolae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种单一图形处理器上下文中的统一服务与微调系统，通过融合前向计算并调度批次组成，提升实时科学成像工作流的精度、吞吐量与时限可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学成像工作流需要在严格时限内处理高速传感器数据。预训练代理模型虽能显著加速计算，但输入分布会随实验变化，冻结模型会导致精度下降；与推理并行运行的独立微调上下文又会争抢图形处理器资源，增加推理延迟及其波动，造成时限违约。

Method: 提出统一流水线系统，在单一图形处理器上下文中联合执行推理与微调。系统将训练数据与推理数据合并进行一次前向计算，再仅对训练数据子集执行反向计算，以复用计算。它还利用现代图形处理器的单样本延迟随批次大小呈非线性变化这一特点，以及工作负载在各时间区间重复出现的特征，通过多项式时间调度算法离线确定批次组成。

Result: 在真实的相干衍射成像重建工作流中，传感器速率最高达到每秒五千个衍射图样。相较于仅使用预训练模型，重建均方误差最多降低百分之三十，推理均方误差最多降低百分之五十；相较于多上下文方案，时限违约率降低百分之十三至百分之二十二。延迟波动范围由平均延迟的百分之一百零六降至百分之五十七，总吞吐量也有所提高。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将推理与在线微调统一到单一图形处理器上下文，并针对延迟特征进行批次调度，可以缓解资源争用，在保持实时性与提升吞吐量的同时改善模型精度，适用于具有硬性处理时限的高速科学工作流。

Abstract: DNN surrogates are increasingly used to accelerate scientific workflows that must process high-rate sensor streams in near real time, exemplified by Ptychographic imaging at next-generation X-ray light sources. Such surrogates deliver 100-1000x higher throughput than iterative solvers, enabling faster steering of experiments. To preserve surrogate accuracy during an experiment, as the input distribution evolves, state-of-the-art deployments freeze the pretrained surrogate or run a separate CUDA context that fine-tunes concurrently with serving. However, multi-context concurrency causes significant contention on the GPU, inflating both the mean and the variance of inference latency, triggering deadline misses in workflows where sensor data has to be processed within a hard per-unit deadline. To address this challenge, we propose UniPipe, a single-context system that unifies inference serving and fine-tuning of a DNN surrogate on a single edge GPU. UniPipe is driven by three key insights: both serving and fine-tuning require a forward pass and can be fused into one; the per-sample latency of modern GPUs is non-linear in batch size; and the workload is repetitive across intervals, enabling offline scheduling. Specifically, UniPipe fuses training and inference data in a single forward pass with a selective backward pass on the training subset, and uses a polynomial-time scheduler that exploits the latency curve to compute the batch composition. We evaluate UniPipe on a real-world Ptychographic reconstruction workflow with sensor rates up to 5000 patterns/sec.; UniPipe lowers reconstruction MSE by up to 30% and inference MSE by up to 50% over a pretrained baseline, and reduces the deadline-miss rate by 13%-22% over a multi-context baseline. UniPipe's unified deployment reduces the latency spread from 106% to 57% of the mean, and increases total throughput compared to multi-context baseline.

</details>


### [228] [From Nodes to Pixels: Topological and Structural Two-View Graph Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14750)
*Md Joshem Uddin,Soham Changani,Sai Karthik Navuluru,Cuneyt Gurcan Akcora,Baris Coskunuzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种将不同图结构转为固定尺寸图像表示的方法，并以互补视图和跨视图对比学习提升图分类与分子预测表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图数据结构不规则，规模和密度各异，且节点编号可有多种等价方式，因此难以像图像切块或文本词元那样形成跨任务、跨模型通用的输入接口。论文旨在提供一种固定预算、稳定且可复用的图表示形式。

Method: 该方法把每个图转换为两种互补的固定尺寸视图：拓扑网格将图的内在双过滤结构编码为紧凑的多重持久性曲面网格；结构网格则依据内在节点分数对节点分组，并汇总组间边密度。两种表示均对节点置换不敏感，并具有由分辨率控制的稳定性保证。随后使用轻量二维编码器处理这些张量，并通过有监督的跨视图对比目标对齐两种视图。

Result: 在图分类和分子预测基准上，该方法在所比较的方法中取得较强的平均表现，优于单视图方案及常规融合方案；此外，它无需改变整体架构即可扩展到包含丰富属性的分子图。

Conclusion: 固定预算的图像化表示有望成为图表示学习实用且可复用的输入接口。通过结合稳定的拓扑信息与高带宽结构信息，该框架兼顾标准化输入和表达能力；不过，摘要未提供具体数据集、数值提升或计算成本等细节。

Abstract: Graph learning still lacks a broadly reusable input interface comparable to patches in vision or tokens in language. Unlike images or text, graphs are irregular, vary in size and density, and are invariant under many equivalent node labelings, making it difficult to standardize inputs across tasks and architectures. We introduce G2Image, a fixed-budget graph imaging framework that converts each graph into two complementary image-like views. TopoGrid provides a stable topological view by encoding an intrinsic bifiltration as a compact multipersistence surface grid, while GraphGrid provides a higher-bandwidth structural view by aggregating edge densities between groups defined by intrinsic node scores. Both views are permutation-invariant and come with resolution-controlled stability guarantees. Because the resulting representations are fixed-size tensors, they can be processed by lightweight 2D encoders and aligned through a supervised cross-view contrastive objective. Across graph classification and molecular prediction benchmarks, G2Image achieves strong average performance among the compared methods, improves over single-view and standard fusion variants, and extends to attribute-rich molecular graphs without changing the overall architecture. These results suggest that fixed-budget graph images offer a practical and reusable interface for graph representation learning.

</details>


### [229] [Graph Label Alignment: A Diagnostic Atlas for Graph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14760)
*Neelam Akula,Varun Shiralkar,Murat Kantarcioglu,Baris Coskunuzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种轻量诊断方法，衡量图分类标签能否仅凭结构摘要或节点特征汇总被预测，并据此分析基准难度；结果显示，边际信息不足时，模型必须保留结构与特征之间的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同图分类基准的难度差异很大：有些任务简单的池化方法即可解决，有些则对图神经网络和图变换器仍具挑战。论文关注这些差异是否源于真正的结构—特征联合推理，还是标签信号已存在于受限的结构或特征视图中。

Method: 提出图标签对齐诊断，通过两个无需训练图编码器的监督探针衡量标签可及性：结构标签对齐利用对节点置换不敏感的拓扑与谱摘要；特征标签对齐则移除边后汇总节点特征。研究在真实图分类基准及多种池化骨干模型上分析两类对齐度与测试准确率的关系，并设计交互主导的压力测试，包括异配性自我中心子图，以及类别间结构边际和特征边际相同、但角色与特征耦合不同的合成双生任务。

Result: 结构与特征标签对齐构成的平面能够较好地组织测试准确率。两类对齐度都低的数据集形成稳定的困难区域，常规池化流程在其中表现吃力。压力测试进一步表明，当类别差异不体现在结构或特征的单独边际分布中时，模型需要保留结构与特征的对应关系才能识别标签。

Conclusion: 基准上的高准确率在很大程度上可能来自结构或特征边际中已有的标签信号，不一定意味着模型学会了复杂的联合推理。该诊断可用于区分容易基准与真正考验结构—特征交互的任务；低对齐度则提示，仅依赖常规池化表示可能不足。

Abstract: Graph classification benchmarks vary widely in difficulty: some are solved by simple pooled baselines, while others remain challenging for GNNs and graph transformers. We take a data-centric view and ask whether benchmark success is driven by genuine structure--feature reasoning or by label signal already accessible from restricted marginal views. We introduce graph label alignment, a lightweight diagnostic that measures label accessibility through two supervised probes computed without training a graph encoder: structural label alignment (SLA), based on permutation-invariant topological and spectral summaries, and feature label alignment (FLA), based on pooled node features with edges removed. Across real graph classification benchmarks and multiple pooled backbones, the resulting SLA--FLA plane strongly organizes test accuracy, with low-alignment datasets forming a consistent regime where standard pooled pipelines struggle. To probe what lies beyond marginal accessibility, we introduce controlled interaction-dominated stress tests, including heterophilic ego-graphs and synthetic Twins tasks whose structural and feature marginals are matched across classes but whose role--feature couplings differ. These results show that high alignment explains much of easy benchmark performance, while low alignment reveals regimes where success requires preserving structure--feature correspondence beyond standard pooled representations.

</details>


### [230] [SPIRE: Task-agnostic embodied continual learning through autonomous situation inference and causal motor-primitive reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14840)
*Yiyang Jia,Mingyang Lyu,Gongce Cao,Feifei Zhao,Yinqian Sun,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出一种能在线判断情境、保留旧技能并将其用于新任务的脉冲循环控制系统；实验显示其能持续控制，并显著加快新技能学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身持续学习要求控制器在没有任务标签或明确任务边界时识别情境，同时维持既有行为并复用运动技能。关键难点在于判断何时恢复旧行为，以及哪些旧技能适合支持当前的新学习。

Method: 提出情境引导的运动原语整合方法。系统依据动作及其结果推断潜在情境，并在线学习与情境对应的运动控制器；成熟控制器受到保护，作为技能来源，与目标情境的残差控制相结合，共同驱动脉冲循环运动单元。

Result: 在坐标控制、三关节控制和隐藏外力控制任务中，系统维持了控制能力，能够自主推断情境并保留旧行为。对于未见过的技能组合，达到学习标准所需的平均交互次数较新控制器减少约百分之四十。回退兼容的旧控制器会损害所有对应干预中的早期学习，支持技能复用具有因果作用；与七种持续学习及模块化控制基线比较时，系统取得了最强合适无任务标签基线平均既有学习优势的二点四倍。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，在连续具身控制中，控制器能够自主识别情境、恢复既有行为，并因果性地复用旧运动技能来促进新学习。

Abstract: Embodied continual learning requires controllers to infer changing situations, retain prior behavior and reuse motor skills without task labels or known boundaries. The challenge is deciding when to recover earlier behavior and which skills can support new learning. We introduce Situation-guided Primitive Integration for Recurrent Embodiment (SPIRE), a recurrent-spiking system that infers latent contexts from action-outcome evidence and learns context-specific motor actors online. Mature actors become protected sources that combine with a target-context residual to drive recurrent-spiking motor populations. Across coordinate, three-joint and hidden-force control, SPIRE maintained control, autonomously inferred situations, retained prior behavior and accelerated acquisition of an unseen source composition, reducing mean interactions to criterion by approximately 40% relative to matched fresh controllers. Rewinding compatible source actors impaired early acquisition in every matched intervention, demonstrating causal reuse. Against seven recurrent continual-learning and modular-control comparators, SPIRE achieved 2.4 times the mean prior-learning advantage of the strongest eligible task-free alternative selected for each paired stream. These results demonstrate autonomous recovery and causal reuse of motor skills for new learning in continuous embodied control.

</details>


### [231] [Generalize Then Personalize: Wearable-Based Heart Rate Forecasting Under Scarce Ruck-March Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14849)
*Muntaqim Ahmed Raju,Ruizhe Ma,John W. Ramsay,David J. Cornell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究比较了三种训练策略在负重行军心率预测中的表现；总体上，群体数据预训练后再针对个人微调，在数据稀缺条件下最具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提前预测负重行军中的心率变化，有助于生理监测和工作负荷管理。但相关数据难以大规模采集，模型既要适用于不同参与者，又要能用少量个人数据适应个体，因此需要评估适合小样本场景的训练策略。

Method: 使用八名美国陆军现役医护兵的十三次行军记录，比较三种策略：仅用群体数据训练、从头开始进行个人训练，以及先用群体数据预训练再针对个人微调。模型包括长短期记忆网络、带注意力机制的长短期记忆网络和卷积—长短期记忆网络，并与持续性基线比较。预测时距为三十秒和六十秒；评估指标包括平均绝对误差、均方根误差和预测终点平均绝对误差，并进行参与者层面的配对分析及霍尔姆多重比较校正。

Result: 群体预训练后再微调，在所有神经网络架构和预测时距下，平均绝对误差、均方根误差及终点误差的整体表现均优于另外两种训练策略。相较于从头进行个人训练，该策略将轨迹平均绝对误差降低了每分钟心跳二点二二至四点三零次，将终点误差降低了一点二四至二点八八次；六项轨迹误差比较经校正后均达到显著性，六项终点误差比较中有四项显著。三十秒预测时，持续性基线的平均绝对误差最低；卷积—长短期记忆网络微调模型的均方根误差最低，长短期记忆网络微调模型的终点误差最低。六十秒预测时，带注意力机制的长短期记忆网络微调模型在平均绝对误差和均方根误差上最低，卷积—长短期记忆网络微调模型的终点误差最低。

Conclusion: 在负重行军心率预测这一数据稀缺场景中，先利用群体数据学习、再用有限个人数据微调，是一种有实用潜力的个体化建模策略。不同指标和预测时距对应的最佳模型并不相同，因此模型选择应结合具体预测目标；研究样本规模有限，结论仍需在更多参与者和场景中验证。

Abstract: Heart rate (HR) forecasting may provide advance information about near-future cardiovascular response, potentially supporting physiological monitoring and workload-management decisions during ruck marching. However, robust model development is constrained by the difficulty of collecting large-scale ruck-march datasets, creating a scarce-data problem in which models must generalize across participants while adapting to individuals from limited personal data. To address this problem, we benchmark population-only training (POT), personalized-from-scratch training (PFST), and population-pretrained fine-tuning (PPFT) for HR forecasting during loaded ruck marching using data from eight active-duty U.S. Army medics across thirteen sessions. LSTM, Attention-LSTM, and CNN-LSTM backbones were evaluated against a persistence baseline at 30 s and 60 s horizons. Across all neural architectures and horizons, PPFT yielded lower mean MAE, RMSE, and endpoint MAE than both POT and PFST. Participant-level paired analysis after averaging across three training seeds showed that, relative to PFST, PPFT reduced trajectory MAE by 2.22-4.30 bpm and endpoint MAE by 1.24-2.88 bpm. All six trajectory-MAE comparisons remained significant after Holm adjustment ($p_{adj}$ = 0.0469), while four of six endpoint-MAE comparisons remained significant after adjustment. At 30 s, persistence achieved the lowest MAE (3.555 $\pm$ 1.274 bpm), while CNN-LSTM PPFT achieved the lowest RMSE (4.669 $\pm$ 1.916 bpm); LSTM PPFT achieved the lowest endpoint MAE (5.093 $\pm$ 2.365 bpm). At 60 s, Attention-LSTM PPFT achieved the lowest MAE (5.573 $\pm$ 1.939 bpm) and RMSE (7.134 $\pm$ 2.260 bpm), while CNN-LSTM PPFT achieved the lowest endpoint MAE (7.553 $\pm$ 2.357 bpm). These results suggest that population pretraining followed by participant-specific fine-tuning may provide a practical strategy for scarce-data wearable HR forecasting.

</details>


### [232] [FOE-Dreamer: Deployment-Efficient Learning of Cyber Defense Policies in Operational Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14880)
*Yinuo Du,Christopher Kiekintveld*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向真实网络在线训练的模型式强化学习防御方法，通过拆分防御者可控因素、正常用户活动和攻击者特征，提高训练效率；在云端测试床上，其表现优于两种强化学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络防御策略通常在模拟器中训练，但模拟环境难以准确反映认证延迟、动作执行失败、服务依赖及用户流量等现实因素，导致策略迁移到真实网络后性能下降。直接在真实网络训练虽可避免模拟器与现实的差距，却因交互耗时、成本高而受限，因此需要提升样本效率。

Method: 提出一种因子化、对手增强的模型式强化学习框架，使防御策略无需经过模拟器训练阶段，直接在云端网络中在线学习。该方法将潜在状态拆分为三个部分：由防御动作控制的因素、由正常用户活动驱动的外生因素，以及仅依据防御者可观察信号推断的攻击者表征。作者指出，这种拆分可降低利用学习到的世界模型进行想象规划时策略梯度的方差。

Result: 在包含八台主机的云端测试床上，研究使用用户活动模拟框架生成正常流量，并测试两种脚本化攻击者。该方法在单块图形处理器、三天训练预算内完成训练；在计算资源匹配的比较中，其每回合损失约为两种基线算法的一半。消融结果表明，状态因子化和攻击者模型均对性能提升有所贡献，学得的潜在表征也符合预期的因素划分。

Conclusion: 研究展示了在真实运行网络中直接训练模型式强化学习防御策略的可行性，并表明对不同状态变化来源进行显式建模，有助于提升样本效率和防御表现。其结论目前基于规模有限的测试床及两种脚本化攻击者，能否推广到更复杂的网络和自适应攻击仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Autonomous cyber defense is widely studied with deep reinforcement learning, but most work trains policies inside simulators because learning requires many trial-and-error interactions. Simulator-based training carries a fidelity gap: trained policies face authentication delays, partial action failures, service dependencies, and user traffic patterns that the simulator did not represent, and degrade on transfer. Training directly in the network being defended removes the gap but makes every interaction slow and costly, so sample efficiency becomes the binding constraint. Model-based reinforcement learning has reduced sample requirements on games and physical robots by orders of magnitude by planning inside a learned world model, but it has not been applied to defenders trained in operational networks. We present FOE-Dreamer (Factored Opponent-Enhanced Dreamer), a model-based reinforcement learning framework that trains a defender online and directly in a network on cloud infrastructure, without a simulator stage. FOE-Dreamer separates three sources of variation in the latent state: a controllable component driven by defender actions, an exogenous component driven by benign user activity, and an opponent embedding inferred from defender-observable signals alone. Separating the components reduces the variance in policy gradients during planning in imagination. Against two scripted attacker profiles with user activity emulated by the GHOSTS framework on an 8-host OpenStack testbed, FOE-Dreamer trains within a 3-day budget on a single GPU and achieves about 2$\times$ better episode loss than Rainbow and IQN baselines under matched compute. Both the factoring and the opponent model contribute to the gain, and the learned latents are consistent with the intended separation.

</details>


### [233] [Machine Learning Moment Closure for the Boltzmann Transport Equation: Enforcing Hyperbolicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14894)
*Bongseok Kim,Jiahao Zhang,Guang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多维线性玻尔兹曼方程的学习型矩闭合方法，通过构造共享对称正定对称化矩阵，保证所有空间方向上的矩系统均为双曲型；二维实验中，十矩的三阶系统稳定且精度优于包含更多矩的经典截断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻尔兹曼方程需解析速度变量，计算代价较高；矩方法虽能降低成本，却依赖对未解析高阶矩的闭合。数据驱动闭合有望提升精度，但若缺乏结构约束，可能产生复特征速度并导致模拟不稳定。多维情形还要求系统在所有空间方向上同时保持双曲性，而既有具双曲性保证的学习型闭合仅适用于一维。

Method: 为多维线性玻尔兹曼方程的厄米特矩系统构造数据驱动闭合。神经网络学习一个适用于所有空间方向的对称正定对称化矩阵，并将其限制在能够保留下阶已知输运方程的子空间内；未定行通过对称补全确定。由此，对任意网络输出及任意截断阶数，各方向的雅可比矩阵均具有实特征值和完备特征向量。训练数据仅包含动理学参考解中未解析矩的空间导数，不在训练环节嵌入求解器；时间演化采用部分守恒间断伽辽金方法。

Result: 在二维过渡区和散射主导区实验中，单一的三阶、十矩学习型系统在所有测试情形下均保持稳定。按时间平均，其精度在每项实验中都优于经典七阶和十五阶截断，后两者分别包含三十六矩和一百三十六矩；与同为十矩的经典三阶截断相比，该方法的密度误差约小两个数量级。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了通过结构化学习实现多维矩闭合双曲性保证的可行方案。其结果表明，兼具稳定性与精度的低阶学习型系统，可能以远少于传统高阶截断的矩数量有效模拟复杂输运过程。

Abstract: The Boltzmann transport equation describes particle transport across collisional regimes, but resolving its velocity dependence is expensive. Moment methods reduce this cost by evolving finitely many velocity moments, requiring a closure for unresolved higher-order moments. Data-driven closures can represent these moments accurately, yet without structural constraints they may produce complex characteristic speeds and unstable simulations. Learned Boltzmann closures with guaranteed hyperbolicity have so far been formulated in one spatial dimension; in multiple dimensions, hyperbolicity must hold in every direction simultaneously. We develop, to the best of our knowledge, the first data-driven closure for multidimensional Hermite moment systems of the linear Boltzmann equation that is hyperbolic by construction. The network learns a symmetric positive-definite symmetrizer shared by all spatial directions and restricted to a subspace that preserves the known lower-order transport equations; the unresolved rows follow from a symmetric completion. Every directional Jacobian thus has real eigenvalues and a complete set of eigenvectors for every network output and at any truncation order. The closure is trained only on spatial derivatives of unresolved moments from kinetic reference solutions, with no solver in the training loop, and evolved by a partially conservative discontinuous Galerkin method. In two-dimensional experiments in the transition and scattering-dominated regimes, a single learned third-order system with ten moments remains stable in all cases. Averaged over time, it is more accurate than the classical P7 and P15 truncations with 36 and 136 moments in every experiment, and its density error is about two orders of magnitude smaller than that of the P3 truncation with the same moments.

</details>


### [234] [Wayfinder: Adaptive Resource Routing from Agent Citations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16053)
*Miguel Romero,George Karypis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种能根据后续智能体引用的文档持续更新资源判断的自适应路由方法，并构建三个测试环境进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体需要从聊天、问题跟踪系统、电子邮件和知识库等多种资源中搜集信息，但通常不知道相关内容存放在哪里，因而会在连续交互中重复探索。依赖资源静态描述的现有路由方法难以区分内容相似或重叠的资源，也无法利用后续交互进行改进。

Method: 论文提出自适应路由器“寻路器”：将下游智能体引用的文档作为反馈，据此更新内部资源表示，并在后续交互中优先检索更可能包含相关信息的资源。此外，论文在离线强化学习阶段引入资源增强，通过匿名化并置换资源身份，减少模型对训练环境中固定主题—资源对应关系的过拟合。研究还基于既有数据集构建了三个受控测试环境，并采用统一的闭环评估协议与多种基线方法比较。

Result: 实验表明，当不同资源包含语义重叠、静态描述难以区分的内容时，寻路器的路由效果优于监督学习、工具检索和上下文多臂老虎机等基线；当资源来自彼此差异明显的数据语料时，基于描述的工具检索已足够有效。消融结果还显示，资源增强在训练环境较少时帮助最大。

Conclusion: 利用智能体实际引用的文档作为反馈，可以让资源路由器逐步适应特定环境，并改善对内容相似资源的选择。论文同时提供了配套测试环境，为研究真实查询与资源访问模式相结合的路由问题提供了评估基础。

Abstract: To act effectively, agents must gather contextual information across resources such as chats, issue trackers, email and knowledge bases, to answer questions like "what was decided in the last project meeting?" or "where is the FAQ for our internal tooling?" Where this information lives in a user's environment is unknown to the agent, so it re-explores the same resources across consecutive interactions. Common routing approaches represent each resource as a tool and rely on high-level descriptions to generalize to unseen environments. Such descriptions often fail to separate similar or overlapping resources and cannot improve from the agent's subsequent interactions. We introduce Wayfinder, an adaptive router that uses the documents cited by the downstream agent as feedback about where relevant information resides. These citations update Wayfinder's internal resource representations, which subsequent interactions use to prioritize the resources likely to contain relevant information. We also introduce resource augmentation during offline reinforcement learning, which anonymizes and permutes resource identities to discourage training-time overfitting of which resource holds which topic; our ablations suggest it helps most when few training environments are available. Because no openly available professional environments pair realistic queries with resource-access patterns, we construct and release three controlled testbeds derived from existing datasets: NFCorpus, FEVER and Tech Startup. Compared with supervised, tool-retrieval and contextual bandit baselines under a shared closed-loop protocol, Wayfinder gives the best routing when resources contain semantically overlapping content that static descriptions cannot distinguish, while description-based tool retrieval suffices when resources are drawn from distinct corpora.

</details>


### [235] [Social Engineering Attack Detection in Corporate Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14897)
*Hanzla Hamid,Pappu Jha,Nick Rahimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究利用真实邮件数据构建钓鱼邮件识别基线，并检验紧迫感、信任、贪婪等心理操纵线索的检测价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会工程攻击日益复杂，邮件、聊天和电话中的欺诈信息难以与真实沟通区分，可能导致机密信息泄露，并使攻击从基层员工扩展至管理层。仅依赖表层词汇特征，可能无法充分捕捉钓鱼信息中的操纵意图。

Method: 使用真实世界邮件数据构建机器学习基线，以词频—逆文档频率特征训练邮件分类模型，区分钓鱼邮件与安全邮件。进一步从合并后的邮件数据中提取可解释的词典型心理操纵指标，包括紧迫感、信任和贪婪等说服线索；单独评估这些指标所包含的检测信号，并测试其向未见邮件来源迁移的能力。

Result: 词频—逆文档频率模型的分类准确率超过百分之九十。仅使用心理操纵特征时，在留出测试集上的受试者工作特征曲线下面积为零点七四。研究还评估了这些特征在未见邮件来源上的迁移表现，但摘要未提供具体迁移结果。

Conclusion: 心理操纵线索能够为钓鱼邮件检测提供具有一定区分能力、且较易解释的信号，但其单独表现低于词频—逆文档频率基线所报告的准确率。摘要支持将这类特征作为补充检测依据；其跨来源泛化效果仍需结合具体实验结果判断。

Abstract: Social Engineering attacks continue to pose a significant risk to the corporate world with a growing level of sophistication. It is difficult to distinguish between authentic and phishing communication via email, chat, and phone. They pose a risk of exposing trade secrets and confidential communications and can create a chain of attack from lower-level employees up to executives. Therefore, we study this growing threat using real-world datasets to build baseline machine-learning models. Our TF-IDF models classify emails as phishing or safe, achieving accuracy above 90%. We build on this by exploring whether psychological manipulation and persuasion indicators can be measured to play a significant role in detecting phishing. Instead of relying solely on surface-level lexical features, we derive interpretable lexicon-based indicators of urgency, trust, greed, and other persuasion cues from the combined email dataset. By evaluating these manipulation-oriented features on their own, we measure how much signal they carry (ROC-AUC of 0.74 on a held-out test split) and test whether they transfer to held-out email sources.

</details>


### [236] [Epigraph Form Reinforcement Learning-based Safe Trajectory Tracking Control for Connected and Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14903)
*Xiaoke Yang,Jin Chen,Yafei Wang,Qi Zhang,Ming Li,Haiwen Wu,Chengliang Yin,Yong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向联网自动驾驶车辆安全轨迹跟踪的强化学习框架，通过改进优化形式与梯度调节提升跟踪精度、训练稳定性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高度动态环境中，联网自动驾驶车辆需要准确跟踪参考轨迹并避免碰撞。传统非学习方法常因采用简化的车辆及交互模型而牺牲跟踪精度；常用的拉格朗日强化学习方法则可能因乘子增大导致策略梯度幅值放大，进而训练不稳定。

Method: 研究提出安全轨迹跟踪控制上图形式强化学习框架，将问题建模为上图形式的极小极大优化问题。该框架利用与上图变量相关的递减标量调整策略梯度下降方向，而不增加梯度幅值，以改善训练稳定性；同时设计计算高效的射线投射方法，用于计算多面体缩放因子并量化安全性。

Result: 实验及车辆在环测试表明，该方法相较于所考察的非学习基线具有更高的轨迹跟踪精度；相较于常用的拉格朗日强化学习方法，训练过程更加稳定，并能在联网环境中满足安全要求。

Conclusion: 该研究通过上图形式优化和梯度方向调节，缓解了安全轨迹跟踪中精度与训练稳定性方面的局限，并结合高效安全量化方法验证了其应用潜力。

Abstract: Safe trajectory tracking (STT) aims to enable autonomous vehicles to follow a reference trajectory accurately while avoiding collisions. This problem is particularly challenging for Connected and Autonomous Vehicles (CAVs) in highly dynamic environments. Existing non-learning-based STT methods often use simplified vehicle and interaction models to meet real-time requirements, which may result in suboptimal tracking accuracy. Although Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) can help address this limitation, widely used Lagrangian DRL methods often suffer from unstable training because increasing Lagrange multipliers amplify policy-gradient magnitudes. To address these issues, we propose a novel reinforcement learning framework for STT, called STT Control Epigraph Form RL (STTC-EFRL). In this framework, the STT problem is formulated as an epigraph-form min-max optimization problem. Based on this formulation, we develop a learning mechanism that uses a decreasing scalar related to the epigraph to adjust the policy gradient descent direction without increasing the gradient magnitude, thereby improving training stability. We also develop a computationally efficient ray-casting method to compute a polyhedron scaling factor for safety quantification. Experimental results, including Vehicle-in-the-Loop tests, show that STTC-EFRL achieves higher tracking accuracy than the considered non-learning-based baseline and more stable training than widely used Lagrangian DRL methods, while satisfying safety requirements in connected environments.

</details>


### [237] [Rethinking "RL Generalizes, SFT Memorizes": The Role of SFT Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14912)
*Yunlong Hou,Fengzhuo Zhang,Yuan Cheng,Jiachun Pan,Xingyao Li,Zhuoran Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文重新审视监督微调与强化学习在推理泛化上的差异，指出经过合理的数据来源与规模设计，监督微调不仅能达到强化学习的泛化水平，还可能表现更好；训练数据中的少数解题方法也有助于泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习因展现出较强的推理能力和泛化能力，常被认为优于容易记忆训练样本、迁移能力有限的监督微调。论文希望检验这种差异是否源于训练方式本身，还是受到监督微调数据的来源、规模及方法覆盖范围影响，并进一步从理论上分析两种训练方式的训练动态。

Method: 论文围绕常用模型系列开展实证研究：比较不同来源的监督微调数据；控制正确推理轨迹数量及总生成预算，考察数据规模对泛化的影响；利用大语言模型标注训练轨迹中的解题方法，分析数据集规模与少数方法覆盖及泛化之间的关系；最后，通过分析浅层变换器在监督微调和强化学习下的训练动态，为实证结果提供理论解释。

Result: 数据来源至关重要，精心混合的数据集明显优于完全由更大模型生成的数据。若监督微调与强化学习拥有相同数量的正确推理轨迹，监督微调的泛化能力会显著提升；若进一步匹配总生成预算，监督微调可达到与强化学习相当的泛化水平。结合数据来源与规模的改进后，监督微调的泛化表现还能超过强化学习。较大数据集覆盖更多少见解题方法，而这些方法能提供可迁移的推理信号。

Conclusion: 论文表明，监督微调与强化学习的泛化差异并非不可改变。通过优化数据来源、扩大有效训练规模并覆盖多样化解题方法，监督微调可以达到甚至超过强化学习的泛化表现；少见解题方法的覆盖可能是其中的重要机制。理论分析进一步支持了这些经验发现。

Abstract: Large language models trained with Reinforcement Learning (RL) with verifiable rewards exhibit strong reasoning ability and broad generalization, whereas models trained with Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) are often viewed as more prone to memorization and limited transfer. This paper rethinks this distinction through the lens of SFT training data on widely used Qwen2.5 and Qwen3 series models. First, we study the role of data source and show that it is critical: a carefully mixed SFT dataset substantially outperforms data generated solely by a larger model. Second, we study the role of data scale and show that matching the number of correct rollouts between SFT and RL greatly improves SFT generalization, while matching the total rollout budget enables SFT to generalize as well as RL. Combining these two factors further enables SFT to generalize even better than RL.
