On two agentic tool-use benchmarks, AppWorld and AutomationBench, JudgeMix demonstrates consistent superior performance to existing methods, with improvements up to 10 pass@1 points.

</details>


### [305] [SAME: Semantic Watermark Embedding for Open-Source Large Pretrained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15771)
*Xinyao Song,Jialiang Shen,Hui Kang,Huajie Chen,Ruiqi Liu,Jiyang Zheng,Huaxi Huang,Zhenliang Lu,Chris Russell,Runqi Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于语义私有词汇的水印方法，可在不同下游任务及后续微调中保持水印有效，用于验证模型所有权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模预训练模型水印通常依赖针对特定任务设计的触发词—响应模式，因此跨任务迁移能力有限，也容易在正常微调后失效。论文旨在提升水印的适应性、持久性和知识产权保护能力。

Method: 为模型建立固定的私有词汇，并为每个原生词元分配一个私有嵌入对应项；在模型表示空间中对齐两套词汇的语义表达能力。由此，私有词元可以灵活组合成具有语义含义的触发内容，诱导模型产生相应响应。水印与模型原生语义能力相结合，使其能够适应不同任务，并在下游微调中延续。

Result: 摘要称，大规模实验表明，该方法在不同规模和架构的模型上均能提供可靠且稳健的水印，并支持有效的模型所有权验证；但摘要未给出具体实验数据或对比幅度。

Conclusion: 该方法通过将水印建立在模型的语义表示之上，减少了对固定任务专属触发模式的依赖，有望改善水印的跨任务适用性及微调后的持久性，为开放模型的知识产权保护提供一种灵活方案。

Abstract: Open-source large pretrained models (LPMs) have become increasingly valuable assets for model development and downstream applications, but their accessibility also facilitates unauthorised copying and modification. To protect their intellectual property (IP), existing approaches typically embed tailored trigger-response patterns as watermarks for ownership verification. However, such trigger designs need to be tailored to task-specific data that constrain their transferability across downstream tasks, and are not durable under subsequent benign fine-tuning. To address these limitations, we propose SemAntic waterMark Embedding (SAME), which introduces a semantically meaningful private vocabulary that enables flexible composition of adaptive and persistent watermarks. Specifically, SAME introduces a fixed private vocabulary that assigns each native token a private embedding counterpart, while aligning the semantic expressiveness of the two vocabularies within the model's representation space. This alignment allows private tokens to be flexibly composed into semantic triggers that elicit meaningful responses, enabling adaptive watermarks beyond predefined trigger-response pairs. By grounding the private vocabulary in the model's native semantics, SAME couples the watermark with its evolving semantic capabilities, allowing this connection to be preserved throughout downstream fine-tuning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SAME provides reliable and robust watermarks across LPMs of varying scales and architectures, enabling effective ownership verification for IP protection.

</details>


### [306] [Does Deep Knowledge Tracing Learn Semantic Relations Between Knowledge Components?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16323)
*Kia Karbasi,Mahdi Saiedi,Kevin Hong,Gregory Pottie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，深度知识追踪模型学到的知识点关系与文本语义关系仅有较弱且不稳定的一致性；引入文本语义信息也未能稳定提升模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度知识追踪通过共享潜在状态处理学习者的交互序列，因此一个知识点上的作答可能影响其他知识点的预测。研究旨在检验模型学到的知识点关联是否具有语义基础，并考察文本语义信息能否改善预测。

Method: 研究采用九种流程，根据知识点文本构建语义表示。首先比较模型推导的知识点关系图与文本表示之间的成对余弦相似度所形成的语义结构；其次将预训练文本语义表示引入深度知识追踪模型，并与标准模型比较。此外，研究在逐步缩减的数据规模下进行实验，并考察知识点监督微调文本编码器的效果。

Result: 在所评估的数据集、文本嵌入模型和构建流程中，模型推导的关系结构与语义结构仅呈现较弱且不一致的对齐。引入预训练文本表示后，模型表现并未稳定优于标准基线。在一个二〇一七年作业辅导数据集的缩减数据实验中，语义初始化在九种递增样本规模中的八种取得更高的测试受试者工作特征曲线下面积；知识点监督微调仅在部分匹配比较中带来小幅提升，且其跨运行稳定性尚未得到证实。

Conclusion: 研究结果不支持深度知识追踪学到的知识点关系主要由语义决定。文本语义结构无法有力复现模型推导的关系，模型关联还可能受到行为、时间、结构或心理测量因素影响。语义信息在特定数据条件下可能有帮助，但目前观察到的效果有限且不稳定。

Abstract: Deep Knowledge Tracing (DKT) is a knowledge tracing (KT) model that updates a shared latent state from sequences of interactions, each pairing a knowledge component (KC) with a response. An interaction with one KC can therefore affect predictions for other KCs. We ask: Are the KC relations learned by DKT semantic? We construct text-derived semantic KC representations using nine pipelines to test whether the conclusions hold across alternative ways of constructing those representations.
  The first test compares DKT-derived KC relation graphs with semantic relation structures induced by pairwise cosine similarities among the KC representations. Across the evaluated datasets, embedding models, and pipelines, the two structures align only weakly and inconsistently. The second test introduces semantic information into DKT; the pretrained text-derived representations do not consistently outperform the standard DKT baseline.
  In the reduced-data experiments, semantic initialization yields a higher test area under the ROC curve (AUC) than the baseline at eight of nine nested sample sizes for ASSISTments 2017. KC-supervised fine-tuned BERT produces small positive AUC differences in some matched comparisons, but the study does not establish their stability across runs. These effects remain limited and inconsistent. The findings suggest that behavioral, temporal, structural, or psychometric signals may also shape DKT's learned relations. DKT-derived relations are not primarily semantic: the tested text-derived semantic structures do not strongly recover the DKT-derived relation structure.

</details>


### [307] [From Link Prediction to Linear Community Detection: A Finite-Sample Guarantee for Graph Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15778)
*Ying Chen,Zhangyang Wang,Yixuan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文在有限样本随机块模型中证明：图神经网络的链路预测效果越好，学得的节点嵌入越适合线性社区分类，并给出相应理论界与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链路预测常被用作图神经网络的预训练目标，但预测边的能力为何能转化为节点嵌入的分类能力，过去缺少定量理论解释。论文旨在刻画链路预测风险与社区分类误差之间的关系。

Method: 在有限样本随机块模型下，研究有界图神经网络编码器与对称双线性解码器。证明条件性归约：总体留出链路预测超额风险较小时，基于嵌入的传导式线性分类误差也较小。误差界由优化残差、架构近似残差和统计误差组成；在有界稠密情形下，统计项为约 O(√(d/n))（忽略对数因子）。此外，构造一种少标签分类器：在半正定二次型度量下进行最近质心分类，并将其标签误差与链路预测质量联系起来。论文还针对采用谱位置编码和双线性解码器的图同构网络，给出约 O(log n/n) 的架构近似残差。

Result: 理论结果建立了从链路预测风险到边得分误差、再到社区分类误差的界。合成随机块模型实验验证了这一理论链条，并显示在某些情形下，理论界中的最坏情况常数会使预测偏保守。

Conclusion: 链路预测目标在特定模型与假设下，能够为节点分类提供可量化的表示学习保证。该框架通过架构近似残差保持架构无关性，并为图神经网络预训练的分类效用提供了理论依据；其保证强弱仍受统计条件及界中常数影响。

Abstract: Link prediction (LP) is a standard pretraining objective for graph neural networks (GNNs): hide edges, train an encoder-decoder to predict them, and reuse the node embeddings for downstream node tasks. Despite its empirical success, there is little quantitative theory explaining why an edge-level objective should produce embeddings that are linearly useful for node classification. We study this question in the finite-sample stochastic block model (SBM). For any bounded GNN encoder paired with a symmetric bilinear decoder, we prove a conditional reduction: small population held-out LP excess risk implies small community-classification error for a transductive linear classifier on the learned embeddings. The bound decomposes into an optimization residual, an architectural approximation residual, and explicit statistical terms of order $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{d/n})$ in bounded dense regimes. We further give a constructive few-label classifier: nearest-centroid classification under the positive semidefinite quadratic form $G = W Z^\top Z W$, whose label error is coupled to LP quality. The framework is architecture-agnostic through the approximation residual, and we instantiate this residual as $O(\log n / n)$ for a graph isomorphism network (GIN) with spectral positional encoding and a bilinear decoder. Synthetic SBM experiments validate the theorem chain from LP risk to score error to classification error, and show when the worst-case constants become conservative.

</details>


### [308] [Recursive State-Augmented Language Models: Persistent Latent-State Adaptation of Pretrained Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16375)
*Omer Haq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出递归状态增强语言模型，使有限维潜在状态跨轮次保存信息，并通过该状态调整预训练模型；在特定多轮推理任务中，即使解码器无法访问早期证据，也能取得有竞争力的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型通常受有限上下文窗口限制，难以直接利用较早的交互信息。论文旨在探索一种持续更新的潜在状态，使模型在不把早期证据放入当前解码上下文的情况下，仍能进行跨轮次推理。

Method: 提出递归状态增强语言模型框架，将处理过程模块化为观测编码、潜在状态转移和预测器适配三个环节，并给出确定性与随机性两种形式。实验仅评估确定性版本：冻结预训练的小型生成式语言模型，以潜在状态调节低秩适配器；早期证据不进入解码器上下文。

Result: 在四轮假设推断任务中，潜在状态门控的低秩适配器准确率为百分之八十九点一，接近使用完整上下文训练的低秩适配器基线（百分之八十八）。将状态清零或打乱会显著降低准确率，表明状态承载了有用信息。在两轮、训练中未见过码字的任务中，若干接口能提升候选排序表现，但在所评估的训练方案下，模型很少能准确生成目标码字。

Conclusion: 结果表明，持久化的潜在状态能够通过调节预训练语言模型，支持早期证据不在解码上下文中的序列推理。不过，实验仅覆盖确定性模型和有限任务；对于生成训练中未见码字的能力，现有结果仍较弱，不能据此推断该框架已普遍解决长程记忆或跨轮次生成问题。

Abstract: Language models rely on finite token contexts, limiting access to earlier interactions. We introduce Recursive State-Augmented Language Models (Recursive SALM), a framework in which a persistent latent state evolves with each observation and generates adaptations of a pretrained language model. A modular read-transition-write architecture separates observation encoding, latent dynamics, and predictor adaptation. We develop deterministic and stochastic formulations.
  The experiments evaluate only deterministic SALM with frozen GPT-2 small and exclude earlier evidence from its decoder context. On a four-turn hypothesis-inference task, latent-gated LoRA reaches 89.1% accuracy, comparable to 88.0% for a trained full-context LoRA baseline, with earlier evidence available solely through a 64-dimensional state. Zeroing or shuffling the state substantially reduces accuracy. In a two-turn task with codewords unseen in training, several interfaces improve candidate ranking over chance, but rarely generate the exact codeword under the evaluated training schedule. These experiments show that persistent latent-conditioned adaptation can support sequential inference when earlier evidence is absent from the decoder context.

</details>


### [309] [Sparse PCA with Overlapping Supports: NP-Hardness and an FPTAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15810)
*Alberto Del Pia,Dekun Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多个稀疏且两两正交的主成分优化问题：该问题即使只有两个成分也为难解问题，但在秩和成分数固定时可获得全多项式时间近似方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个主成分必须同时满足各自的稀疏约束与两两正交约束；当成分的非零位置重叠时，两类约束会相互影响。已有理论主要覆盖单个成分、支持集互不相交或共同支持集的情形，尚未解决支持集任意重叠且稀疏度可分别指定的一般模型。

Method: 将问题表述为：给定有理矩阵及各列稀疏度上限，寻找列正交且逐列稀疏的矩阵，使其与输入矩阵的投影能量最大。进一步将其约化为稀疏正交普鲁克鲁斯问题，即在原问题的可行域上最大化线性函数。针对成分数固定的情形，设计全多项式时间近似方案：按行进行近似动态规划，利用双列更新恢复精确正交性，并对无法通过此类更新处理的少数变量进行精确计算。

Result: 证明该稀疏主成分分析问题在秩与成分数均为二时仍为难解问题；其线性化后的稀疏正交普鲁克鲁斯问题在成分数为二时也为难解问题。与此同时，当秩与成分数固定时，算法可给出近似最优解，输出列严格正交，且元素可用精确形式表示为实代数数。固定精度参数后，算法步数关于输入维数近线性，仅相差多对数因子。

Conclusion: 研究将一般重叠支持集下的稀疏主成分分析推进到可证明近似求解的范围：尽管问题具有计算难度，但当秩和成分数固定时，仍可在近线性规模的计算步数内获得近似最优且严格满足正交约束的解。

Abstract: In sparse principal component analysis (SPCA) with several components, each must be sparse and all must be pairwise orthogonal. When the supports of the components overlap, these two requirements interact. For a covariance matrix of fixed rank, polynomial-time algorithms with optimality or approximation guarantees are known for one component, and for several components with disjoint supports or with a common support. We study the general model, with individual sparsity constants and arbitrary support overlaps. We are given a rational $n\times r$ matrix $R$ and $d$ sparsity constants $s_i \le n$, and the goal is to find an $n\times d$ matrix $X$ with orthonormal columns that maximizes $\|R^\top X\|_F^2$, such that column $i$ has at most $s_i$ non-zero entries. Here $RR^\top$ plays the role of the covariance matrix and has rank at most $r$. We show that SPCA is NP-hard even when $r = d = 2$, but admits a fully polynomial-time approximation scheme (FPTAS) when $r$ and $d$ are fixed. The returned solution has exactly orthonormal columns, and its entries are real algebraic numbers in an exact representation. For fixed $ε$, the number of steps of the algorithm is linear in $n$ up to a polylogarithmic factor. Our approach reduces SPCA to a sparse version of the orthogonal Procrustes problem, that is, to maximizing a linear function over the feasible region of SPCA. We show that this problem is NP-hard even for $d = 2$, and we give an FPTAS for it when $d$ is fixed. This FPTAS combines approximate dynamic programming over the rows, two-column updates that restore exact orthogonality, and an exact computation of the few variables for which these updates are not available.

</details>


### [310] [Experience-Handoff Reinforcement Learning for Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16409)
*Doyoung Kim,Byeongguk Jeon,Kailin Zhang,Sherry Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种训练方法，同时利用标量奖励进行梯度优化，并让模型从环境经历中提炼可供后续任务使用的经验；在多个智能体基准任务上，该方法优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习通常只依据标量奖励训练智能体，因而忽略了试验过程中产生的细粒度环境信息，例如错误日志。已有方法会通过反思或压缩整理经验，并将其传递给后续运行，但这些经验材料的生成方式本身通常没有经过学习优化。本文希望结合奖励驱动的梯度更新与环境经验传递，提升智能体的持续表现。

Method: 研究固定采用智能体上下文工程框架，训练同一个模型承担三种角色：生成行动、反思经验，以及整理任务专属手册。所提出的方法依据经验交接材料对后续行动生成表现的影响来评估其价值，并在固定框架下联合优化这三种角色的行为。

Result: 在多个智能体基准任务上，该方法的留出测试表现高于基线方法。摘要未提供具体任务名称、数值或统计显著性信息。

Conclusion: 智能体训练不应只优化当前运行中的行动，也应优化模型撰写、并由后续运行继承的经验材料。对经验提炼与交接进行联合学习，有望将环境反馈更充分地转化为未来表现提升。

Abstract: It is common to train Large Language Models (LLMs) for agentic tasks with Reinforcement Learning (RL) from scalar rewards. However, this throws away the fine-grained information about the environment that the agent experienced during the trial (e.g., error logs). To utilize this rich information, recent works wrap the agent in a harness that refines its own inputs through reflection or compaction and hands past experience to future rollouts, but the construction of the handed-off artifacts (e.g., a playbook) is itself not learned. We want the best of both worlds: gradient updates from a scalar reward, while also utilizing environment information to hand off experience. As a concrete testbed, we fix the Agentic Context Engineering (\ace{}) harness and train one model to act, reflect, and curate task-local playbooks. Our method, \acerl{}, evaluates candidate experience-handoff artifacts by their downstream effect on future actor rollouts, and co-optimizes the role-specific behaviors (action generator, insight reflector, and playbook curator) under a fixed scaffold. Across agentic benchmarks, \acerl{} achieves higher held-out performance than baseline methods. The results suggest that agent training should optimize not only the actions in the current rollout, but also the model-written experience that future rollouts inherit.

</details>


### [311] [Large Payments Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15814)
*Natraj Raman,Abhenandhan Manoharan,Vishwasreddy Thuniki,Dhvani Kansara,Anirudh Som,Hassan Firouzi,Kostis Gourgoulias,Laukik Hoshing,Sudhir Kanala,Austin Reiter,Ebby Abdul,Shantanu Chandra,Ryan Schmiedl,Zack Anderson,Sumitra Ganesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出大型支付模型：针对支付交易数据训练的领域基础模型，可复用于欺诈检测、授权预测和支付优化等任务，并兼顾实际部署的效率与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺诈检测、授权预测和支付优化等应用依赖相互重叠的交易信息。若为每项任务独立开发模型，会造成信息与建模能力重复利用。构建共享的支付智能能力，则需应对字段异质、标识符取值众多、属性随交易生命周期变化、历史记录冗长及结果稀少等难题，同时满足延迟和成本限制。

Method: 研究从头开始，使用大规模结构化交易数据预训练领域专用基础模型，最多处理四千亿个词元。模型采用字段专属编码与感知交易生命周期的自监督学习，并提供两种互补架构：独立式架构采用混合掩码，支持高效的逐笔交易推理；序列式架构通过紧凑的潜在表示纳入实体关联历史，以获取更丰富的时间上下文。研究还提供可选的知识蒸馏，以适应资源受限的部署。

Result: 实验考察了模型规模扩展、下游任务表现及表示复用。摘要报告称，该模型在商户服务和资金管理服务中的多个预测任务上优于有竞争力的专用基线；其学得的表示还可通过紧凑的任务专属组件，迁移适配到预训练阶段未见的目标。摘要未提供具体数值或显著性检验结果。

Conclusion: 大型支付模型展示了将交易数据上的共享表示用于多种支付预测任务的可行性，并通过不同架构和可选蒸馏兼顾上下文建模与部署效率。结合生产环境部署结果，研究将其定位为构建可复用支付智能能力的实用基础。

Abstract: Payment applications such as fraud detection, authorization prediction, and payment optimization draw on overlapping transaction information. This motivates a reusable payments intelligence layer that shares learned representations and predictive signals, rather than developing predictive models independently for each task. Realizing this shared capability requires handling heterogeneous transaction fields, high-cardinality identifiers, lifecycle-dependent attributes, long histories, and rare outcomes while meeting latency and cost constraints. We present Large Payments Model (LPM), a domain-specific foundation model designed for operational use and trained from scratch on large-scale structured transaction data to learn reusable representations for payment prediction tasks. LPM employs field-specific encodings and lifecycle-aware self-supervision in two complementary architectures: the Independent variant uses hybrid masking and supports efficient per-transaction inference, while the Sequence variant incorporates entity-linked histories through compact latent representations for richer temporal context. Optional distillation enables resource-constrained deployment. We pretrain LPM on up to 400 billion tokens and study scaling, downstream performance, and representation reuse. LPM improves over strong task-specific baselines across multiple prediction tasks in Merchant and Treasury Services, demonstrating applicability in distinct payment domains. Its representations also facilitate adaptation to a target not observed during pretraining through compact task-specific components. Together with production deployment, these results position LPM as a practical basis for reusable payments intelligence.

</details>


### [312] [Detection Is Not Early Warning: Evaluating Token-Level Alarms on Language Model Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16803)
*Marco De Negri,Luca Sartori,Lorenzo Baggi,Moritz Reihs,Eduard Frank Ďurech,Mennatallah El-Assady,Valentina Pyatkin,Anna Hedström*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向语言模型生成故障的预警评估协议，强调监测器是否能在故障起始前发出警报，而不只是判断最终回答是否有问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多语言模型故障从生成过程中的某个特定词元开始。常见的回答级指标忽略警报出现的时机，无法区分事后识别、故障发生后的检测与故障开始前的预警；只有提前预警才有机会中止或引导生成。

Method: 研究提出一套适用于具有标注起始点的故障的评估流程：先在验证数据上校准监测器阈值，使其达到应用设定的错误率，再运行监测器，并依据警报相对故障起始点的时间，对事后分类、故障中检测和故障前预警进行评分。研究以三个开放语言模型中的重复退化为案例，评估基于模型激活的线性探针及六种未经训练的基线方法。

Result: 在重复退化案例中，回答级指标趋于饱和；仅依据回答长度的规则就能取得近乎完美的表现。即使线性探针的受试者工作特征曲线下面积相同，其预警表现也可能差异很大。探针能在三个模型中的两个模型的大多数退化回答出现故障前发出预警。低秩适配器使起始点前紧邻区域内被标记的词元比例提高一倍以上，但主要原因是在故障真正开始前很久就标记了退化回答。未经训练的基线方法发出有效预警的频率没有显著高于随机警报。

Conclusion: 评估生成故障监测器时，必须考虑警报相对故障起始点的时机，并在明确的错误率约束下衡量提前预警能力。回答级指标可能掩盖监测器在实际干预时机上的重要差异；该协议为这类评估提供了更贴近应用需求的框架。

Abstract: Many failures of language models begin at a specific token, the onset. A monitor that reads the model during generation can classify the finished response, detect the failure once it is under way, or warn before it begins, and only a warning leaves time to stop or steer the generation. Monitors are usually evaluated with response-level metrics such as AUROC, which ignore when the monitor fires. We introduce PREWARN, an evaluation protocol for any failure with an annotated onset. It calibrates each monitor's threshold on validation data to an error rate chosen for the application, runs the monitor, and scores its alarms relative to the onset on all three tasks. As a case study we apply it to repetitive degeneration in three open language models, with early warning as the main task, using linear probes on activations and six untrained baselines. Response-level metrics saturate on this failure, and a rule that reads only the response length scores almost perfectly, while probes that AUROC ranks as equal warn very differently under PREWARN. Probes warn before the onset on most degenerate responses of two of the three models, low-rank adapters below the probe more than double the share of tokens just before the onset that it flags, largely by flagging degenerate responses long before the onset, and no untrained baseline warns much more often than random alarms. We release the probes with calibrated thresholds at https://huggingface.co/collections/marcodene/prewarn-degeneration-probes

</details>


### [313] [Training Calibrated Conditional Generative Models with a Sample-Only Coverage Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15829)
*Benjamin Remy,Raphael Rossellini,Chihway Chang,Rebecca Willett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种仅依赖生成样本、可微且能严格识别真实后验分布的覆盖率损失，用于改善科学逆问题中的不确定性校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件生成模型已广泛用于科学逆问题的后验采样，但常出现过度自信，导致不确定性估计不可靠。现有方法各有局限：基于覆盖率的可微目标需要计算模型密度，严格适当评分规则虽只需样本，却不能直接约束覆盖率。

Method: 提出基于期望覆盖概率的可微损失，并通过生成样本估计，无需计算概率密度，也不要求修改模型架构。理论证明该损失是一种严格散度：当且仅当模型分布等于真实后验分布时，损失才为零。

Result: 在基于模拟的推断基准上，该方法在不计算密度的情况下，达到与基于密度的覆盖率损失相当的表现。在高维成像逆问题中，相比基于评分规则的正则化方法，它得到校准更好的后验分布，同时点估计的重建质量变化很小。

Conclusion: 该方法弥补了覆盖率约束与样本型评分规则之间的不足，为各类条件生成模型提供了一种无需密度计算、具有理论保证的后验校准手段。

Abstract: Conditional generative models have become the tool of choice for sampling posteriors in scientific inverse problems, where we seek to sample images or signals conditioned on a set of indirect and noisy measurements. Yet they are often overconfident, which is a critical failure in scientific applications where accurate uncertainty quantification is essential. Existing regularizers fall short in complementary ways: differentiable coverage objectives require the model's density and do not identify the posterior, while strictly proper scoring rules such as the energy score need only samples but do not directly enforce coverage. We close this gap by introducing a differentiable loss function based on the expected coverage probability, estimated from samples alone. It applies to any conditional generative model, with no need for tractable densities or architectural changes. We prove that it is a strict divergence, vanishing if and only if the model equals the true posterior. On simulation-based inference benchmarks, it matches a density-based coverage loss without evaluating the density. On high-dimensional imaging inverse problems, it yields better-calibrated posteriors than scoring-rule regularizers, with little change in the reconstruction quality of point estimates.

</details>


### [314] [Fewer Expert Transfers Without Changing Routing: Expert Eviction for MoE Offloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15842)
*Pierre-Luc Bacon,Diego Calanzone,Jean-Maxime Larouche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，减少专家切换并不一定能减少实际传输开销；依据未来请求预测淘汰专家，可在不损害质量的情况下显著减少复制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家混合语言模型每个词元只调用少数专家，因此可将大部分专家权重保存在中央处理器内存中，并在需要时复制到图形处理器。已有方法通过让路由器连续多个词元使用同一组专家来减少路由切换，并将切换次数视为复制次数的替代指标，但这种代理指标未必反映真实传输开销，也可能损害模型质量。

Method: 研究者搭建了实际执行并计时中央处理器至图形处理器权重复制的测试环境，直接比较不同缓存策略的复制成本与下一个词元损失。提出一种不改变路由决策的淘汰规则：缓存需要腾出空间时，淘汰下一次使用时间最晚的专家；同时评估利用未来请求信息和历史请求计数进行预测的效果。

Result: 在一款图形处理器上的测试中，所有比最近最少使用缓存解码更快、且固定专家集合的策略，下一个词元损失至少增加零点零六七纳特。相比之下，按未来使用时间最远淘汰专家的规则，在质量不下降的前提下，将专家复制次数减少至最近最少使用策略的约二分之一点一，且距离理论最少复制次数仅差百分之三点五。观察后续十六个词元的请求可实现其复制量降幅的百分之九十六；基于历史请求计数的规则则仅实现百分之二十七。

Conclusion: 评估专家缓存策略时，应直接测量真实复制行为，而不能仅以路由切换次数推断开销。相较于改变路由以维持专家集合稳定，利用未来请求信息优化缓存淘汰，可以在保持模型质量的同时大幅减少复制；短期未来需求预测尤其有效。

Abstract: A mixture-of-experts (MoE) language model uses only a few experts per token, so a server can keep most expert weights in CPU memory and copy an expert to the GPU when the router requests it. Recent methods train the router to keep the same experts for several tokens, and treat fewer routing switches as a proxy for fewer copies. We measure the trade-off directly, in a harness that performs and times real CPU-to-GPU copies for OLMoE-1B-7B. On an L40S GPU, every tested policy that holds an expert set fixed and decodes faster than the least-recently-used (LRU) cache increases next-token loss by at least 0.067 nats. A change that leaves every routing decision intact does better: when the cache must evict an expert, evict the one whose next use lies farthest in the future. This rule copies 2.1x fewer experts than LRU at no loss in quality and is within 3.5% of the fewest copies possible. It needs future requests; the next sixteen tokens of demand recover 96% of its reduction, whereas a rule based on counts of past requests recovers 27%.

</details>


### [315] [LazyWeights: Decode-on-Demand Edge LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17108)
*Arnab Sanyal,Gourav Datta,Sandeep P. Chinchali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“惰性权重”方案：对量化模型权重进行无损熵编码，并在存储器与缓存之间按需解码，从而减少边缘设备的数据传输、推理延迟和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备运行大语言模型时，内存容量和带宽往往是主要瓶颈。现有量化格式通常为矩阵中的整数编码分配相同位数，但编码实际分布并不均匀，因而仍有压缩空间。若能在不改变现有图形处理器内核的前提下减少从存储器读取的数据，就有望提升推理效率。

Method: 保留现有量化格式，并调整其中一种量化方法，使权重编码更易压缩；随后将编码通过霍夫曼方法无损压缩，分布在交错数据通道中。在动态存储器与二级缓存之间加入小型解码单元，按需解码，使原有图形处理器内核保持不变。研究还设计并实现了专用集成电路解码单元，并与另一种熵编码方法比较。

Result: 在百分之一和百分之三点五的困惑度增幅限制下，三种模型的压缩版本比最佳现有格式小百分之五点七至三十一点四。解码单元在一纳米级工艺、十亿赫兹下的面积为零点二一平方毫米；在杰特森奥林纳米超级平台上，单令牌延迟最多降低百分之三十六、能耗最多降低百分之三十八，配合提示查找式推测解码时分别最多降低百分之五十四和百分之四十四。在杰特森纳米平台上，相比中央处理器上的既有推理实现，每令牌耗时减少百分之五十八至六十四、能耗减少百分之五十三至五十七；流式运行七十亿参数模型时，速度提升六点六倍，权重每个参数为三点八九位。霍夫曼解码在吞吐量相同的条件下，压缩率和电路面积均优于区间非对称数系解码；解码单元也在赛灵思超大规模加平台上实现了逐位精确运行。

Conclusion: 通过利用量化权重编码分布的不均匀性进行无损压缩，并在存储器与缓存之间硬件解码，可以在保持原有图形处理器内核的同时减少数据传输，改善边缘端大语言模型的存储占用、推理速度与能效。

Abstract: Memory decides which LLMs an edge device runs, and how fast: capacity on a $4$ GB Jetson Nano, bandwidth on an $8$ GB Jetson Orin Nano Super. Deployed formats (GPTQ, AWQ, $\texttt{llama.cpp}$'s K- and I-quants) spend equal bits on every integer code of a matrix, although the codes are far from uniform. $\texttt{LazyWeights}$ keeps these formats but tunes GPTQ so the codes compress, Huffman-codes them losslessly in interleaved lanes, and decodes them on demand in a small unit between DRAM and L2, so GPU kernels run unchanged and DRAM moves only coded bytes. At $1\%$ and $3.5\%$ perplexity budgets, coded Phi-3-mini, Mistral-7B and SmolLM-1.7B are $5.7-31.4\%$ smaller than the best $\texttt{llama.cpp}$ format. A bit-exact $0.21$ mm$^2$ ASIC decoder unit ($1$ GHz, $7$ nm) cuts Orin latency and energy per token by up to $36\%$ and $38\%$ ($54\%$ and $44\%$ with prompt-lookup speculation). On the Nano, $\texttt{LazyWeights}$ needs $58-64\%$ less time and $53-57\%$ less energy per token than CPU $\texttt{llama.cpp}$, and runs a streamed Mistral-7B 6.6$\times$ faster at $3.89$ bits per weight. A Huffman decoder beats an rANS decoder in rate and area at equal throughput; the ASIC decoder unit runs bit-exact on a Zynq UltraScale+ FPGA (AMD ZCU102).

</details>


### [316] [Your Hypergradient is Skewed: Antithetic Neumann Estimation for Bilevel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15895)
*Jason Bohne,Pawel Polak,Gary Kazantsev,David Rosenberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，截断新曼级数的逆海森矩阵估计会受到噪声偏斜性的额外影响，并提出反对称新曼估计器，通过配对海森矩阵样本抑制奇数阶误差，在多项双层学习任务中优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层优化广泛用于超参数调整、数据重加权、鲁棒学习等场景，其超梯度计算通常需要近似内层海森矩阵的逆。以往分析主要关注偏差和方差，本文揭示了第三种误差来源：新曼级数的乘积结构会放大各因子的噪声偏斜性。与普通梯度中的偏斜性可被小批量平均削弱不同，这种放大效应在收缩较弱、截断深度较大、内层条件数较高或单因子噪声不对称时会更加显著。

Method: 提出反对称新曼估计器：将每个海森矩阵样本与其相对于参考海森矩阵的镜像样本配对，并对两者得到的逆海森矩阵估计取平均。若参考矩阵精确，乘积中所有奇数阶噪声扰动项均被消除；若参考矩阵近似精确，残余奇数阶误差则受参考误差线性控制。该方法只需修改海森矩阵向量乘步骤，可与现有随机或方差缩减双层优化方法结合，并据此构造随机梯度版和递归方差缩减版。

Result: 在鲁棒回归、数据清洗、离线强化学习和类别不平衡分类任务上，每项实验均使用二十个随机种子。基于该估计器的方法在所有任务中都优于随机及方差缩减基线，相较最佳基线将损失降低百分之四至百分之二十五。鲁棒回归中的收益最大：测试损失降低百分之二十五，测试决定系数提高百分之五十四。

Conclusion: 新曼级数估计中的噪声偏斜性是不可忽视的超梯度误差来源。通过反对称配对抵消奇数阶扰动，所提出的方法能有效提升多种双层学习任务的表现，且易于融入现有优化流程。

Abstract: Bilevel optimization is a core tool in practical machine learning workflows, including hyperparameter tuning, data reweighting, robust learning, reinforcement learning, and imbalanced classification. Hypergradient computation requires the inverse of the inner Hessian, typically estimated via a truncated Neumann series. While existing analyses focus on bias and variance, we show that the product structure of the Neumann series introduces a third source of error: skewness. We identify that, unlike ordinary gradients, whose asymmetry is suppressed by mini-batch averaging, the Neumann estimator amplifies skewness across factors, with the third-moment error growing, under weak contraction, with truncation depth, inner condition number, and per-factor noise asymmetry. This arises in many practical settings such as corrupted labels, outliers, heterogeneous or class-imbalanced datasets. To address this, we introduce the Antithetic Neumann Estimator (ANE), which pairs each Hessian sample with a mirrored counterpart around a reference Hessian and averages the resulting inverse-Hessian estimates. With an exact reference, the antithetic product retains only even-order noise, eliminating all odd-order perturbation terms; with an approximate reference, the residual odd-order contribution is bounded linearly in the reference error. ANE requires only a modification of the Hessian vector step and combines with existing stochastic or variance-reduced bilevel methods. We instantiate ANE-SGD and ANE-VR, the latter adding recursive variance reduction. Across robust regression, data cleaning, offline reinforcement learning, and imbalanced classification, ANE-based methods improve on stochastic and variance-reduced baselines on every task (20 seeds each), lowering loss by 4 to 25% relative to the best baseline; the largest gains, on robust regression, are a 25% lower test loss and a 54% higher test r-squared.

</details>


### [317] [Owned Edits to a Shared Decision Rule: Learning Private Exceptions as Typed, Gated, Withdrawable Weight Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15917)
*John J. O'Hare*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种以“归属式修改”为单位的规则学习机制：各方只用自己的记录，在严格门控下提出有限、可撤回且仅影响自身对象的规则例外。合成实验显示，该方法在多数测试中优于原始规则，并达到多种调优基线的非劣效水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织可能共同采用同一项公开决策规则，但各自掌握的私有证据表明规则不适用于自身个案；由于不愿共享记录，常见的集中式或联邦式学习难以充分利用这些证据。论文旨在让各方能够改进规则，同时限制信息披露，并避免修改影响其他方。

Method: 先将公开规则精确编译为一个小型修正线性单元网络，作为结构化先验。每一方随后在规模有限、类型明确的声明空间中搜索修改，例如围绕对象、适用范围、形式和规模等字段提出具名取值；只有当本方记录通过考虑样本选择过程的证据门槛时，才提出修改。接收方依据声明重新计算修改，并写入专属于该方的单元。由此，修改可撤回、不会改变其他对象的决策，且披露信息受声明空间大小限制。

Result: 两项预先登记的合成数据分析涵盖二十种抽样得到的隐藏真实规则。在每方三十条记录时，该方法准确率为百分之九十六点六，相对于七种调优基线均在两个百分点以内，达到非劣效；未修改的公开规则为百分之九十一点七。移除先验或证据门槛的同类搜索，准确率降至百分之八十四。第三项预先登记测试中，在共享记录训练出的网络上叠加类型化修改，准确率提高五点四个百分点；在公开规则上叠加修改，则提高三点一个百分点。论文也报告了局限：当真实规则为各方共有、需要放宽规则、每方只有十条记录，或预先设定的门槛条件在两次运行中均未满足时，方法表现受限；另有一次测试中，获得记录的语言模型准确率与该方法大致相当。对于仅有规则的基础情形，按归属登记例外也能产生相同决策。

Conclusion: 研究表明，围绕公开规则进行受证据门槛约束的局部修改，可能是在不汇集私有记录的情况下适应组织差异的一种有效方式。其核心价值不仅在于预测表现，也在于修改范围可控、可撤回且信息披露有限；但优势取决于各方确有局部差异证据，并会受到数据量和门槛设计的影响。

Abstract: Organisations that apply the same published decision rule often hold private evidence that the rule is wrong for their own cases, and will not pool it. We describe a learning mechanism for that setting whose unit of change is an owned edit. The published rule is compiled exactly into a small ReLU network, a structured prior. Each party's learner searches a small typed space of declarations, a few named values such as subject, scope, form and size, and proposes one only when its own records pass a selection-aware evidence gate. The learning is that gated search around the prior. In two analyses registered before they ran, on synthetic data with 20 sampled hidden truths, the learner at 30 records per party was non-inferior, within 2 points, to all seven tuned baselines (a confined gradient learner, Ditto, FedRep, federated averaging, two pooled networks and pooled gradient-boosted trees), scoring 96.6% against 91.7% for the unchanged rule; the same search without the prior or the gate scored 84.0%. In a third registered test, typed edits over a network trained on shared records beat that network alone by 5.4 points and the same edits over the published rule by 3.1. An acceptance body recomputes each edit from its declaration and writes it into units reserved for that party, so by construction an edit changes no decision about any other subject, withdraws exactly, discloses at most log2 of the typed space's size in bits, and can hide nothing beyond its declared values. We report where it loses: truths shared by all parties, relaxations, ten records, a pre-stated gate criterion that failed in both runs, and a language model given the records that decided about as accurately on one draw. On a rule-only base, a table of owned exceptions makes the same decisions.

</details>


### [318] [Reinforcement Learning for Inventory Control under Regime-Switching Demand: A Controlled Comparison of LSTM, MLP, and (s,S) Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15945)
*Alex Haines,Nathanael L. Baisa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在需求隐含切换且补货提前期较长时，具备循环记忆的强化学习策略优势更明显；提前期为十天时，其表现达到无记忆策略的二点一七倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典订货策略在需求平稳时具有最优性保证，但难以应对隐藏需求状态之间的切换；仅依赖固定观测窗口的无记忆深度强化学习策略，也难以持续估计潜在需求结构。研究旨在检验循环策略能否优于无记忆策略和经典基线，并分析其优势如何随供应链补货提前期增长。

Method: 构建定制化仿真环境：每日需求由具有两种隐藏状态的马尔可夫过程生成，并以泊松分布产生需求量；评估时状态对智能体隐藏。比较经过优化的经典订货策略、多层感知机近端策略优化智能体，以及长短期记忆网络循环近端策略优化智能体。在计算资源匹配的条件下训练，并于零、三、六、十天四种补货提前期下，各进行三十次确定性回合评估；另用主成分分析考察循环网络隐藏状态的表征。

Result: 循环策略在所有补货提前期下均优于另外两种方法。其相对多层感知机策略的优势随提前期单调扩大：提前期为零时两者表现相当，提前期为十天时循环策略达到其二点一七倍。隐藏状态分析显示，短提前期下表征主要编码需求状态指示，长提前期下则转向编码多步在途库存状态。

Conclusion: 当需求状态不可直接观测、且补货提前期延长时，循环记忆能帮助策略整合历史信息并跟踪复杂的库存管线状态，因而成为提升表现的关键因素。研究结果支持在长信用分配跨度的库存决策任务中采用循环策略；不过结论依据特定仿真环境和有限数量的评估回合，适用范围仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Classical inventory policies such as (s,S) are provably optimal under stationary demand but cannot adapt when demand switches between hidden regimes, and memoryless deep reinforcement learning policies, confined to a fixed observation window, cannot maintain a belief over latent demand structure. We ask whether a recurrent policy offers a measurable advantage over both a memoryless policy and a classical baseline under regime-switching demand, and how that advantage scales with supply-chain lead time. We build a custom Gymnasium environment in which daily demand is generated by a two-state hidden Markov process with Poisson emissions, so that the regime is known at evaluation time but hidden from the agent. An optimised (s,S) heuristic, an MLP PPO agent, and an LSTM Recurrent PPO agent are trained under matched compute and evaluated at lead times of 0, 3, 6, and 10 days over 30 deterministic episodes each. The recurrent policy outperforms both alternatives at every lead time, and its margin over the MLP widens monotonically with lead time, from parity at lead time 0 to 2.17$\times$ at lead time 10. Principal component analysis of the LSTM hidden state explains the widening gap: the representation shifts from encoding a binary regime indicator at short lead times to encoding multi-step pipeline state at long lead times. Recurrent memory becomes the decisive architectural factor as the credit-assignment horizon lengthens.

</details>


### [319] [Maximizing Capacity Utilization of LoRA Adapters for Mitigating the Learning-Forgetting Trade-off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17191)
*Tomohiro Sawada,Kartik Goyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现低秩适配器常未充分利用设定秩所提供的容量，并提出通过有效秩正则化改善学习与保持之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩适配在异质任务和持续学习场景中常不及全量微调，且容易出现遗忘。研究指出，一个可能原因是适配器的有效秩远低于设定秩，导致更新容量未被充分利用，限制了更新能量的分布范围。

Method: 在语言模型训练损失中加入简单、稳定、可微且无需奇异值分解的二阶瑞尼有效秩正则项，在优化原有任务目标的同时鼓励适配器提高有效秩。研究还将容量最大化与有范数约束的更新联系起来，并开展不同秩、任务和训练调度设置的实验。

Result: 在三个模型、多个随机种子的混合领域秩扫描实验中，正则系数为零点零一时，所有不低于十六的测试秩上，维基语料平均困惑度均低于未加正则的低秩适配。除一组模型与秩的组合外，训练困惑度均有所改善或最多仅上升零点零二。单任务与调度实验显示，学习和保持之间的平衡会随配置而变化；文献中的若干低秩适配变体也存在容量利用不足的问题。

Conclusion: 有效秩不应只作为事后评估适配器质量的指标，也可以作为训练目标。通过显式提升有效秩，有望改善低秩适配的学习—遗忘前沿，并为参数高效微调提供一条与既有方法互补的改进方向。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) often underperforms full fine-tuning, especially in heterogeneous and continual-learning settings in which substantial forgetting occurs. In general, it suffers from an undesirable learning-forgetting trade-off across diverse fine-tuning settings. We identify a potential contributor: LoRA adapters, especially high-rank ones, frequently fail to realize the available capacity afforded by the configured rank. Specifically, we find that standard LoRA updates have effective rank far below their configured rank, limiting how broadly the update energy is distributed. Therefore, we propose training mechanisms that optimize the original objective while maximizing adapter effective rank. Our main method adds a simple, stable, differentiable, and SVD-free Rényi-2 effective-rank regularizer to the language-modeling loss. We also connect capacity maximization to norm-bounded updates. In mixed-domain rank sweeps on three models evaluated with multiple random seeds, a penalty weight of $λ=0.01$ lowers mean Wiki perplexity at every tested rank of at least $16$ relative to unpenalized LoRA. Mean training perplexity improves or increases by at most $0.02$ in all but one model-rank comparison. Single-task and scheduling experiments further show that the balance between learning and retention depends on the configuration. We also observe that several LoRA variants in the literature also suffer from the identified capacity issue, and our approach provides a complementary axis of improvement to LoRA-based parameter-efficient fine-tuning. These results suggest that effective rank is not only a post-hoc diagnostic of adapter quality, but a trainable objective for improving the learning-forgetting frontier. Our code is available at https://github.com/tomohiro-sawada/lora-capacity-utilization

</details>


### [320] [Riordan Arrays as Structured Operators for Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15954)
*Dennis Davenport,Dewayne A. Dixon,Moussa Doumbia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将瑞奥丹阵列作为人工智能与数据分析中的结构化算子，用于构造具有因果性、参数效率和可解释性的线性变换；文章主要提出研究方向与实验方案，尚未报告验证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 瑞奥丹阵列传统上用于描述生成函数变换及组合关系。作者希望把这种代数结构引入数据建模，以便为序列及层级数据提供兼具结构先验与灵活性的算子，并探索其在参数效率、可变长度处理和可解释性方面的潜力。

Method: 将输入序列表示为生成函数，并通过瑞奥丹阵列对应的变换，将其映射为生成函数乘以一个函数与输入变量替换的组合。由此构造参数化的因果线性算子，融合卷积、变量代换和多尺度混合。文章进一步讨论了可能的网络层、可变长度序列模型、可逆架构等设计，并梳理理论与数值方面的挑战及初步实验计划。

Result: 摘要未提供具体实验数据或性能比较，因此没有实证结果可供判断。其主要产出是提出一类潜在模型构件，说明其可能适用的任务，并指出无限维表述本身并不能自动保证实际模型有效，还需通过理论分析和实验检验。

Conclusion: 瑞奥丹阵列可能为特定类型的数据提供有用的归纳偏置，尤其是因果序列、递归生成或层级结构数据。作者并未主张其普遍优于现有方法，而是提出一项探索性研究方向；其有效性、数值稳定性及实际收益仍有待验证。

Abstract: Riordan arrays have traditionally been studied as infinite lower-triangular matrices that encode generating-function transformations and combinatorial relationships. This note proposes a complementary direction: using Riordan arrays as structured operators in artificial intelligence and data analysis. For a data sequence with generating function $A(z)$, a Riordan array $(g(z),f(z))$ produces the transformation \[
  A(z)\longmapsto g(z)A(f(z)). \] This yields a parameterized family of causal linear operators that combine convolution, substitution, and multiscale mixing. The resulting structure may support parameter-efficient neural-network layers, variable-length sequence models, interpretable recurrent systems, invertible architectures, and models for hierarchical or recursively generated data. We explain what the infinite-dimensional viewpoint does and does not provide, outline several possible architectures, identify theoretical and numerical challenges, and propose an initial experimental program. The aim is not to claim that Riordan arrays are appropriate for all data, but to identify settings in which their algebraic structure supplies a useful inductive bias.

</details>


### [321] [Negative-Only Policy Optimization for One-Sided Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17287)
*Jiacheng Xu,Shuo He,Fuxiang Zhang,Chaojie Wang,Bo An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文针对只能可靠识别错误、却无法确认正确的场景，提出只利用可信负例优化策略的方法，并在多类任务上取得更好表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现实任务缺乏完整可靠的验证器，廉价检查只能证伪失败，未被检查否定的答案仍可能是错误的。若像常规强化学习那样直接奖励这些答案，模型可能学会迎合局部检查的捷径。

Method: 论文将这种验证条件形式化为“单侧可验证”：已验证的负例是可靠反例，未被证伪的候选答案则视为未标注数据。提出“仅负例策略优化”，不奖励候选正例，而是通过相对于参考策略的负例抑制损失降低可信错误答案的概率；同时依据一组回答中未被证伪答案的比例，调节每次更新的强度。

Result: 在无法获得真实奖励的条件下，该方法结合单元测试、约束检查和大语言模型自验证器，在代码、逻辑与数学基准上提升了多次采样通过率，优于对照方法。收益在推理时增加计算量以及跨基准迁移时仍然保持。

Conclusion: 当正向验证不可靠时，不应把“未被证伪”误当作“已被证实”。只利用可信负例进行策略优化，可以减少对错误正例和检查捷径的强化，并适用于多种验证能力有限的任务。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) typically relies on a verifier that can reliably judge whether sampled solutions are correct. However, in many realistic settings, complete verification is generally unavailable, while cheap checks can only falsify failures. We formalize this regime as one-sided verifiability (OSV): verified negatives are reliable counterexamples, but verified positives are merely non-falsified candidates and may contain many false positives. This asymmetry makes standard RLVR unreliable, because reinforcing verified positives can directly reward shortcuts that pass partial checks. To address this issue, we propose Negative-Only Policy Optimization (NOPO), which treats verified positives as unlabeled and updates the policy only by suppressing trusted verified negatives. Specifically, NOPO combines a reference-relative negative suppression loss with a group-level adapter that scales each update by the proportion of non-falsified responses. Across unit-test, constraint-check, and LLM self-verifiers, NOPO improves pass@k over baselines on code, logic, and math benchmarks when oracle rewards are unavailable. These gains persist under inference-time scaling and cross-benchmark transfer.

</details>


### [322] [WELP : Weighted Ensemble in the LooP for committor learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15963)
*Jacky Chen,Rishal Aggarwal,David Ryan Koes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出将加权系综采样与承诺函数学习交替耦合的方法，以更有效地研究分子体系的稀有态间转变，并发布丙氨酸二肽承诺概率基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 承诺函数描述从给定构型出发，轨迹先到达两个目标态中哪一个的概率；它既刻画转变进程，也反映转变机制，因此是理想的集体变量。然而，学习承诺函数需要稀有转变轨迹，而提高转变采样效率又依赖良好的集体变量，形成相互依赖的难题。

Method: 提出“加权系综采样闭环”框架，交替进行采样与学习：先用加权系综模拟生成转变数据，并通过自洽损失训练神经网络承诺函数；再将学习到的承诺函数作为改进的集体变量，用于后续加权系综模拟。该过程持续迭代，使采样与模型逐步相互改进。

Result: 在双通道二维系统、丙氨酸二肽和折叠蛋白小蛋白三个体系中，该框架均优于贪心基线方法，表明有原则的探索策略有助于提升承诺函数学习效果。研究还整理并公开了丙氨酸二肽经经验确定的承诺概率基准数据集。

Conclusion: 该框架通过闭环结合稀有事件采样与承诺函数学习，缓解了转变数据不足和集体变量不佳之间的相互依赖，为高维分子体系的稀有转变研究提供了新方法；公开基准数据集也为评估承诺函数学习方法提供了标准。

Abstract: Understanding how complex molecular systems transition between metastable states is a central challenge in computational chemistry and biophysics. A key tool for characterizing these transitions is the committor function: the probability that a trajectory initiated from a given configuration reaches one state before another. Because the committor encodes both the progress and the mechanism of a transition, it is an ideal collective variable (CV). Learning it, however, requires sampling the rare transitions it describes. We address this circular dependency with a ``WE-in-the-loop'' framework that couples committor learning with weighted ensemble (WE), an enhanced sampling method that uses a CV to drive transition dynamics. Our framework alternates between the two: WE simulation generates transition data for training a neural network committor with a self-consistent loss, and the learned committor then serves as an improved CV for subsequent WE simulation. Across a two-channel 2D system, alanine dipeptide, and chignolin, our framework consistently outperforms greedy baselines, highlighting the importance of principled exploration. We also curate a public benchmark of empirically determined committor values for alanine dipeptide. Together, the framework and benchmark provide a new method for studying rare transitions in high-dimensional molecular systems and a standard for evaluating committor learning methods.

</details>


### [323] [Metal Sandbox: Isolated Execution and Command Scheduling for GPU Coding Agents on Apple Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17294)
*Haozhe Wang,Lina Chen,Fanqi Wan,Qi Han,Yinmin Zhang,Zheng Ge,Daxin Jiang,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 金属沙箱通过在多台苹果电脑之间调度开发命令，提高生成苹果图形计算内核任务时的硬件利用率；实验显示命令吞吐量提升，工具等待和批次耗时均下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成苹果图形计算内核的智能体需要使用原生系统工具和本地图形处理器。智能体会在模型请求、开发命令与图形处理器执行之间交替，导致图形处理器在不少执行阶段处于空闲状态。研究旨在共享多台电脑的执行资源，同时保留各智能体独立的工作空间与开发环境。

Method: 提出金属沙箱基础设施，为每个实例限制文件访问，并支持原生命令执行及多机间的命令级调度。并行命令可同时执行，串行命令则共享每个节点的单一执行槽；符合条件的等待命令可转移到有空闲资源的电脑运行，结果再返回原智能体。系统还接入港湾工作流，以统一流程完成任务执行、自动验证和执行过程数据收集。

Result: 在四台苹果迷你电脑节点的受控实验中，集中需求下的命令吞吐量提升至原来的1.68至2.05倍。对16条静态智能体执行轨迹的估算显示，在持续补充会话且平均执行时长不变的假设下，每个节点运行四个会话相较于一个会话，可使持续吞吐量提升约2.14倍。按动态调度重放实测轨迹时，在各阶段时长不变的假设下，工具平均等待时间减少18.8%，批次总耗时减少22.3%。

Conclusion: 金属沙箱表明，通过保留智能体隔离环境并跨节点调度命令，可以更充分地利用本地硬件，提升多会话任务的执行效率。不过，吞吐量与耗时收益依赖于实验设定及执行时长不变等假设。该系统及其港湾工作流集成已开放源代码。

Abstract: On-device inference requires kernels that make effective use of local accelerators. Efficient training and evaluation of models that generate Apple Metal kernels benefit from native macOS tools and concurrent agent sessions. These sessions alternate between model requests, development commands, and GPU execution, leaving GPU capacity idle for much of a rollout. We present Metal Sandbox, infrastructure that combines restricted per-instance file access, native command execution, and command-level scheduling across interconnected Macs. Each agent retains its workspace and private development environment. Parallel commands can run concurrently, while serial commands share one execution slot per node; eligible waiting commands can migrate to available Macs and return results to the original agent. Integration with Harbor supports task execution, automatic verification, and rollout collection through a common workflow. Controlled experiments on four Mac mini M4 nodes achieve $1.68\times$--$2.05\times$ gains in command throughput under concentrated demand. Sixteen static agent traces give an estimated $2.14\times$ sustained-throughput gain for four sessions per node versus one, assuming continuously replenished sessions and unchanged mean durations. Replaying these measured traces under dynamic scheduling reduces mean tool waiting by 18.8% and batch elapsed time by 22.3%, assuming unchanged phase durations. Metal Sandbox and its Harbor integration are open-source on GitHub.

</details>


### [324] [PhysICL: In-Context Learning of Physical Fields on Unstructured Meshes from Procedural Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15965)
*Fabien Casenave*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需针对新任务更新权重的物理场预测模型：模型仅用程序生成的合成任务预训练，即可从少量标注仿真中学习，并在不同大小、不同连通结构的非结构网格上预测场分布及其分位数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程设计研究通常只有数十至数百次仿真，每次仿真可能使用不同网格；传统代理模型往往需要针对每项研究重新训练，成本较高。论文希望将表格基础模型的上下文学习能力拓展到非结构网格上的物理场预测，使模型能根据少量示例直接适应新任务。

Method: 作者构建了物理场上下文学习模型，并只用程序生成的任务进行预训练。合成任务包含通用物理规律，以及在形变二维、三维模板网格上构造的边界元物理模型；模型只在采样到的网格点上评估这些任务，任务可由中央处理器在线生成，以持续供给图形处理器。推理时，模型根据带标签的仿真示例，在查询网格上直接预测场及其分位数，无需更新权重；同一模型支持可变数量的上下文示例、标量与场输入、任意数量的输出场，以及任务间变化的网格规模和连通结构。

Result: 在五个预训练中未出现的工程仿真数据集上，上下文示例增加时场误差总体下降；示例数达到32后，所有数据集和上下文抽样下的误差均低于最近邻仿真基线。模型在其中两个数据集上接近各数据集单独训练的降阶模型，在场具有低秩结构的数据集上则落后。面对中位节点数约110万的车体表面网格，模型以固定空间预算运行，在64个上下文示例时峰值显存约为4.27吉字节，与仅6000节点的二维结构力学网格上的4.07吉字节相近。对于与预训练先验差异较大的二维结构力学任务，仅用复现其几何与载荷、但不包含任何目标输出的程序任务进行微调，且成本为预训练的十分之一，便将应力误差降低至原来的约三分之一，即改善约2.7倍。

Conclusion: 研究证明，完全依靠程序合成任务预训练的模型，可以在非结构网格物理场预测中实现无需更新权重的上下文学习，并适应变化的示例数量、输出变量和网格规模。其效果会受任务与预训练先验的匹配程度及场的低秩特性影响；针对几何和载荷进行无输出信息的程序化微调，则可低成本改善分布外任务表现。

Abstract: Engineering design studies produce a few tens to a few hundred simulations of one problem, each on its own mesh, and their surrogates are retrained for every study. Tabular foundation models instead infer a new task from its examples in one forward pass, after pretraining on synthetic tasks. We demonstrate the feasibility of in-context learning for physical field prediction on unstructured meshes. A single model, PhysICL, pretrained solely on procedural tasks, predicts fields, with quantiles, directly on each query mesh from labeled simulations, without updating its weights. The same checkpoint accepts variable numbers of context examples, scalar inputs and input fields, and any number of output fields, while mesh size and connectivity can vary within a task. Its procedural prior draws generic laws and boundary-element physical models on morphed 2D and 3D template meshes, evaluated only at the points the model samples, fast enough that tasks generated on the fly by CPU workers keep the GPUs busy throughout pretraining. On five PLAID datasets never seen in pretraining, its field error decreases as the context grows and, from $K = 32$, is below that of the nearest context simulation on every dataset and context draw. It is on par with per-dataset reduced-order models on two of them, behind them where the fields are low-rank. On AhmedML surfaces with a median of 1.1 million nodes, twenty times the largest pretraining mesh, its fixed spatial budgets keep the peak GPU memory at $K = 64$ at 4.27 GiB, against 4.07 GiB on the 6,000-node meshes of 2D\_MSHE. Finally, on 2D\_MSHE, far from the prior, fine-tuning on procedural tasks that reproduce its geometry and loads, without any of its outputs, at a tenth of the pretraining cost, divides the stress error by 2.7.

</details>


### [325] [Linking Rapid Learning to Compositional Structure in Neural Networks: Evidence from Additive Analogies in Meta-Learned Parameter Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17314)
*Abigail L. Tenenbaum,R. Thomas McCoy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，经过元学习训练的神经网络，其适应不同函数后得到的参数向量会呈现有意义的加减关系；按函数间的符号关系重组参数，网络能够直接实现相应的新函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够凭少量经验快速学习，而神经网络通常需要大量训练。尽管元学习能让网络快速适应新任务，其内部机制仍不清楚。本文旨在揭示元学习网络如何组织学习过程，以及这种快速学习是否具有可解释的结构。

Method: 研究者先让网络通过元学习接触许多相互关联的函数。随后，将网络针对某一特定函数完成快速适应后的全部参数拼接为一个向量，视作该函数的表征，并考察这些向量之间是否存在类似词向量加减关系的规律。

Result: 不同函数对应的参数向量能够依据函数之间的符号关系进行加减和重组。由此得到的新参数向量所构成的网络，能够实现符号运算所预测的目标函数，而且这些目标函数并未经过直接训练。

Conclusion: 元学习不仅能提升网络对新函数的快速适应能力，也可能将初始参数塑造成一种有组织的几何结构，使后续学习沿着具有功能意义的轨迹展开。这为理解神经网络如何形成归纳偏置提供了参数空间层面的解释。

Abstract: Humans can learn rapidly from limited experience, while neural networks typically require extensive training. In this work, we investigate what properties of a neural network can enable it to learn rapidly. We study neural networks that have been optimized via meta-learning, a technique that exposes a network to many related functions to facilitate its subsequent learning of similar functions. Prior work has shown that these meta-learned networks can learn new functions rapidly, but how they do so remains unclear. To investigate their internal structure, we take inspiration from classic work demonstrating additive relationships among the learned vectors that neural networks use to represent words (e.g., "king" minus "man" plus "woman" approximates "queen"). Analogously, we look for additive relationships among a meta-learned network's "representations" of different functions; when a meta-learned network rapidly learns to implement a specific function, all of its adapted parameter values can be concatenated into a single vector that we treat as representing the function. We find that such parameter vectors can be added and subtracted according to symbolic relationships among the functions they represent, yielding new parameter vectors that successfully represent the functions predicted by the corresponding symbolic manipulations: the networks specified by these artificially recombined vectors actually implement the intended functions without any direct training on them. Our results suggest that meta-learning can tune a network's starting point to organize subsequent learning along systematic, functionally meaningful trajectories through the space of possible parameter vectors, providing a geometric basis for realizing inductive biases in neural networks.

</details>


### [326] [Subject-Held-Out Evaluation of Deep Ground Reaction Force Estimators Across Two Laboratories: Leakage and Cross-Laboratory Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15966)
*Hernan David Santos,Yinong Chen,Yu Ding,Xudong Zhang,Jian Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，数据划分方式和坐标系一致性对地面反作用力估计精度的影响，往往大于模型架构；跨实验室迁移表现尤其差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究常在单一实验室、未充分说明的坐标系中验证模型，有时还采用试次级划分，使同一受试者同时出现在训练集和测试集中。这些做法可能高估模型精度，并掩盖模型跨受试者、跨实验室应用时的实际局限。

Method: 研究使用两个独立数据集：步行与运动数据（六名受试者）和职业性患者搬运数据（十名受试者）。在统一流程、调参方法和三个随机种子下，比较卷积模型、卷积循环模型及卷积变换器模型，并评估四种方案：试次级划分、留一受试者验证、两实验室数据合并训练并匹配留出受试者，以及跨实验室迁移。同时检查输入与力学目标的坐标系是否一致。

Result: 试次级划分使决定系数相较于留出受试者评估高估了0.040至0.194；在职业性搬运数据中，这种高估超过了不同架构之间的全部差异。留出受试者时，三类模型的决定系数为0.583至0.804，最佳架构因数据集而异，且架构差距处于受试者间变异范围内。合并两实验室数据对匹配受试者的结果没有改善；两个方向的跨实验室迁移均低于随机水平。输入按行进方向对齐、而力目标仍留在测力台坐标系，会使步行数据中前后向力的解释方差降低0.57；基于牛顿定律的相关性检查可在训练前发现这一坐标系错配。

Conclusion: 地面反作用力估计模型应采用受试者隔离的验证方式，在训练前核查输入与目标的坐标系一致性，并在声称可部署前进行跨实验室测试。相比单纯更换网络架构，严谨的数据划分和坐标系处理更能避免精度高估并揭示模型的泛化边界。

Abstract: Deep networks that estimate ground reaction forces and centers of pressure from motion-capture markers are validated within one laboratory, in a seldom-stated coordinate frame, and sometimes with trial-level splits that put the same person in the training and the test set. We measured what each practice costs, and whether such models transfer between laboratories. Three sequence models (convolutional, convolutional-recurrent, and convolutional-transformer) were evaluated on two independent datasets, locomotion (6 subjects) and occupational patient handling (10 subjects), under one pipeline, one tuning procedure, three seeds, and four protocols: trial-level split, leave-one-subject-out, pooled two-laboratory training with matched held-out subjects, and cross-laboratory transfer. At a fixed configuration, trial-level splits overstated the subject-held-out coefficient of determination by +0.040 to +0.194; on occupational data the overstatement exceeded every difference between architectures. With subjects held out, the three models reached values between 0.583 and 0.804; the leader differed between datasets, and gaps lay within subject-to-subject variability. Pooling the laboratories changed neither dataset on matched subjects. Transfer between laboratories was below chance in both directions, including the vertical force, which no in-plane rotation can affect. Leaving force targets in the plate frame while inputs were heading-aligned cost 0.57 of locomotion anteroposterior-force explained variance, enough to make that force look hardest; a Newton's-law correlation detected the mismatch before training. Split protocol and target frame changed reported accuracy more than architecture did. Learned force estimators should be validated with subjects held out, checked for frame consistency before training, and tested across laboratories before deployment claims are made.

</details>


### [327] [Characterizing the Edge of Stability in Variational Training Without Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15990)
*Juraj Marusic,Jonathan Wenger,Beau Coker,John P. Cunningham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，在仅优化期望损失的变分深度学习中，较大学习率和较少的参数采样会促使模型落入更平坦的损失极小值，并可能提升泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分方法通过对权重分布施加先验约束来改善不确定性估计和分布外泛化，但深度学习中的先验难以设定，实践中常大幅削弱先验散度正则项。仅依赖优化器隐式正则化、直接训练期望损失可绕开先验设定问题；然而，学习率等优化超参数如何影响这种隐式正则化，现有理论尚未充分解释。

Method: 作者从理论和实验两方面研究仅训练期望损失的变分方法，分析学习率、参数蒙特卡洛采样数量与损失景观平坦性的关系，并刻画该方法所处的修正稳定性边缘，将其与梯度估计的信噪比及采样数量联系起来。随后在分布外基准数据集上验证理论结论。

Result: 理论分析和实验均显示，较大学习率以及较少的参数样本会促使训练收敛到更平坦的极小值；这类极小值通常与更好的泛化表现相关。研究还发现，该方法的修正稳定性边缘受梯度估计信噪比和蒙特卡洛样本数共同刻画，分布外基准实验验证了这些结论的实际意义。

Conclusion: 在不显式依赖先验散度正则化时，优化超参数仍可通过隐式正则化显著影响模型的解与泛化表现。学习率和参数采样数因此不仅是计算设置，也是在期望损失训练中调节平坦性及分布外泛化的重要因素。

Abstract: Variational approaches in deep learning promise to deliver improved uncertainty quantification and out-of-distribution generalization by inferring a distribution over weights, regularized by a divergence to a chosen prior. However, prior elicitation presents a significant challenge in deep learning, resulting in the common practice of significantly downweighting the regularization term in the variational objective. Recently, it was proposed to train solely via the expected loss by relying on implicit regularization from the optimizer, rather than explicit regularization from the divergence to the prior. While this approach largely sidesteps the issue of prior elicitation, current theory lacks an understanding of how key optimization hyperparameters impact the implicit regularization. Here, we theoretically and empirically demonstrate that larger learning rates and fewer parameter samples lead to flatter minima of the loss landscape, which are known to generalize better. We show that training on the expected loss operates at a modified edge of stability, characterized by the signal-to-noise ratio of the gradient estimates and the number of Monte Carlo samples. Experiments on out-of-distribution benchmark datasets confirm the practical significance of the theoretical results.

</details>


### [328] [Fit-LRP: Fitting Linear Relaxations to Polytopes for Neural Network Verification over Non-Convex Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16003)
*Brian Yarbrough,Jesse Wayment,Jingbo Wang,Philip E. Paré,Shreyas Sundaram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多面体区域优化神经网络线性界的方法，减少验证中的过度近似与分支搜索；在困难区域上，分支数最多降低四十七倍，最坏验证时间缩短十五倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络验证器通常在轴对齐盒状区域上计算线性松弛，但控制系统中的可达性、避障和神经李雅普诺夫证书往往对应斜置或非凸多面体。将这类区域近似为盒子会造成过松的界；在分支定界过程中，轴对齐划分还可能引发组合爆炸，甚至难以终止。

Method: 提出适配多面体的线性松弛框架，可在由多个多面体构成的非凸区域上，对仿射界进行精确优化。该方法通过依次求解规模较小、维度较低的线性规划实现，并可接入线性松弛传播及阿尔法、贝塔皇冠分支定界算法的最终优化步骤。它直接利用原始多面体状态约束，避免因盒状近似产生的额外松弛，从而更早剪除搜索分支。

Result: 在国际自动验证竞赛基准和神经李雅普诺夫基准上，困难区域所需的分支数最多减少四十七倍，最坏情况下的验证时间缩短十五倍。采用区域级划分后，该方法在单个中央处理器上的验证速度比阿尔法、贝塔皇冠方法在图形处理器上快六点一倍。

Conclusion: 该方法将神经网络验证中的最终界优化从盒状输入域扩展到精确的多面体域，有助于缓解斜置约束造成的界过松和搜索膨胀，并显著提升复杂控制系统验证的效率。

Abstract: State-of-the-art neural network verifiers evaluate linear relaxations over axis-aligned bounding boxes. However, verification domains in cyber-physical systems are rarely boxes. Reachability specifications, obstacle-avoidance constraints, and neural Lyapunov certificates typically yield oblique or non-convex polyhedral regions, over which box-based bounds become extremely loose. This paper proposes Fit-LRP (Fitting Linear Relaxations to Polytopes), a framework the implements exact optimization of affine bounds over non-convex domains (represented by unions of polytopes) by solving a sequence of small, low-dimensional linear programs. Our approach seamlessly enhances the final optimization step of LiRPA and $α,\! β$-CROWN branch-and-bound (BaB) algorithms. In BaB verification, approximating oblique constraints with axis-aligned splits causes severe combinatorial blowup and risks non-termination. By optimizing the final affine bound of a nonlinear function over the exact polyhedral state-space constraints our method eliminates box-induced over-approximations, enabling earlier search-tree pruning. On ARCH-COMP and neural Lyapunov benchmarks, Fit-LRP reduces branching on the hardest region by $47\times$, cuts worst-case verification time by $15\times$, and with region-wise partitioning, verifies on a single CPU $6.1\times$ faster than $α,\! β$-CROWN on a GPU.

</details>


### [329] [Runtime Analysis of Cartesian Genetic Programming on Max: A Proven Exponential Speedup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16013)
*Duc-Cuong Dang,Roman Kalkreuth,Andre Opris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文首次为笛卡尔遗传编程在经典最大化问题上给出理论运行时间保证，证明其找到等效最优程序的期望时间至多为最优程序长度对数的九次方，较树式遗传编程展现指数级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 笛卡尔遗传编程以图结构编码程序，虽已发展约二十年，却主要依靠实验研究，理论性能保证十分有限。此前针对该最大化问题的严格分析仅涉及树式遗传编程，且其期望运行时间随最优程序长度呈超线性增长。论文旨在弥补这一理论空缺，并检验图式表示是否能带来搜索效率优势。

Method: 作者从理论上分析采用常见变异算子的（一加一）笛卡尔遗传编程，研究其在最大化问题上的搜索过程，重点考察算法找到输出与最优程序相同的等效最优程序所需的期望时间，并以树式遗传编程已有的运行时间结果作为比较依据。实验用于补充和验证理论分析。

Result: 理论证明表明，该算法找到等效最优程序的期望时间至多为最优程序长度对数的九次方，即具有多对数级上界。相较于树式遗传编程已知的超线性期望运行时间，这意味着图式表示在该问题上可实现指数级加速。实验结果与理论发现相互补充。

Conclusion: 研究为笛卡尔遗传编程在一个经典基准问题上的性能提供了首个严格理论保证，说明图式程序表示的紧凑性及其搜索空间结构有助于高效搜索。不过，该结论针对特定问题和算法设定，不能直接视为对所有程序演化任务的普遍保证。

Abstract: Genetic Programming (GP) is a search paradigm inspired from natural evolution for the automated discovery of expressions, functions, and computer programs. Cartesian GP (CGP) is a flavor of GP that uses a graph-based model to encode candidate programs, as contrasted to the conventional Tree-based GP (TGP). Since its inception two decades ago, CGP has predominantly been analyzed empirically, thus little is known regarding its theoretical performance guarantees. This paper analyzes CGP for the Max problem, which has been only rigorously studied for TGP with a proven expected runtime superlinear in the optimal program length $n$. We prove that the expected time for the ($1$+$1$)~CGP employing common mutation operators to evolve an equivalent optimal of the same output is polylogarithmic in $n$, specifically at most $O(\log^9{n})$. This is remarkable, as it shows an exponential speedup by switching to CGP. Our results shed light on the benefit and the compactness of the graph-based representation for programs, and on how CGP can navigate its search and fitness spaces efficiently. This is a first proven performance guarantee for CGP on an established benchmark problem. Experiments complement our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [330] [Protocol-Aware Auditing of Domain-Blind Feature Attribution in Network Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16018)
*Emmanuelli El-Mahmoud,Abanisenioluwa Kolawole Orojo,Pablo Rivas,Erika Leal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出面向协议规则的特征归因查询控制方法，减少解释过程因访问规则无效状态而产生的偏差，并通过多组网络入侵检测实验检验其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征归因方法可能通过扰动、特征组合、参考样本、积分路径或特征消融，查询违反网络协议规则的状态。因此，解释结果及其评估可能受模型在实际运行域之外的行为影响。论文将这一问题形式化为“域盲视”，并指出：若两个模型在声明的规则有效域内具有相同的必要信息，且归因程序只查询该域，那么程序应对两者给出相同归因。

Method: 论文将协议规则转化为可检验的查询约束，并在局部可解释模型无关解释、沙普利值解释、积分梯度和深度学习传播解释四类方法中实现协议感知的查询控制。研究在四个网络入侵检测数据集和三类分类器上，对标准配置与受控配置进行配对审计；另以支持集建模、原始流量重建和自适应解释攻击作为补充验证层。

Result: 在六种标准局部可解释模型无关解释显示顶级特征完全可重复的设置中，协议感知版本揭示了显著波动，平均重复性为0.575。经快速梯度符号攻击并修复协议后，协议感知深度学习传播解释和协议感知积分梯度的归因相似度曲线面积最高，分别为0.452和0.413。协议感知方法内部的平均排序一致性由0.217升至0.366。

Conclusion: 研究表明，解释方法的查询是否符合领域规则，会显著影响归因结果及其可靠性评估。将结构化领域假设明确落实为查询控制，可使解释审计更贴近实际运行域；不过，论文也强调需要结合支持集、原始流量重建和自适应攻击等手段进行互补验证。

Abstract: Feature-attribution methods can query rule-violating network states through perturbations, coalitions, references, paths, and ablations, making explanations and their evaluation sensitive to model behavior outside the operational domain. We formalize this failure mode as domain blindness and prove valid-query invariance: when models agree on the required oracle information within a declared rule-valid set, a procedure confined to that set returns the same attribution. We instantiate one protocol-aware query-control principle in LIME, SHAP, Integrated Gradients, and DeepLIFT and audit paired configurations across four NIDS datasets and three classifier families. In six settings where standard LIME reports perfect top-feature repeatability, PA-LIME reveals substantial variability, with a mean of 0.575. Under FGSM followed by protocol repair, PA-DeepLIFT and PA-IG achieve the largest attribution-similarity curve areas, 0.452 and 0.413, while mean within-PA rank agreement rises to 0.366 from 0.217. PA-XAI therefore turns structured domain assumptions into explicit, testable controls for explanation auditing; support modeling, raw-flow reconstruction, and adaptive explanation attacks provide complementary validation layers.

</details>


### [331] [Imitating the Imperfect: Offline-to-Online Robust Imitation Learning from Heterogeneous Demonstrators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16020)
*Cheng Pan,Nan Lu,Kai Arulkumaran,Josie Hughes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多来源、质量参差且示范者水平未知的模仿学习方法，通过联合估计状态—动作的最优性分数与示范者的潜在专业水平，并利用在线交互持续改进策略和评分模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法往往假设示范来自同质或接近最优的专家，但现实中的示范通常由能力不同的示范者提供，质量混杂且水平标签缺失。直接使用这类数据可能使学习到的策略受低质量示范误导；此外，离线学习与在线执行之间的分布偏移也会削弱策略效果。

Method: 研究提出一种基于潜在专业水平与最优性评分的模仿学习框架。该框架从无标签的次优离线示范中联合学习状态—动作最优性评分模型，以及每位示范者的潜在专业水平。随后，智能体在在线策略优化中最大化估计得到的最优性分数，并利用在线轨迹迭代更新评分模型，以缓解在线适应中的分布偏移。论文还针对最优性评分识别、条件最优占用分布及策略恢复给出了满足明确充分条件时的理论保证。

Result: 在连续控制基准实验中，该方法在示范者差异显著、示范质量较低等设置下，整体表现持续优于现有基线。摘要未提供具体任务、数值或显著性检验结果。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，不必将所有示范视为同等可靠，也可以从水平未知、质量混杂的离线数据中提取有用的最优性信号，并通过在线交互进一步修正模型。其理论分析与实验结果共同支持该框架处理现实异质示范的潜力；实际效果仍依赖于相关充分条件及在线优化过程。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL) typically relies on large-scale offline demonstrations collected from multiple human or algorithmic demonstrators. However, most existing approaches assume these demonstrators are homogeneous or near-optimal experts, a convenient but unrealistic assumption in real-world applications. In practice, demonstrations are often collected from heterogeneous demonstrators with unknown and varying levels of expertise, resulting in highly mixed-quality data. In this work, we study a challenging yet practical setting of imitation learning from such heterogeneous imperfect demonstrators. We propose Latent Expertise and Optimality Scoring based Imitation Learning (LEOS-IL), a principled framework that jointly learns (i) a state-action optimality scoring model and (ii) latent expertise levels for each demonstrator from unlabeled suboptimal offline demonstrations. The learned scoring model is then integrated into an online policy optimization procedure, where the agent is trained to maximize estimated optimality scores while its online rollouts are iteratively leveraged to refine the scoring model, thereby mitigating distributional shift during online adaptation. We further provide theoretical guarantees for optimality score identification and conditional optimal occupancy and policy recovery under explicit sufficient conditions. Experiments on continuous control benchmarks demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms existing baselines across highly heterogeneous and low-quality demonstration regimes.

</details>


### [332] [A Unified Interpretable Semantic Framework for Multi-Modal Fiber Sensing Under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16027)
*Khouloud Abdelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出面向数据稀缺场景的统一、可解释多模态光纤传感框架，在三类传感数据上分别取得百分之九十五、百分之九十一点一和百分之九十四点六的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光纤传感部署环境不断变化，而实际数据往往有限；不同传感模态产生的测量数据异质，传统方法可能难以跨模态适用，也不易解释判断依据。论文旨在以统一且数据高效的方式应对这些问题。

Method: 框架将偏振态监测、分布式声学传感和光时域反射测量的数据，映射为一组具有物理依据的共享语义描述，包括突发性、持续性、结构特征和能量集中度。随后通过推理比较语义类别画像；在判断存在歧义时，可检索语义相似样例辅助决策，并支持适应新场景而无需重新训练模型。

Result: 在真实的三类光纤传感数据集上进行了验证，偏振态监测、分布式声学传感和光时域反射测量的准确率分别为百分之九十五、百分之九十一点一和百分之九十四点六。结果显示，该框架能够跨传感模态保持较好的表现，并提供透明、具有物理可解释性的判断。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一种统一、可解释且数据高效的光纤传感分析方案，适用于数据稀缺且持续演变的部署环境。

Abstract: We propose a unified interpretable semantic framework for multi-modal fiber sensing under data scarcity. The framework maps heterogeneous measurements from state of-polarization (SOP) monitoring, distributed acoustic sensing (DAS), and optical time-domain reflectometry (OTDR) into a shared set of physics-grounded semantic descriptors capturing impulsiveness, persistence, structural characteristics, and energy concentration. Inference is performed through reasoning over semantic class profiles, optionally assisted by retrieval of semantically similar examples to resolve ambiguity and support adaptation without model retraining. Experimental validation on real SOP, DAS, and OTDR datasets demonstrates robust performance across sensing modalities, achieving accuracies of 95.0%, 91.1%, and 94.6%, respectively, while providing transparent and physically interpretable decisions. The proposed framework establishes a unified and data efficient approach for optical fiber sensing in evolving and data scarce deployment environments.

</details>


### [333] [Robustness Within a Fairness Budget: Parity Frontiers and Equal-Opportunity Reversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16031)
*Pablo Rivas,Jeremy Frank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究如何在抵御对抗攻击的同时控制群体公平性，并发现准确率、公平性与不同群体指标之间存在需要综合权衡的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练能够提升模型在攻击下的预测表现，却不能决定模型的决策如何分布于受保护群体之间。因此，仅评估鲁棒性不足以判断模型是否公平，还需在训练中直接约束群体差异，并审查多种群体指标。

Method: 将二分类建模为投影梯度下降对抗风险最小化问题，并加入平方形式的软人口统计均等约束。通过投影乘子与单侧惩罚更新优化。实验使用成人收入数据集，以性别字段进行群体审计但不将其作为预测特征；比较逻辑回归与单隐层多层感知机，在三组配对随机种子、四种攻击强度和五种公平预算下进行测试。

Result: 在攻击强度为零点三时，相较经验风险最小化，对抗训练使平衡准确率分别提高七点七七和十一点四五个百分点，并消除了攻击导致的硬人口统计均等差异增幅的百分之八十五点七和百分之八十九点五。在代表性预算零点零一五下，受约束训练相较仅对抗训练将该差异分别降低百分之五十二点二和百分之四十九点二，同时仍比经验风险最小化高三点一八和八点三四个百分点。八条模型与攻击路径的种子平均结果均显示，所测预算路径对差异和准确率有严格排序，并使平均有符号机会均等差异发生反转。论文还给出了反转所需的真正例率变化条件，实测群体率满足该条件。

Conclusion: 在本文受控的特征空间实验中，鲁棒性与人口统计均等可以实现有价值的联合工作点，但公平预算会影响不同群体指标，且某一项公平指标不能替代完整的群体率审计。

Abstract: Adversarial training protects predictive performance under attack but does not determine how decisions are distributed across protected groups. We formulate binary classification as projected gradient descent (PGD) adversarial-risk minimization under a squared soft demographic-parity (DP) budget, optimized with projected multiplier and one-sided penalty updates. On Adult Income, using the binary sex field supplied by the benchmark for group auditing but excluding it from prediction, we test logistic regression and a one-hidden-layer multilayer perceptron (MLP) across three paired seeds, four PGD strengths, and five budgets. At $\varepsilon=0.3$, PGD training improves balanced accuracy over empirical-risk minimization (ERM) by $7.77$ and $11.45$ percentage points and removes $85.7\%$ and $89.5\%$ of attack-induced growth in hard demographic-parity difference (DPD). At the representative interior value $τ=0.015$, constrained training cuts DPD relative to PGD training by $52.2\%$ and $49.2\%$, while remaining $3.18$ and $8.34$ balanced-accuracy points above ERM. Across all eight seed-averaged model-attack paths, the sampled budget path strictly orders DPD and accuracy and reverses mean signed equal-opportunity difference (EOpp). A rate theorem gives the exact true-positive-rate shift required for reversal, and the measured group rates satisfy it. Within this controlled feature-space study, robustness and parity admit useful joint operating points, but one fairness metric cannot replace a full group-rate audit.

</details>


### [334] [Continual Event Recognition for Autonomous Optical Network Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16038)
*Khouloud Abdelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出面向光网络监测的持续事件识别框架，可识别已知扰动、发现并纳入新扰动，并探索重叠扰动的组合推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主光网络中的扰动会不断变化，传统监测系统往往需要反复重新训练，甚至中断服务。论文旨在实现能够持续适应新事件、同时保持网络运行的监测机制。

Method: 框架以偏振态监测为基础，结合原型表示、开放集识别和少样本原型注册：识别已知扰动，检测未知事件并将其纳入系统；此外，还通过已有原型的组合推理，考察能否识别重叠扰动。

Result: 在长达二千六百公里、基于可重构光分插复用设备的光纤测试平台上，已知类别识别准确率达到百分之九十九点八二。系统无需重新训练编码器即可适应新扰动。部分重叠扰动可通过原型组合实现零样本识别，另一些则需要少样本注册。

Conclusion: 实验表明，持续事件识别有望成为自主光网络监测的重要范式。重叠扰动的可组合性并不一致，因此实际系统需结合组合推理与少样本注册，以适应不同类型的新事件。

Abstract: Autonomous optical networks require monitoring systems capable of adapting to evolving disturbances without repeated retraining or service interruption. We propose a continual event recognition framework for state-of-polarization (SOP)-based optical monitoring based on prototype representations, open-set recognition, and few-shot prototype registration. The framework supports recognition of known disturbances, detection and integration of novel events, and investigates whether overlapping disturbances can be inferred through compositional reasoning from existing prototypes. Experimental validation on a 2600-km ROADM-based optical testbed demonstrates 99.82% closed-set recognition accuracy, successful adaptation to novel disturbances without encoder retraining, and reveals that overlapping optical disturbances exhibit varying degrees of latent compositionality. While some overlaps can be recognized in a zero-shot manner through prototype composition, others require explicit few-shot registration. These results establish continual event recognition as a promising paradigm for future autonomous optical network monitoring systems.

</details>


### [335] [Improved Elastic Weight Consolidation as an Optimization Constraint for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16039)
*Davide Wiest*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从优化约束角度重新解释弹性权重巩固，指出其经验费舍尔重要性会受样本损失梯度大小影响，并提出改进方法以更公平地保持旧任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统弹性权重巩固以经验费舍尔矩阵构造二次惩罚，通常将其合理性归因于概率曲率。论文指出，这一解释掩盖了关键缺陷：方法实际按损失梯度大小为样本加权，因此已拟合较好的样本影响会变小，难以可靠保留旧任务行为。

Method: 提出“持续学习作为优化约束”的框架，将新任务训练表述为保留旧任务中指定行为函数。由此推导出，传统方法的经验费舍尔矩阵恰是以每个样本损失为保留函数时的约束一阶形式。进一步提出改进弹性权重巩固：依据旧样本损失等值面的局部一阶近似，对输出变化施加惩罚，从而消除对损失梯度尺度的依赖，并降低错误样本支配重要性矩阵的风险。

Result: 在分类、分割、语言、预测和扩散等任务中，改进方法的不同变体整体上达到或超过传统方法，并优于针对分类问题提出的既有改进方法。在独立留出评估中，使用验证集选择超参数后，均匀版本在图像分类基准上的准确率比匹配的传统方法高百分之二，百分之九十五置信区间为百分之零点九三至百分之三点零七。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了传统弹性权重巩固样本加权机制的普遍缺陷，并将其归结为约束选择所致，而非仅限于分类任务。基于损失等值面构造的改进方法能更均衡地保护旧任务行为，且在多类任务和留出评估中表现出竞争力。

Abstract: Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) is a widely used regularization-based technique for continual learning, protecting performance on old tasks with a quadratic penalty whose empirical Fisher is usually justified through probabilistic curvature. We instead introduce Continual Learning as an Optimization Constraint (CLOC): new training must preserve chosen functionals of learned behavior. EWC's empirical Fisher is exactly the first-order form of this constraint when the preserved functional is each sample's loss. This derivation exposes a flaw: EWC implicitly weights each sample by the scale of its loss gradient, so well-fitted samples lose importance. EWC-DR observed this failure in classification; CLOC identifies its general cause. Improved Elastic Weight Consolidation (IEWC) penalizes output changes according to the first-order approximation of each old sample's loss level set, removing the weighting for any differentiable loss and output geometry, and prevents corrupt samples from dominating the importance matrix. Across classification, segmentation, language, forecasting, and diffusion, IEWC variants match or improve on EWC and outperform EWC-DR. In a held-out evaluation with validation-selected hyperparameters, uniform IEWC improves ImageNet-R accuracy over matched EWC by 2.00 percentage points (95% CI 0.93-3.07).

</details>


### [336] [Human Capital in Context: What Employment Survival Reveals About Worker-Firm Match](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16058)
*Jung Ho Choi,Mario Milone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于劳动者与企业配对关系的匹配质量指标。该指标能预测雇佣关系是否持续、劳动者收入及企业后续表现，也能预测劳动者流动去向和并购中的商誉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 劳动者并不存在脱离企业情境的普遍“最佳人选”：同一劳动者可能适合某家企业，却不适合另一家。只衡量劳动者、企业或职业本身的指标，无法捕捉这种配对特有的契合度。论文旨在构建并检验一种直接刻画劳动者—企业匹配质量的指标。

Method: 研究以雇佣关系的存续情况学习匹配质量。双塔神经网络分别根据劳动者的职业经历与当前职位、企业的特征与业务描述，将双方映射到同一向量空间，并以向量余弦相似度衡量匹配程度。指标的函数形式借鉴企业特定人力资本的技能权重模型；模型唯一估计目标是离职风险，将工作匹配视为需要通过实际经历逐渐了解的体验品。样本覆盖超过十亿个人—时期记录、约一亿零一百万名劳动者和八百四十万家企业。

Result: 匹配质量提高零点一，与离职几率降低百分之四十八相关。匹配质量的方差约四分之一来自劳动者与企业配对本身，而劳动者、企业或职业层面的指标按定义无法体现这部分差异。匹配更好的劳动者收入更高，且这种关系在雇佣关系初期尤为明显。员工匹配得分较高的企业，在随后五年中相较于同行增长更快、盈利能力更强，并获得数量更多、价值更高的专利。该指标还可用于预测尚未形成雇佣关系的劳动者会流向哪些企业；在并购中，若目标企业的劳动者更适合收购方而非原企业，交易价格中计入的商誉比例也更高。

Conclusion: 劳动者与企业的契合度具有重要且独立的配对特征，并与雇佣关系稳定性、劳动者收入、企业创新和增长等结果相关。由于该指标也能评估尚未实际形成的配对，它可用于研究劳动者流动和并购中的人力资本价值。摘要报告的是经验关联，不能仅据此断定匹配质量对这些结果具有因果效应。

Abstract: There is no best worker in general, only a best worker for a particular firm. We develop a measure of worker-firm match quality that treats fit as a property of the pair, learned from the survival of employment relationships. A two-tower neural network embeds workers, from their career histories and current positions, and firms, from their characteristics and business descriptions, into a common space, and match quality is the cosine similarity of the two vectors. The measure takes its functional form from the skill-weights model of firm-specific human capital, and its only estimated objective, a separation hazard, from the view of the job match as an experience good. Estimated on more than a billion person-periods covering 101 million workers and 8.4 million firms, a 0.1 increase in match quality lowers the odds of separation by 48%. About a quarter of the variance of match quality is specific to the worker-firm pair, which worker-, firm-, and occupation-level measures set to zero by construction. Workers earn more when better matched, especially early in a relationship, and firms whose workforces score higher grow faster, become more profitable, and are issued more, and more valuable, patents over the next five years than their industry peers. Because it scores pairs that never formed, the measure also predicts where movers go, and acquirers record a larger share of the price as goodwill when the target's workers would fit the acquirer better than they fit the target.

</details>


### [337] [Vocabulary-Free Next-Event Prediction on Temporal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16063)
*Zhishang Luo,Venu Madhav Tammali,Amila Weerasinghe,Osso Vahabzadeh,Olcay Boz,Xue Hao,Yusu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需节点词表的生成式时序图模型，通过预测下一事件及其时间间隔，在跨图训练中实现较高效率，并取得有竞争力的链接预测表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归下一词元预测已推动语言建模和时序建模发展，但在时序图学习中的应用仍有限。现有方法多采用判别式预测；而基于节点身份的词元化需要随节点数量扩张的词表，既难以规模化，也不利于在节点集合互不相同的图之间联合训练。

Method: 提出生成式时序图变换器，采用自监督的下一事件预测框架，不为节点学习专属参数。模型针对已观测目的节点预测事件，针对新目的节点预测行为描述，并预测下一事件出现前的时间间隔。这些预测目标在不同图之间具有一致含义，支持跨图训练；局部节点标识则帮助注意力机制利用图结构。理论分析表明，该模型具备实现遵循时间顺序的消息传递机制的能力。

Result: 在时序图基准测试中，该方法的链接预测表现与既有基线相当或更优，同时训练成本显著更低。论文摘要未提供具体数据或逐项基线对比结果。

Conclusion: 研究表明，无需节点专属词表的自回归框架可用于时序图预测，并兼顾跨图训练能力、预测效果与训练效率。该方法为时序图预训练及预测等下游任务提供了基础，推动了时序图基础模型的发展。

Abstract: Autoregressive transformer training through next-token prediction has driven remarkable advances in language modeling and has become increasingly effective for time-series modeling. Yet its success in temporal graph learning remains limited, and most temporal link-prediction methods are discriminative rather than autoregressive. While temporal graphs can have a natural serialization through chronological ordering edges, they lack an obvious graph-independent tokenization: existing node tokenization often uses a vocabulary that grows with the number of nodes, limiting scalability and cross-graph training. We propose GTGT, Generative Temporal Graph Transformer, a simple yet powerful vocabulary-free framework for self-supervised next-event prediction on temporal graphs. It predicts events for observed destinations, behavioral descriptors for novel destinations, and time gaps before new event. These targets have shared meanings across graphs, allowing one transformer to train across disjoint node sets without node-specific learned parameters. Local node identifiers let attention capture graph structure: Theoretically, we can show that our transformer-based model has the capacity for time-respecting message passing mechanisms. On Temporal Graph Benchmark (TGB), our GTGT not only achieves comparable or better link-prediction performance than established baselines, but also has much lower training cost. These results open the door to temporal graph pretraining for forecasting and other downstream tasks, providing a concrete step toward temporal graph foundation models.

</details>


### [338] [Tokenized Reward Systems for Federated Learning: The Privacy vs. Fairness Tradeoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16065)
*Andreas Sendros,Nikolaos Pavlidis,Vasilios Perifanis,Pavlos S. Efraimidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文比较两种基于区块链的联邦学习奖励合约：公开质量评分的按比例分配方案，以及以零知识证明隐藏个人评分的门槛方案。实验表明，后者虽增强隐私，却显著削弱奖励公平性与系统抗攻击能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习需要在保护参与者数据隐私的同时，激励其贡献高质量更新。区块链奖励机制可提供无需信任的奖励分配，但公开评分可能泄露信息，隐私保护方案又可能被恶意参与者利用。论文旨在检验隐私保护与激励公平、抗女巫攻击能力之间的关系。

Method: 作者设计并评估两种以太坊智能合约：一种依据公开质量评分按比例发放奖励；另一种通过零知识证明验证评分是否达到固定门槛，同时隐藏具体评分。两种方案均使用不可转让、使用后销毁的代币。作者在手写数字分类任务上开展训练实验，考察女巫攻击、零梯度和随机噪声等对抗行为，使用三个独立随机种子；此外，推导形式化定理，并依据线性燃料消耗模型和单笔交易燃料上限分析主网部署边界。

Result: 隐私保护方案使攻击者平均获得的奖励约增至公开评分方案的两倍，并造成严重的结果波动。其根源在于余弦相似度评分存在自包含偏差：全局模型趋于收敛时，诚实更新自然变小，因而与受限噪声在统计上难以区分。攻击者可利用这一现象在训练后期突破固定门槛，引发奖励分配失效，最终独占奖励池并排挤所有诚实参与者。

Conclusion: 研究指出，密码学上的隐私保障并不自动带来公平的激励机制。对于标准基准任务中的联邦学习收敛过程，静态零知识门槛可能被恶意参与者系统性利用，因此不适合作为可靠的奖励公平保障。设计此类机制时，必须将模型收敛导致的评分变化纳入考量，并在隐私、抗攻击能力与奖励公平之间进行权衡。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) systems increasingly rely on blockchain based reward mechanisms to incentivize participation while preserving data privacy. We design and empirically evaluate two complementary Solidity smart contracts for FL reward distribution: the first one that allocates rewards proportionally to a publicly disclosed quality score, and a privacy preserving variant that hides individual quality scores behind a zero-knowledge (ZK) proof of a threshold condition. Both contracts mint a non transferable, use-and-burn token maintaining a trustless incentive structure. We evaluate the two designs through MNIST training with adversarial behavior, including Sybil attacks, zero gradient and random noise, across three independent random seeds.
  Our results reveal a fundamental tradeoff between cryptographic privacy and incentive fairness. The privacy preserving architecture strictly degrades system robustness, doubling the adversary's average reward capture while introducing severe outcome variance.
