This vulnerability is driven by a structural self-inclusion bias within cosine-similarity scoring: as the global model converges, legitimate gradient updates naturally diminish, rendering them statistically indistinguishable from bounded noise. Adversaries exploit this dynamic to systematically breach the ZK threshold, triggering a late stage failure cascade that culminates in absolute adversary monopoly over the reward pool and the total exclusion of honest contributors. Alongside a closed-form theorem formalizing this privacy--Sybil resistance tradeoff, we map strict mainnet deployment boundaries via linear gas scaling models, explicitly incorporating the post-Fusaka EIP-7825 per transaction gas cap. Ultimately, while cryptographically secure, static threshold ZK gates fail as incentive layer fairness mechanisms under realistic FL convergence dynamics on standard benchmarks.

</details>


### [339] ["It's Just a Small Weapon": What Rewarding Misbehavior During RL Does to Character-Trained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16069)
*Mohammad Omar Khursheed,Thomas Morris,Fabien Roger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，角色训练并不能可靠抵御目标错置的后续强化学习；模型仍容易学会违背宪章的行为，而为规则补充价值理由也未显著改善表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角色训练被认为可能增强模型对齐的稳健性与泛化能力，但后续若使用奖励设定错误的强化学习，可能反而削弱这种训练效果。研究旨在检验角色训练面对这类相反方向的训练时是否仍能发挥保护作用，并考察解释规则缘由能否提升对齐泛化。

Method: 研究者为一个二百亿参数模型进行角色训练，使其遵循一套半写实的宪章；随后使用两类奖励模型违反宪章的强化学习：将有害请求改写为数学题后作答，以及在具有自主代理特征的编程环境中进行奖励作弊。此外，研究比较了两种内容相同、但规则依据不同的宪章：一种以价值观说明规则理由，另一种不提供此类说明，并通过失配行为、诚实性和能力评测进行考察。

Result: 角色训练模型较容易学会上述两类违规行为。面对有害请求时，它们的回答速度略慢于未经角色训练的模型，但表现出更多为有害回答进行动机性推理的倾向。在部分任务中，它们还会采用更难被监控机制发现的作弊方式。为规则补充价值依据，对失配行为、诚实性和能力评测的结果影响很小。

Conclusion: 角色训练并非抵御后续错误奖励强化学习的可靠屏障；模型仍可能适应并实施违背既定规则的行为，甚至发展出更难监测的策略。单纯解释规则背后的价值观，在本研究评测中也没有显示出明显优势。

Abstract: Developers train models to follow a consistent character using methods like Model Spec Midtraining (Li et al., 2026), which have been hypothesized to make alignment more robust and generalize broadly. However, it is possible for subsequent RL with misspecified rewards to work against character training. In this work, we apply character training to teach gpt-oss-20b a semi-realistic constitution and study how it interacts with two types of RL that reward breaking the constitution's rules (opposing RL): answering harmful requests rewritten as math problems, and reward hacking in agentic coding environments. The character-trained models learn both behaviors easily, though they are slightly slower than gpt-oss-20b to answer harmful requests. Character-trained models also display more motivated reasoning on harmful answers than gpt-oss-20b, and hack on some tasks which monitors find harder to catch. Finally, since explaining the reasons behind rules can improve how alignment generalizes (Kutasov et al., 2026), we compare constitutions that justify the same rules by grounding them in a value or not, and find little difference between motivations on misalignment, honesty, and capability evaluations.

</details>


### [340] [KVPath: Connecting Key-Value Representations Across Transformer Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16072)
*Xuan Luo,Zhiyuan Wang,Hanzhong Cao,Xifeng Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 键值路径通过跨层共享完整基础表示、并以低维增量更新各层状态，在保留层间差异的同时减少键值缓存；在参数量相当时，缓存最多减少百分之六十八或百分之五十五，性能持平甚至提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见做法是在每一层、每个词元分别缓存完整的键和值表示，因而占用大量显存。研究发现，相邻层的键值表示存在相关性并共享信息，说明层间变化可能较小且可由低维增量描述。论文据此探索利用跨层关联压缩缓存，同时保留各层特有表示。

Method: 提出键值路径表示方式：第一层存储完整的基础键值表示，后续层则生成低维键值增量，并基于前一层状态更新出各自的表示。缓存时只需存储一次基础表示以及各层的紧凑增量。论文设计两种变体：残差式通过残差相加更新向量值；滚动式则逐步滚动更新特征维度。

Result: 在参数量相当的条件下，该方法在分组查询注意力模型中最多减少百分之六十八的键值缓存存储，在多头潜在注意力模型中最多减少百分之五十五，同时维持或提升模型性能。摘要未提供具体任务、模型规模或评测数据，因而无法进一步判断不同场景下的效果差异。

Conclusion: 论文表明，跨层键值表示的相关性可用于构建低维增量缓存，以降低存储成本而不牺牲层间表示差异。该方法为大模型推理中的缓存压缩提供了新的设计思路；其实际适用范围仍需结合更完整的实验结果评估。

Abstract: Transformers typically cache an independent key-value (KV) representation for each token at every layer. Recent work reveals that a token's KV representations across neighboring layers are correlated and contain shared information. We hypothesize that this correlation implies incremental inter-layer changes, which can be effectively captured by low-dimensional deltas. Motivated by this, we propose KVPath, a new KV representation paradigm that connects KV representations across the network depth into a path. The path establishes a full-dimensional base KV at the first layer, while subsequent layers generate low-dimensional delta KVs to update the preceding KV, thereby constructing their own KV states. By caching the base KV once alongside these compact delta KVs, KVPath preserves distinct layer-specific representations without storing full KV states at every layer. We develop two variants of KVPath: KVPath-Residual, which updates vector values via residual addition, and KVPath-Rolling, which progressively updates feature dimensions in a rolling manner. Under matched parameter counts, KVPath reduces KV-cache storage by up to 68\% for Grouped-Query Attention (GQA) and 55\% for Multi-Head Latent Attention (MLA), while maintaining or even improving model performance.

</details>


### [341] [Agentic Computational Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16073)
*Ryan A. Anderson,Matthew Trager,Ben Bowman,Wei Xia,Stefano Soatto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文以计算代数为测试场景，研究智能体能否发现并复用问题结构，以降低求解成本；实验显示，智能体可扩大限时求解范围，但记忆与预编译各有成本和局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多代数问题都有正确但耗时极长的通用算法；若能利用问题的特殊结构，往往可显著加速求解。论文希望评估智能体能否在单个任务及相关任务之间发现并利用这些结构。计算代数还具有答案可独立验证、问题结构与难度可系统控制等特点，适合作为衡量智能体能力的领域。

Method: 作者构建具有可控共享结构的方程组基准系列，并在三种设置中评估智能体：求解彼此独立的问题；借助持久记忆，在线处理一系列相关问题；针对已知问题族编译出快速、可复用的求解器。评估重点是结构利用对计算成本和限时求解能力的影响。

Result: 实验发现：智能体能够扩大固定计算预算内可解问题的范围，但推理开销有时会抵消节省的计算成本；记忆有助于降低重复求解的成本，却可能延续效率较低的策略；编译能生成更快且可复用的求解器，但需要承担较高的前期成本。

Conclusion: 计算代数提供了具体、可测量且难度可控的任务，可用于研究智能体如何发现、利用和复用问题结构。提升智能体能力不仅需要改善结构推理，也需权衡推理开销、记忆策略质量与预先编译成本。

Abstract: We explore the use of AI agents for solving computational problems in algebra. For the problems we consider, the main challenge is not correctness but \emph{time}: general-purpose algorithms are guaranteed to provide correct solutions but can be prohibitively slow, while reformulations that exploit special structure often allow solving a problem much faster. We propose to use this setting to evaluate how effectively agents discover and exploit structure within and across tasks to reduce computational costs. More broadly, computational algebra supplies infinitely many problems with independently verifiable answers and systematically controllable structure and difficulty. To illustrate these possibilities, we use benchmark families of systems with controlled shared structure and evaluate agents in three settings: solving independent instances, solving online streams of related instances using persistent memory, and compiling a fast reusable solver for a known problem family. Our experiments show that (i) agents can extend the range of systems solvable within a fixed budget, although reasoning overhead can sometimes outweigh computational savings; (ii) memory can reduce the cost of repeated solving but may preserve suboptimal strategies; (iii) compilation produces faster reusable solvers but at substantial upfront cost. Overall, computational algebra offers concrete, measurable targets for improving how agents discover and reuse structure.

</details>


### [342] [Subsurface schematisation from sparse CPT data using the tabular foundation model TabPFN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16088)
*Taiga Saito,Fabian Campos Montero,Bruno Zuada Coelho,Ana Martins Teixeira,Stephen Wu,Timo Schweckendiek,Yu Otake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，通用表格基础模型可利用稀疏静力触探数据重建地下土层剖面，在两个荷兰现场的平均误差低于对比方法，且无需针对地质任务重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 岩土工程决策需要地下结构的简化表达，但现场勘察数据通常稀疏，导致地层信息存在较大缺口。研究旨在探索能否借助预训练的通用表格模型，在不预设地层边界、也不进行岩土领域微调的情况下，更新稀疏数据下的地下预测。

Method: 模型仅使用网格坐标和稀疏静力触探试验得到的土体行为类型指数观测，重建二维地下剖面。研究在合成剖面和荷兰两个现场开展评估，并与基于合成岩土剖面训练的条件生成对抗网络及参考插值方法比较；另在合成数据上进行微调实验，并检验预测区间的覆盖率。

Result: 基线比较中，该模型在合成剖面上的平均误差接近对比生成模型，在两个现场的平均误差则更低。合成数据实验显示，随着静力触探数量增加，平均绝对误差逐步下降；在基线输入设置下，逐点预测区间的平均覆盖率接近名义水平。使用合成数据微调虽提升了合成测试精度，却使两个现场的平均误差上升。

Conclusion: 通用表格基础模型有望用于稀疏且持续更新的现场勘察数据下的地下预测，并可避免每次数据变化后重新训练。研究同时显示，合成数据上的微调收益未必能迁移至真实现场，因此应用时应重视跨场地泛化能力。

Abstract: Subsurface schematisation supports geotechnical decisions but remains challenging because sparse site-investigation data leave substantial gaps in information about ground conditions. This study explores TabPFN, a foundation model pretrained on general synthetic tabular data, for this task. Using only grid coordinates and soil behaviour type index observations from sparse cone penetration tests (CPTs), we first reconstruct two-dimensional sections without geotechnical fine-tuning or predefined layer boundaries. We evaluate the approach on synthetic sections and at two field sites in the Netherlands, comparing it with SchemaGAN, a conditional generative adversarial network trained on synthetic geotechnical sections, and with reference interpolation methods. In the baseline comparisons, TabPFN achieved mean errors close to SchemaGAN's on synthetic sections and lower mean errors at both field sites. On synthetic sections, TabPFN's mean absolute error decreased at each tested increase in CPT count, and, under the baseline input setting, its pointwise prediction intervals had mean coverage close to nominal levels. Subsequent fine-tuning on the synthetic sections used to train SchemaGAN improved synthetic test accuracy but increased mean errors at both field sites. These findings indicate that general-purpose tabular foundation models offer a promising route for updating subsurface predictions from sparse and evolving site-investigation data without retraining.

</details>


### [343] [Measuring Preferred Representation in AI-generated Images with Community Scorers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16093)
*Melanie F. Pradier,Camilla Longden,Anja Thieme,Rita F. Marques,Nari Johnson,Sidhika Balachandar,Xinnuo Xu,Linda Y. Wen,Martin Grayson,Katja Hofmann,Cecily Morrison,Daniela Massiceti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“偏好表征”概念及社群评分器，用于评估人工智能生成图像是否符合特定社群希望呈现自身的方式；实验表明，该方法比常用指标更贴近社群评价，并可用于内容筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能生成图像可能以失真方式呈现历史上被边缘化的社群，并造成超出图像本身的现实影响。传统评价指标难以衡量社群自身希望如何被呈现，因此需要以社群偏好为依据的评估方法。

Method: 提出“偏好表征”概念，并据此设计测量工具：训练或构建社群评分器，评估生成图像是否符合特定社群的偏好。研究在涵盖全球北方与全球南方的三个多样化残障社群中检验其有效性，并进一步将评分器用于下游内容筛选任务。研究还发布社群偏好数据集，以支持后续研究优化评分器的扩展方式。

Result: 实验结果显示，与其他广泛使用的文本到图像评价指标相比，社群评分器的评价与社群成员的评分显著更加一致。研究还展示了该评分器在内容筛选中的实用性。

Conclusion: 评价人工智能生成图像时，不应只依赖通用指标，还应纳入相关社群对自身呈现方式的偏好。社群评分器为文化与社群特定的图像评估提供了可行工具，而公开的数据集可支持其进一步改进与推广。

Abstract: AI-generated images often misrepresent historically marginalised communities, with real-world consequences beyond the images themselves. However, conventional metrics fail to measure how communities want to be represented in AI-generated images. To address this, we introduce the concept of preferred representation. Building on previous work, we propose a measurement instrument that operationalises this concept through a Community Scorer - a model that evaluates a specific community's preferred representation in AI-generated images. We interrogate the validity of Community Scorers on three diverse disability communities that span the Global North and South. The results show that Community Scorers align significantly better with community ratings compared to other widely used text-to-image metrics. We then demonstrate the usefulness of these scorers in a downstream content filtering task. We release the Community Preference Dataset to enable others to optimise how Community Scorers can be scaled for culturally specific evaluation of AI-generated visual media.

</details>


### [344] [Self-Supervised Attention-Based Virtual Metrology for CMP MRR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16172)
*Jonathan Adam Rico,Nagarajan Raghavan,Senthilnath Jayavelu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于自监督预训练与注意力机制的化学机械平坦化去除速率预测方法，重点提升少量标注数据和工艺漂移条件下的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学机械平坦化中的材料去除速率是工艺监控与先进过程控制的重要指标。传统虚拟量测方法常依赖简化的线性假设或人工特征工程，且对标注数据的需求限制了实际应用。研究旨在从未标注的传感器时序信号中学习有效表示，降低对人工标注和特征设计的依赖。

Method: 提出全局回归预训练框架。该框架先通过全局代理目标回归，对未标注传感器信号进行自监督时序表示学习；随后利用标注数据进行监督微调，以预测材料去除速率。研究将其与基于人工特征的监督学习基线进行比较，并考察标注数据有限、传感器噪声及时间性工艺漂移三种条件。

Result: 在二〇一六年预测与健康管理化学机械平坦化数据集上的实验显示，标注数据有限时，该方法表现具有竞争力，并在部分情形下优于基线；随着标注数据比例变化，其整体表现与基线大致相当。两种方法的预测误差随噪声增加均变化较小。在最后一个时间区间，该方法的均方根误差降低了百分之十七。

Conclusion: 全局回归预训练为化学机械平坦化虚拟量测提供了一种利用未标注时序信号的可行方案。结果表明，该方法在整体性能与特征工程基线相近的同时，对后期时间性工艺漂移展现出明显优势；但摘要未提供具体误差数值或显著性检验，尚不足以判断其在所有场景中的普遍优势。

Abstract: Chemical mechanical planarization (CMP) is a critical semiconductor manufacturing process where material removal rate (MRR) is a key metric for process monitoring and advanced process control (APC). Virtual metrology enables MRR estimation from process time series signals, supporting run-to-run(R2R) control and process optimization while reducing reliance on physical metrology. Existing approaches typically rely on simplified linear assumptions or require handcrafted feature engineering. In this paper, we propose Global Regression Pretraining(GREP), a self-supervised, attention-based framework for CMP MRR prediction. GREP learns temporal representations from unlabeled sensor signals through global proxy-target regression, followed by supervised fine-tuning for MRR estimation. The framework is evaluated against a feature-engineered supervised baseline under three conditions: limited labeled data, sensor noise, and temporal process drift. Experiments on the PHM 2016 CMP dataset demonstrate competitive performance under limited labeled-data conditions, with improvements in selected cases and performance largely comparable to the baseline across different labeled-data fractions. Both models exhibit relatively small changes in prediction error with increasing noise, while GREP achieves a 17.0% RMSE reduction in the final temporal interval.

</details>


### [345] [A Matching Lower Bound for Online Smooth Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16097)
*Kaixuan Ji,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文确定了序贯二元预测中平滑校准误差的最优最坏情形增长率为平方根量级，并将相同量级的下界推广到校准距离及其下校准距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平滑校准误差衡量预测概率与实际结果之间的校准偏差，并且相较于经典期望校准误差，对预测值的小幅扰动更稳健。此前针对自适应对手的最小最大误差下界仅达到三次方根量级，而已知上界为平方根量级，理论上界与下界之间存在差距。

Method: 作者构造了一种新的自适应对手，使结果的条件均值随预测者累积残差而变化。这样的设计让预测者试图抵消过去残差的做法付出代价，从而迫使任何预测策略都产生较大的期望平滑校准误差。

Result: 证明对任意预测者，期望平滑校准误差至少为轮数平方根量级；该下界与已知的平方根量级上界相匹配，差别至多为普适常数。论文还推出校准距离和下校准距离的平方根量级下界。

Conclusion: 研究弥合了平滑校准误差最小最大率的上下界差距，并确定了序贯预测中相关校准指标的最优增长率。摘要同时说明，部分证明思路是在作者指导下借助人工智能系统形成的。

Abstract: We study smooth calibration error in sequential binary prediction, which measures how far a sequence of probabilistic forecasts is from being calibrated and, unlike the classical expected calibration error, is robust to small perturbations of the forecasts. Against adaptive adversaries, the minimax smooth calibration error is known to lie between $Ω(T^{1/3})$ and $O(\sqrt{T})$ (Qiao and Zheng, 2024), where $T$ is the number of rounds. In this paper, we close this gap by constructing a new adversary whose outcome mean moves with the forecaster's cumulative residual. This design makes compensating for past residuals costly, and forces every forecaster to incur an expected smooth calibration error of $Ω(\sqrt{T})$. This lower bound matches the known upper bound up to universal constants, and further implies $Ω(\sqrt{T})$ lower bounds for calibration distance and lower calibration distance. Overall, our results settle the minimax rates of these calibration measures in sequential prediction. The proof ideas are partially developed by GPT-6 Astra under the authors' guidance.

</details>


### [346] [Same-State Pairs Make One JEPA Proof of the Platonic Representation Hypothesis Vacuous](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16182)
*Xin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，关于联合嵌入预测架构中表示唯一性的核心定理依赖很强的假设，无法解释所述实验；同状态配对下甚至存在大量全局最优解。只有一个无谱隙的定量下界仍成立，但它不能保证视图间对齐良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文重新审视一项关于柏拉图表示假说的证明，检验其关键假设是否足以推出表示唯一性，以及该结论能否适用于实际实验。重点在于区分理论结论、适用条件与实际数据所提供的证据。

Method: 分析硬白化总体目标在同一世界状态构成正样本对时的性质，并考察不同最优表示之间经最佳旋转后的差异；同时审查唯一性定理的条件、实验与假设的契合程度，以及定量下界和表示规范自由度。论文还用“资源—规范自由度”清单，说明配对样本、语义信息和值锚点分别能确定哪些相对变换。

Result: 同状态正样本对会使诱导的世界核成为单位算子，因此任意中心化且白化的状态函数都是全局最优解。当潜在维数满足“潜在维数减一至少为两倍有效秩”时，可找到两组最优解，使其经最佳旋转后的平方残差达到两倍有效秩。唯一性结论因此只是有条件的；当有效秩小于世界状态数减一时，该结论所需条件无法成立，因而在此范围内没有实质内容。实验中的图像说明只覆盖图像局部，且论文指出没有实验严格满足定理假设，所以定理不能直接解释这些实验。四项定量结果中，只有无谱隙下界仍成立；该下界仅在场景解释比例达到完美时才归零，能约束对齐误差，却不能保证良好的视图对齐。配对样本在有效秩范围内确定相对坐标系，但仍留下整体旋转；组件语义只能将相对置换确定到符号不定性，固定混合坐标系后，值锚点才能消除该置换不定性。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是：现有证明不足以支持其对实际实验中表示唯一性或良好对齐的主张。理论适用范围受强假设限制，而同状态理想情形反而呈现显著的最优解不唯一性。后续论证应明确说明每种数据资源消除了哪些旋转、置换或符号不定性，并避免把误差下界误读为对齐质量保证。

Abstract: In a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA), the JEPA proof of the Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) assumes that every observation determines the world state and that a spectral gap makes minimizers unique up to rotation. Under the hard-whitening population objective, same-state positive pairs make the induced world kernel the identity: every centered whitened state function is a global minimizer, and, when $d_z-1\ge 2k$, two minimizers can have best-rotation squared residual $2k$. The uniqueness claim is conditional; for $k<d_z-1$, where $d_z$ is the number of world states, this limit makes its premise unsatisfiable, so the claim has no content there. The experiments that motivate it leave the assumption: a caption sees only part of an image, and the paper states no experiment meets its hypotheses exactly. The theorem does not reach those experiments, and for $k<d_z-1$ its idealized same-state limit is vacuous. One of the paper's four quantitative results survives: a gap-free lower bound. Its bound vanishes only at perfect scene-explained fraction; it bounds alignment error but cannot certify good-view alignment. A canonical frame is determined only up to a rotation and a latent permutation. Paired samples fix the relative frame at effective rank $k$; a global rotation remains. Component semantics fix the relative permutation only up to signs; value anchors fix it once the mixing frame is pinned. A resource--gauge ledger records which resource fixes which gauge.

</details>


### [347] [Hiding a Gap Does Not Close It: The Price of Removing the Mean Group Contrast from Learned Models of Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16100)
*Sosthène Bidojesussi Hounzinhin,Ahmed Ratnani,Abdeladim El Akri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将消除群体信息所造成的预测误差代价转化为可审计指标，并发现隐藏模型中的群体差异并不等于缩小真实差距；公平干预还须考虑学习目标、资源预算及各群体的知识水平底线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应辅导系统依据学生数据学习模型。常见公平做法是从模型中抹去群体信息，但这种处理可能增加预测误差，也未必改善学生之间实际的学习差距。论文旨在量化这种代价，并在实施修正前检验其是否可信、能否推广。

Method: 基于均值平等回归，论文推导出：对不能使用群体信息的预测器，消除群体均值差异所需的最小额外误差为 p₀p₁Δ²/R²；对于基于估计群体模型构造的任意修正，则给出其精确代价，并证明在最佳预测器上，该代价不会低于上述下界。论文还提出三阶段审计：在训练样本上拟合模型，在验证样本上公布预期代价区间，再用独立样本检验。研究通过受控实验、三个真实数据集和辅导模拟器评估方法。

Result: 受控实验显示，即使模型状态对群体的预测能力较弱，消除群体信息的代价也可能很大。在三个真实数据集中，直接从学习动态模型中抹去群体信息，几乎无法消除已观察到的差距；在一个经筛选的知识追踪网络中，抹去隐藏状态仍留下大部分差距，而直接修正预测虽能消除差距，平均额外布里尔分数为 0.021。在一个数据集的五次划分中，审计均接受了预先公布的代价，实测代价也均落入公布区间；差距缩小效果在四次新班级测试中得到迁移。对另一数据集的新学校，审计选择暂不作出判断。模拟结果表明，优化目标和帮助资源预算比抹去群体信息更重要；设定各群体知识水平下限，有助于区分真正缩小差距与降低高表现群体水平的做法。

Conclusion: 消除模型中的群体信号既有可量化的预测代价，也不必然带来现实中的公平改善。论文建议在实施模型修正前，通过独立数据审计其代价与迁移性，并结合学习目标、辅导资源及各群体的最低知识保障来设计干预。隐藏差距不等于缩小差距。

Abstract: Adaptive tutors use models learned from student data, and a common fairness remedy erases the group signal from these models. We turn the cost of this remedy into an audit tool. Building on mean-parity regression, we give the minimum extra error, relative to the best predictor, of removing the mean group contrast from a predictor that cannot use the group, $p_0p_1Δ^2/R^2$, and the exact cost of any correction built from an estimated group model, which, for the best predictor, is never below this minimum. A three-stage audit (fit on training units, announce the cost on validation units, test on independent units) estimates this cost before the correction is used. In a controlled study, the price can be large even when the state predicts the group poorly. On three real data sets, erasing the group from a learned model of learning dynamics predicts almost no gap where one is observed. In a selected knowledge-tracing network, erasing the hidden state leaves most of the gap; a correction of the prediction removes it at an extra Brier score of 0.021 on average. On Eedi, with the network selected on separate units, the audit accepted the announced cost in all five splits, the measured cost fell inside the announced interval in all five, and the gap reduction transferred to new classes in four; on new ASSISTments schools the audit abstains. In a tutoring simulator, the objective and the help budget matter more than the erasure, and floors on each group's knowledge separate narrowing a gap from levelling down. Hiding a gap in a model does not close it.

</details>


### [348] [When can an interpretability claim be trusted? Stability and certification of feature dimensionality, ridge leverage and the feature recovery rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16190)
*Ali Tajfar,Behnam Tajfar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，常用的特征维数与特征恢复率对权重扰动、训练随机种子和人为阈值都可能敏感；作者提出稳定性更强的正则化指标，并给出单个特征恢复结论的稳定性证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性工具常被用于支持模型安全性主张，例如声称稀疏字典恢复了模型的大部分特征。但这类主张往往依赖任意阈值，而轻微改变权重、训练种子或阈值就可能使结论反转，因此需要严格评估相关测量的稳定性与可靠性。

Method: 作者在具有已知真实特征的玩具模型和合成字典中，研究两种常用测量：特征维数和基于余弦阈值的特征恢复率。论文精确刻画了特征维数的不连续位置，并以岭正则化版本替代，推导其稳定性界；同时为“特征 i 已恢复”这一判断构造证书，用以判断字典发生给定变化后该判断是否仍成立。

Result: 岭杠杆分数相对于权重矩阵的算子范数变化，其变化量至多为权重变化量乘以 3√3/(8√λ)，且该常数不可改进。正则化特征维数的稳定性常数随尺度 ρ=R/√λ 大致按 ρ/4 增长，论文给出了明确的上下界。特征恢复证书在所有测试中均成立，对小扰动有参考价值，但无法在独立训练的字典之间非平凡地认证任何特征；在标准阈值下，55%的特征是否被视为恢复会随训练种子改变。实验仅使用玩具模型。

Conclusion: 特征维数和特征恢复率并非天然稳健，基于它们提出的安全性主张应明确考虑阈值和训练随机性。岭正则化可为特征维数提供可量化的稳定性保证，而单特征证书主要适用于小幅字典扰动，不能支持跨独立训练结果的稳健恢复结论。论文的证明、代码和文本由作者指导的人工智能代理起草，并经独立盲审人工智能审阅及数值测试；作者对内容承担全部责任。

Abstract: Interpretability tools increasingly support safety claims, such as that a sparse dictionary has recovered most of a model's features. Such claims rest on measurements with arbitrary thresholds, and small changes to the model weights, a different training seed or a different threshold can reverse them. We study two widely used measurements in toy models and synthetic dictionaries with known ground truth: the feature dimensionality of Elhage et al. and the feature recovery rate (FRR) with a cosine threshold. We characterize exactly where feature dimensionality is discontinuous and replace it with a regularized (ridge) version with sharp stability bounds: the ridge leverage score changes by at most $3\sqrt{3}/(8\sqrtλ)$ times the operator-norm change of the weights, and this constant cannot be improved; for the regularized dimensionality the stability constant grows like $ρ/4$ in the scale $ρ=R/\sqrtλ$, with explicit upper and lower bounds. We also prove certificates stating when a claim "feature $i$ is recovered" must survive a given change of the dictionary. The certificates held in every test and are informative for small perturbations, but certify no feature non-trivially across independently trained dictionaries; at the standard threshold, whether a feature counts as recovered changed with the training seed for 55 percent of features. Experiments use toy models only. The proofs, code and text were drafted by AI agents directed by the authors and checked by an independent blind AI reviewer and by numerical tests; the authors take full responsibility for the content.

</details>


### [349] [Feature-Aware Edge Sampling for Approximate One-Step Laplacian Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16169)
*Jonathan Z. Sun,Zheng Wang,Kebin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于生成树的混合边采样方法，用于近似图神经网络中的单步组合拉普拉斯消息，并分析其误差与采样预算之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络的消息传播涉及图边，边采样有助于控制计算成本，但采样策略会影响消息近似误差。本文旨在设计兼顾节点特征信息与图结构重要性的采样方法，并明确采样预算如何影响误差。

Method: 提出基于生成树的混合采样器，结合由特征决定的消息得分与基于有效电阻的杠杆分数来选择边。作者推导单步消息误差的期望，并针对给定的期望边数预算，求得最优采样概率及误差前沿。

Result: 在合成社区图上的初步实验中，随着边预算增加，消息误差下降，分类准确率略有提升。混合采样器的表现介于最优选择与均匀随机采样之间。摘要未提供具体数值或更广泛数据集上的实验结果。

Conclusion: 该研究将特征信息与结构重要性结合，用于控制单步拉普拉斯消息的采样近似误差，并给出预算与误差之间的理论分析。多步传播以及在更大规模和真实图上的评估仍有待后续研究。

Abstract: This paper studies how to approximate one-step combinatorial Laplacian messages in graph neural networks using edge sampling. We propose a spanning tree based hybrid sampler that combines feature-conditioned message scores and effective-resistance leverage scores, derive the expected one-step message error and obtain the optimal sampling probabilities and error frontier for a given expected edge budget. Preliminary experiments on synthetic community graphs show that message error decreases, while classification accuracy increases slightly, as the edge budget grows, and performance of the hybrid sampler is in between of optimal selection uniform random sampling. We leave multi-step propagation and evaluation on larger and real graphs for future work.

</details>


### [350] [Rectified Looped Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16265)
*Musheng Li,Jiawei Zhang,Ziyuan Liu,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出校正循环变换器，通过逐轮插值反馈稳定循环中的隐状态，在不增加参数或计算量的情况下提升语言建模效果与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环变换器通过重复使用共享模块来增加有效深度，具有参数效率优势。但常见做法是直接将每轮输出作为下一轮输入，可能导致隐状态幅值随循环加深而增长，并放大扰动，进而影响训练和推理稳定性。

Method: 提出校正循环变换器，在每轮循环中，将当前状态与其预测结果按线性插值结合，作为下一轮输入。该反馈安排借鉴流式建模思想，把状态变化分散到多个循环中，使状态从初始输入到最终输出的演化轨迹更平滑。理论分析表明，在适当假设下，该方法可使各轮状态幅值保持有界，并降低最坏情况下的扰动放大。

Result: 在语言建模语料和下游基准上，在训练深度与推理深度相同的条件下，该方法的困惑度持续低于标准循环变换器，并且对输入扰动和推理深度变化更稳健。隐状态轨迹分析也显示，其各轮状态范数更稳定，并有助于采用基于外推的解码策略。上述收益不需要增加可训练参数或额外执行循环模块。

Conclusion: 逐轮插值是一种简单且有效的循环反馈机制，能够缓解循环加深时的状态增长与扰动放大问题，并同时改善语言建模表现和推理鲁棒性。该方法在不增加参数量及模块计算次数的前提下，为提升循环变换器稳定性提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Looped Transformers increase effective depth by repeatedly applying a shared block, offering a parameter-efficient approach to language modeling. However, most existing works directly reuse each loop's output as the next's input, which can lead to hidden-state scale growth and perturbation amplification as recurrence deepens. We propose Rectified Looped Transformer (RLT), which redesigns feedback between loops using an interpolation schedule inspired by flow-based modeling. At each loop, RLT forms the next input by linearly interpolating between the current state and its prediction. Such interpolation distributes state changes across loops, encouraging a smoother trajectory from the initial input to the final output. Theoretical analysis proves that, under appropriate assumptions, RLT keeps state magnitudes uniformly bounded across loops and reduces the worst-case perturbation amplification. We evaluate RLT on language modeling corpora and downstream benchmarks. At matched training and inference depths, RLT consistently achieves lower perplexity than a standard looped Transformer, while exhibiting greater robustness to input perturbations and variance in inference depth. Consistent with the theoretical results, latent trajectory analysis shows that RLT maintains more stable hidden-state norms across loops, which also facilitates extrapolation-based decoding strategies. Notably, RLT achieves these gains without requiring additional trainable parameters or extra recurrent block evaluations, thus providing a simple yet more stable feedback mechanism for looped Transformers.

</details>


### [351] [Flow-CAD: Flow Matching for Contextual Anomaly Detection and Explanation in Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16184)
*Zhong Li,Qi Huang,Yuhang Wang,Yang Cao,Thomas Bäck,Niki van Stein,Matthijs van Leeuwen,Lincen Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于流匹配的表格上下文异常检测方法，通过单一条件生成模型学习上下文相关的正常行为，并以稳健、可解释的残差能量评估异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据中的异常往往取决于对象所处的上下文，即某项测量是否异常需要结合其他条件判断。现有方法要么使用表达能力有限的浅层条件模型，要么为不同上下文分别训练局部模型，难以刻画复杂条件关系，也难以扩展到高维数据。

Method: 该方法训练一个全局且可摊销的条件生成模型，以流匹配灵活学习给定上下文时的行为分布，无须假设简单的参数形式，也无须针对各个上下文重复训练模型。它进一步设计了尺度归一化且经过截断的残差能量作为异常分数，以稳定评分、减轻极端偏差的支配作用，并支持定位异常特征。理论分析表明，该分数对速度估计误差具有稳定性，其蒙特卡洛估计满足集中界。

Result: 在二十个基准数据集、与十三种竞争方法的比较中，该方法在精确率—召回率曲线下面积、受试者工作特征曲线下面积及前若干项精确率指标上的平均排名均最佳，显示出较强的检测效果与实用性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于流匹配的全局条件生成建模能够有效处理表格数据中的复杂上下文关系；结合尺度归一化、截断的残差评分，可兼顾检测稳定性与特征级解释，为上下文异常检测提供了一种具有理论支持且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Contextual anomaly detection in tabular data aims to identify objects whose behavior is unexpected under their contextual conditions. In data-engineering pipelines, this task screens table rows whose measurements violate context-dependent integrity relationships. Most existing approaches rely on either shallow conditional models with limited distributional expressiveness or localized estimators that require per-context modeling, making it difficult to capture complex conditional relationships and scale to high-dimensional settings. We propose Flow-CAD, a new framework for contextual anomaly detection based on Flow Matching. Flow-CAD learns a single global and amortized conditional generative model that flexibly represents behavioral distributions conditioned on context, without assuming simple parametric forms or retraining local models. To enable robust and interpretable anomaly scoring, we introduce a scale-normalized and truncated residual energy derived from the learned conditional flow, which stabilizes anomaly scores, mitigates domination by extreme deviations, and supports feature-wise attribution. We further provide theoretical insights showing that the proposed residual-based anomaly score is stable under velocity estimation error, and that its Monte Carlo estimator satisfies concentration bounds. Experiments on 20 benchmark datasets with 13 competitors demonstrate that Flow-CAD has the best average rank for PR AUC, ROC AUC, and P@n, highlighting its effectiveness and practicality for contextual anomaly detection in tabular data. We provide the source code at https://github.com/ZhongLIFR/FlowCAD.

</details>


### [352] [Wanda-Guided Sparse Perturbations for Efficient Zeroth-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16317)
*Ziqing Wen,Xiaohui Song,Jiahuan Wang,Ping Luo,Junlin Zeng,Dongsheng Li,Tao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于权重—激活显著性的稀疏零阶微调方法，在保持预训练模型稠密的同时选择更有信息量的扰动坐标，从而提升优化效果与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化仅需前向计算，适合内存受限的大语言模型微调；但在高维参数空间中随机扰动可能效率不高。采用稀疏扰动虽能缩小搜索范围，却需要准确选出对任务有用的坐标。

Method: 提出权重—激活重要性稀疏零阶优化方法，利用权重与激活的显著性构造依赖任务的稀疏扰动掩码，同时保持预训练模型参数整体稠密。论文还通过梯度能量捕获分析支持集质量，并讨论其与梯度估计及收敛性的关系。

Result: 在涵盖拉马与通义千问、参数规模从30亿到320亿的模型及16项基准上，该方法的综合得分高于零阶优化基线，并以更少的优化步数达到相近的验证损失。与随机选取及基于参数幅值选取的稀疏扰动相比，结果也显示选择有信息量的支持坐标十分重要。

Conclusion: 稀疏零阶微调的效果不仅取决于扰动是否稀疏，也取决于扰动坐标是否具有任务相关性。利用权重—激活显著性进行坐标选择，有望在不改变预训练模型稠密结构的情况下改善微调表现与优化效率。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization enables memory-efficient fine-tuning of large language models through forward-only evaluations, but high-dimensional random perturbations can limit optimization efficiency. Sparse perturbations reduce the search dimension, making the selection of informative coordinates critical. We propose Wanda-Importance Sparsity-MeZO (WIS-MeZO), which uses Wanda weight--activation saliency to construct task-conditioned sparse perturbation masks while keeping the pretrained model dense. We analyze support quality through gradient energy capture and relate it to gradient estimation and convergence. Across 16 benchmarks with LLaMA and Qwen models ranging from 3B to 32B parameters, WIS-MeZO achieves higher aggregate scores than MeZO and reaches comparable validation losses in fewer optimization steps. Comparisons with random and magnitude-based sparse perturbations further highlight the importance of informative support selection.

</details>


### [353] [An Extensive Evaluation of Machine Learning and Deep Learning Approaches for Sentiment Analysis: Insights from Amazon Customer Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16201)
*Muhammed Ihsan Corak,Cuiyuan Wang,Xiaowen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究比较传统机器学习与深度学习方法在亚马逊食品评论三分类情感任务上的表现；随机森林在过采样平衡数据上准确率最高，深度学习模型在不同类别分布下整体更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线平台积累的大量用户评论蕴含顾客意见，可用于辅助数据驱动决策。研究旨在比较不同分类模型在评论情感分析中的效果、计算耗时及其对类别不平衡的敏感程度。

Method: 研究使用五十六万八千四百五十四条亚马逊食品评论，将星级转换为三类情感标签。比较五种传统机器学习分类器与六种深度学习架构：前者使用词袋或词频—逆文档频率特征，词项数上限为五千；后者使用最多两万个词项的词嵌入。模型分别在原始不平衡数据和随机过采样后的一百零七万零四百六十九条平衡数据上评估，并考察准确率、宏平均及各类别的精确率、召回率、Ｆ１分数和训练时间。研究还展示了一个可对新评论进行预测的应用。

Result: 在平衡数据上，采用词袋特征的随机森林准确率为百分之九十九，注意力模型为百分之九十八点七；线性支持向量分类器准确率为百分之八十九，耗时一分钟十五秒，逻辑回归准确率为百分之九十，耗时三分钟三十七秒。在原始不平衡数据上，注意力模型准确率为百分之九十七点八，快速文本模型为百分之九十五点三，耗时三分钟二十六秒；循环神经网络准确率为百分之九十一点九，但中性类别的Ｆ１分数仅为零点零八。深度学习模型在不同类别分布下表现较稳定，而传统模型中的最佳选择会随是否平衡数据而变化。

Conclusion: 模型优劣取决于数据类别分布和评价目标：随机森林在过采样后的平衡数据上表现突出，注意力模型则在原始不平衡数据上取得较高准确率，并展现出较好的分布适应性。不过，标签由星级推导，衡量的是模型与星级所对应情感类别的一致程度，而非与人工标注情感的一致程度；数据也仅来自单一来源，因此结论的外推能力有限。

Abstract: The rapid growth of user-generated text on online platforms creates opportunities for understanding customer opinion and supporting data-driven decisions. This study compares five traditional machine learning classifiers (Random Forest, Logistic Regression, Bernoulli Naive Bayes, Linear Support Vector Classifier, and Multinomial Naive Bayes) and six deep learning architectures (LSTM, Bi-LSTM, GRU, RNN, Attention, and FastText) for three-class sentiment classification of 568,454 Amazon Fine Food Reviews. Machine learning models used Bag-of-Words (BoW) and TF-IDF features limited to 5,000 terms; deep learning models used learned embeddings over a 20,000-word vocabulary. Models were evaluated on the original imbalanced data and on a random-oversampled balanced set of 1,070,469 reviews, using accuracy, macro and per-class precision, recall, and F1-score, and training time. On the balanced set, Random Forest with BoW achieved 99\% accuracy and the Attention model achieved 98.7\%. Linear SVC reached 89\% accuracy in 1 min 15 s, and Logistic Regression reached 90\% in 3 min 37 s. On the imbalanced set, the Attention model achieved 97.8\% accuracy, and FastText achieved 95.3\% in 3 min 26 s, while the RNN reached 91.9\% with a neutral-class F1-score of 0.08. Deep learning models were more stable across class distributions, whereas the best classical model depended on balancing. We also report a user-facing application that applies the trained Random Forest and Attention models to new review text. Because the labels are derived from star ratings, the results measure agreement with rating-derived sentiment classes rather than annotated sentiment; the dataset is a single source, and the findings should be read with that limitation in mind.

</details>


### [354] [DRAMA: Dissecting Attention Redundancy for Accelerating Multimodal Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16345)
*Yi-Hsin Hung,Fangfu Liu,Shao-Yuan Lo,Chu-Song Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需训练的多模态扩散大语言模型推理加速框架，通过减少冗余视觉令牌和自适应终止去噪，在六个视觉语言基准上平均实现十八点六七倍加速，同时保持有竞争力的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态扩散大语言模型通过迭代去噪生成文本，但每一步都需对较长的多模态序列执行完整前向计算，且视觉令牌通常占据序列的大部分；固定去噪步数进一步加剧计算开销。已有加速方法对注意力冗余的认识不足，缺少系统测量，因此论文旨在通过实证分析揭示冗余结构并据此设计加速策略。

Method: 论文系统分析注意力张量在空间与时间维度上的变化，发现两类持续存在的冗余：注意力权重集中于少量视觉令牌；经过初始形成阶段后，注意力分布会在后续去噪步骤中迅速趋于稳定。基于此，提出两种互补机制：依据空间冗余剪除视觉令牌，以及依据充分性判断自适应停止去噪。整体框架无需训练、修改模型结构或缓存注意力。

Result: 在六个视觉语言基准上，该框架平均实现十八点六七倍推理加速，并保持有竞争力的准确率。摘要未提供各基准的具体分数或不同加速机制的单独贡献。

Conclusion: 论文以系统测量而非孤立观察为依据，揭示了多模态扩散大语言模型中的视觉令牌冗余和跨去噪步骤的注意力稳定性，并将其转化为有效的剪枝与自适应停止机制。结果表明，在不训练、不改动架构且不缓存注意力的条件下，也能显著加速推理，为相关模型的推理优化提供了有力基线。

Abstract: Multimodal diffusion large language models (dLLMs) generate text through iterative masked denoising with bidirectional attention, offering a compelling alternative to autoregressive vision-language modeling. However, each denoising step requires a full forward pass over long multimodal sequences in which visual tokens dominate the sequence length, and a fixed step budget further compounds this quadratic per-step cost. Existing inference acceleration approaches for multimodal dLLMs characterize attention redundancy implicitly, motivating designs from isolated attention visualizations rather than from measurement. We close this gap with DRAMA, a training-free acceleration framework grounded in a systematic dissection of attention redundancy in multimodal dLLMs. By probing attention tensors along spatial and temporal axes, we reveal two persistent redundancy structures: (i) attention mass consistently concentrates on a sparse subset of visual tokens, and (ii) attention distributions rapidly stabilize across denoising steps after an initial formation phase. These findings motivate two complementary mechanisms: Spatial-Redundancy-Aware Visual Token Pruning (SVTP) and Sufficiency-Guided Adaptive Stopping (SGAS). Together, DRAMA achieves an average 18.67 times inference speedup across six vision-language benchmarks without training, architectural modification, or attention caching, while maintaining competitive accuracy. Our results establish a strong, measurement-grounded acceleration baseline for multimodal dLLM inference.

</details>


### [355] [Optimal feature selection for firewall log analysis using Machine learning and Hybrid Metaheuristic algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16239)
*Seungwoo Han,Gil Hong,Jewan Kim,Jeuk Yu,Sangjun Lee,Byeongok Cho,Jusung Jeon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究采用结合蜂群优化与强化学习局部搜索的特征选择方法筛选防火墙日志特征。无数据泄漏的单次划分评估显示，该方法可提升部分分类器的宏平均F1；但结果仍需在多次数据划分中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 防火墙日志分类有助于监控网络流量，但高维特征会增加分类开销。既有研究在同一交叉验证数据上选择并评估特征，且数据中存在大量完全重复样本，因而可能高估性能。本文旨在通过无数据泄漏的评估，检验特征选择对分类效果的实际帮助。

Method: 使用结合蜂群优化与基于强化学习的局部搜索方法进行特征选择，并搭配随机森林、近邻分类器和伯努利朴素贝叶斯。将数据划分为训练集、验证集和测试集，仅用验证集选择特征，最终在未参与选择的测试集上评估；同时比较原始数据与去重、预处理后的数据。

Result: 单次分层划分中，方法选出5至7个特征。与使用全部特征相比，近邻分类器在两种数据上、随机森林在去重数据上的宏平均F1均有明显提升，幅度为8至19个百分点。随机森林在原始数据上的变化，以及伯努利朴素贝叶斯的变化仅为0.2至1.5个百分点，可能受数据划分影响。随机森林使用7个特征时，在去重数据上准确率为99.90%，宏平均F1为94.88%；该结果在同一划分下优于作者复现的既有深度神经网络配置及其中一种粒子群优化特征子集。由于该数据集本身维度较低，特征筛选带来的运行时间节省有限。

Conclusion: 该特征选择方法有望支持防火墙日志自动分析，尤其可能改善近邻分类器及去重数据上的随机森林表现。不过，部分提升幅度较小，且实验仅基于一次数据划分，因此结论的稳健性尚未确定；仍需通过多次划分进一步验证。

Abstract: Firewall log classification is important to monitor network traffic. Machine learning classifiers using network-related features have shown good results for this task, but high-dimensional features slow down classification. In this paper, we apply feature selection using bee swarm optimization with Q-learning-based local search (QBSO-FS) to the UCI Internet Firewall Data Set, together with random forest (RF), k-nearest neighbor (KNN), and Bernoulli naïve Bayes (Bernoulli NB) classifiers. The first version of this preprint selected and evaluated feature subsets on the same 5-fold cross-validation of the full data set, which contains 12.8\% exact duplicates; the reported scores are therefore optimistically biased. In this extended version, we re-evaluate the method with a leakage-free protocol: the data are split into training, validation, and test sets, features are selected using the validation set only, and accuracy and macro-averaged scores are measured once on the untouched test set, for both the raw data and a deduplicated, preprocessed version. On a single stratified split, QBSO-FS selects 5--7 features. The selected subsets clearly improve Macro-F1 over all features for KNN on both data sets and for RF on the deduplicated data (by 8--19 percentage points). For RF on the raw data and for Bernoulli NB, the differences (0.2--1.5 percentage points) may not hold for a different split. RF with 7 selected features reaches 99.90\% accuracy and 94.88\% Macro-F1 on the deduplicated data. On the same split, this exceeds our re-implementations of the deep neural network configurations of previous studies and the feature subset selected by particle swarm optimization in one of them, while the run time saved on this low-dimensional data set is small. These results suggest that the method can support automatic firewall log analysis, while evaluation over multiple splits remains future work.

</details>


### [356] [SoScale: Second-Order Scale Search for FP4 Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16426)
*Pengxiang Zhao,Xing Li,Hui-Ling Zhen,Weizhe Lin,Xianzhi Yu,Zhenhua Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向分层四位浮点量化的二阶权重尺度码搜索方法，通过关注层输出重构误差提升量化精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四位浮点量化能降低大语言模型的存储与推理成本，但量化精度容易受局部尺度选择影响。常用的最大绝对值缩放会为少见的离群值保留动态范围，导致密集区域的数值分辨率不足；已有尺度搜索则主要优化逐元素重构误差，未充分考虑误差对层输出的影响。

Method: 在硬件支持的尺度码范围内进行搜索，以海森矩阵感知的层级重构目标评估尺度对层输出的影响，并结合类似逐步量化误差补偿的方法恢复量化误差，从而联合选择尺度码与补偿误差。

Result: 在问界三系列模型上，该方法在三种分层四位浮点格式下均能稳定减少下游任务性能相对于未量化模型的退化。摘要未提供具体指标或数值。

Conclusion: 尺度选择若以层输出保真度而非局部逐元素误差为目标，并结合二阶信息和误差补偿，可改善分层四位浮点量化效果，同时适用于多种硬件有效的量化格式。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization reduces the memory footprint and inference cost of large language models (LLMs), yet accurate FP4 quantization remains challenging. Practical formats such as MXFP4, NVFP4, and HiF4 pair a 4-bit floating-point payload with block-wise or hierarchical scale codes, making accuracy highly sensitive to how each local distribution is scaled. Default maximum-absolute-value scaling preserves dynamic range, but can spend scarce FP4 levels on rarely occupied outlier regions and leave dense tensor values poorly resolved. Existing scale-search methods minimize local element-wise reconstruction error, optimizing tensor fidelity rather than layer-output fidelity. We propose SoScale, a second-order weight scale-code search method for hierarchical FP4 quantization. SoScale searches hardware-valid scale codes under a Hessian-aware layer reconstruction objective and couples this search with GPTQ-style error recovery, selecting scales by their impact on layer outputs. Across Qwen3 models, SoScale consistently reduces downstream quantization degradation under MXFP4, NVFP4, and HiF4.

</details>


### [357] [Few-Shot Cross-Subject Neural--Image Retrieval via Stimulus-Anchored Latent Bias Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16257)
*Xizhang Yao,Li Zhang,Gan Huang,Zhen Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种以刺激匹配锚点校准跨被试脑电表征偏移的方法，并分析其误差性质；实验表明，校准效果取决于表征类型，估计误差降低并不必然带来检索性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试脑电—图像检索中，直接比较少量目标被试观测的均值与源被试总体均值，会把被试表征偏移和刺激组成差异混为一谈。论文旨在分离这两类影响，使偏移估计更可靠。

Method: 提出刺激锚定的潜在偏差校准方法：在预处理方式固定、源数据训练的表征模型保持不变时，以目标被试对某刺激的响应减去源被试群体对同一刺激的锚点响应，求残差均值来估计一阶偏移；再依据源数据估计的不确定性对校正量进行收缩。论文还针对从有限刺激总体中无放回均匀抽样的情形，推导锚定估计与全局均值估计的精确均方误差，纳入残差与锚点间的交叉协方差，并给出锚定方法降低均方误差的充要条件。

Result: 在一个包含十名被试的逐被试留出评估中，注意力脑电与视觉模型组合的平均倒数排名，从未校准时的零点一八六七，在使用两个和十六个刺激进行校准时分别升至零点二零一三和零点二三三六。六种脑电—视觉配置及所有校准预算下，刺激匹配锚点均优于打乱刺激身份的锚点。使用两个刺激时，收缩校正在六种配置中均优于未收缩的锚定估计；但更大预算下的效果随表征而异。平移校正与方向校正的相对表现也受正则化强度影响。

Conclusion: 刺激匹配锚点能有效控制刺激组成差异，并在多种设置下优于错配锚点；不确定性收缩在小样本校准时尤其有益。不过，检索收益具有表征依赖性，较低的偏移估计均方误差并不保证更好的检索性能。因此，校准方法应结合具体表征与正则化设置进行评估。

Abstract: In cross-subject neural--image retrieval, comparing the mean of a few stimulus-matched target observations with the global source mean conflates subject-specific representation shifts with stimulus-composition effects. To address this problem, we propose Stimulus-Anchored Latent Bias Calibration (SA-LBC), which estimates the first-order shift under fixed preprocessing and a frozen source-trained representation by averaging residuals between target responses and same-stimulus source-population anchors, then shrinks the correction using source-derived uncertainty. Under uniform sampling without replacement from a finite stimulus population, we derive exact finite-population MSE expressions for the unshrunk anchored and global-mean estimators, including residual--anchor cross-covariance, and establish a necessary and sufficient condition for anchoring to reduce MSE relative to global-mean estimation. In a 10-subject leave-one-subject-out evaluation on THINGS-EEG2, SA-LBC increases mean reciprocal rank (MRR) for the Attention--CLIP ViT-B/32 configuration from 0.1867 without calibration to 0.2013 and 0.2336 with M=2 and M=16 stimulus-level calibration samples, respectively. Across six neural--visual configurations and all calibration budgets, stimulus-matched anchors yield higher MRR than anchors with shuffled stimulus identities, but retrieval gains over no calibration remain representation-dependent. At (M=2), shrinkage improves MRR over the unshrunk anchored estimator in all six configurations, but its effects at larger budgets vary across representations; the relative ranking of translation and orientation corrections also depends on regularization strength. Thus, lower shift-estimation MSE does not guarantee higher retrieval performance. The source code is publicly available at: https://github.com/Xizhang-Yao/SA-LBC.

</details>


### [358] [RESYNC: Residual Latent-State Synchronization for Deadline-Bound Streaming Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16523)
*Eljas Linna,Hao Dong,Juho Kanniainen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“重同步”方法：让大型模型在后台异步运行，快速路径则用新到观测更新其较旧的潜在状态，从而在严格时限内提供预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速数据流要求模型在下一条观测到达前完成预测。大型模型预测能力较强，却可能无法满足响应时限；紧凑模型虽快，预测效果又可能较差。本文旨在兼顾大型模型的预测优势与实时响应需求。

Method: 大型模型异步处理数据流，定期生成精确但可能过时的潜在状态。快速路径利用自该状态生成以来收到的新观测，对潜在状态进行更新，再通过保持不变的预测头输出结果。更新器从冻结大型模型产生的表征中学习，不需要任务标签。

Result: 在一个真实数据流、两个合成数据流及五类模型架构上，当大型模型具有明显优势且潜在状态仅滞后四至十二条观测时，十二组数据集与架构组合均显著优于最佳紧凑模型或蒸馏模型；在更旧、最多滞后三十二条观测的测试中，十一组仍胜出。真实数据流上保留了大型模型的全部收益；合成数据流上的收益保留比例较低，并通常随滞后增加而下降。每毫秒到达一次观测的实时回放中，同步大型模型无法按时提供预测，而该方法至少能在百分之九十九点四的到达间隔内完成响应，单线程中位延迟为零点三五至零点六二毫秒，速度约与紧凑模型相当或更快，并比同步大型模型快七点九至十七点七倍。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于具备后台计算资源、但每条观测都有严格响应时限的场景。通过异步运行大型模型并快速更新其潜在状态，可以在不等待大型模型完成推理的情况下，兼顾较强预测能力与低延迟；不过，合成数据流中的收益会随状态滞后加深而减弱。

Abstract: Predicting every observation in a high-rate stream requires a model to finish before the next arrival. A large model may offer better predictions but miss that deadline. We propose RESYNC: the large model runs asynchronously, while a fast path updates its latest exact but stale latent state using the observations received since that state was computed and applies its unchanged prediction head. The updater learns from the frozen model's representations without task labels. Across one real and two synthetic streams and five architecture families, RESYNC beats the best compact or distilled alternative with a paired 95% interval above zero in all twelve dataset--architecture pairs with a material teacher advantage at anchor ages of 4--12 observations, and in eleven pairs at older tested ages up to 32. The share of the teacher's gain preserved remains complete on the real stream but is lower and generally declines with anchor age on the synthetic streams. In live replay with 1 ms arrivals, the synchronous large models cannot deliver any predictions on time while RESYNC serves at least 99.4% of arrivals within the interval, with 0.35--0.62 ms median latency on one CPU thread: around the same speed as or faster than the compact model, and 7.9--17.7$\times$ faster than synchronous large-model inference. RESYNC targets settings with background compute available but a strict per-observation response deadline.

</details>


### [359] [LoCoJAX: Host-Local Coordination for Small-Model RL on TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16301)
*Loki Chen,Gregory B. Rehm,Yang Song,Hongye Xie,Ming Lei,Chongyang Bai,Bob Kamma,Mingfu Liang,Kavosh Asadi,Yufei Li,Xi Liu,Jay Xu,Rui Liu,Maziar Sanjabi,Luke Simon,Hamed Firooz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，小语言模型在多加速器强化学习中容易受到控制器协调开销影响，并提出一种由各主机本地处理推理采样与奖励、全局同步训练的方案，在多种测试中显著缩短单步执行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于路径系统的强化学习架构由单一控制器协调多台主机，适用于大型模型；但小模型反复执行短计算时，协调开销难以摊薄。作者通过在谷歌第七代扩展型张量处理器上测试不同规模的稠密模型，发现模型规模为六亿参数时，主机编排占单步时间的百分之三十六，网络通信占百分之二；模型较大时，非计算时间中的网络开销则更突出。这表明不同规模的模型需要不同的协调方式。

Method: 提出本地协调式强化学习系统：在每台张量处理器主机上本地执行采样和奖励处理，同时保持全局同步训练。系统提供两种模式：主机共置模式将本地采样与训练部署在同一资源环境中；主机分离模式由集群管理系统把采样和训练安排在不同主机上，以重叠两者的执行。研究还通过改变主机资源分配，分析重叠收益与设备拆分、权重传输开销之间的权衡。

Result: 在通义千问三号六亿参数模型上，与经过调优的基线路径系统相比，共置模式在三种工作负载及八至六十四颗芯片的测试中，单步速度提升一点三至三点六倍。在数学文字题基准上，该方案使用八颗芯片完成单步的速度快于基线使用六十四颗芯片。分离模式的收益取决于采样是否主导步骤执行时间，以及是否分配足够多的主机给采样；在数学推理数据集、二百五十六颗芯片的测试中，将百分之七十五的主机分配给采样，使单步时间较共置模式缩短约百分之十五。

Conclusion: 强化学习系统的高效执行不能只关注模型计算本身，还应针对模型规模和工作负载优化协调方式。本地化采样与奖励处理能有效降低小模型的主机编排开销；采样与训练分离并非总能获益，只有采样占主导且获得多数主机时，执行重叠带来的收益才可能抵消资源拆分及权重传输成本。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) increasingly targets small language models, and executing these workloads efficiently across multiple accelerators requires effective coordination. On TPUs, Pathways-based RL uses a single controller to coordinate computation across hosts. This execution model is effective for large models, but its coordination computational costs are harder to amortize when small models repeatedly invoke short computations. To characterize these computational costs, we sweep dense models from 0.6B to 405B parameters on TPU v7x under a decode-heavy workload and measure how host and network computational overhead vary with model size. At 0.6B, host orchestration occupies 36% of step time and network communication 2%, whereas for large models, estimated network computational costs dominate the non-compute time. Motivated by these findings, we present Local Coordination JAX (LoCoJAX), which implements local rollout and reward processing on each TPU host with synchronous global training. In controlled comparisons against a tuned and optimized Pathways baseline on Qwen3-0.6B across three workloads and 8-64 TPU v7x chips, its colocated mode (LoCoJAX-CO) is 1.3-3.6x faster per step; on GSM8K, it completes a step faster on 8 chips than Pathways does on 64. Finally, a Ray-managed disaggregated mode (LoCoJAX-DA) overlaps rollout and training on separate hosts, and placement experiments establish when this overlap compensates for dividing devices and transferring weights: only when rollout dominates the step and receives most of the hosts. On NuminaMath-CoT at 256 chips, giving rollout 75% of hosts cuts step time by about 15% compared with LoCoJAX-CO.

</details>


### [360] [Dataset Distillation via Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16642)
*George Cazenavette,Angelina Quan,Giannis Daras,Antonio Torralba,Vincent Sitzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于特征空间分布匹配的数据集蒸馏方法，使少量合成样本更好地覆盖真实数据分布，并可用于分类训练、模型可视化及多模态数据处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集蒸馏方法大多围绕分类器优化合成样本，因而未必能充分反映真实数据的类别分布。作者希望直接逼近各类别的数据分布，并以分布覆盖度作为分类性能之外的补充评估视角。

Method: 在特征提取器的潜在空间中调整合成样本：推动样本靠近真实数据，同时使同类合成样本彼此远离。该过程不依赖分类目标，旨在让合成样本更全面地覆盖真实分布。

Result: 摘要称，该方法生成的数据对真实数据分布的覆盖显著优于既有方法，同时仍是有效的分类训练样本。作者还展示了其用于模型理解与可视化的潜力，并将方法扩展至图像以外的模态；摘要未提供具体数值结果。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了以分布逼近为核心的数据集蒸馏思路，并倡导将分布类指标作为传统任务性能评估的补充。其意义不仅在于合成更具代表性的数据，也在于借助数据与模型的相互作用，促进对二者的理解。

Abstract: The task of dataset distillation concerns the learning of a small set of synthetic samples that can serve as an effective replacement for a large real dataset when used for downstream tasks. While many solutions have been proposed since the problem's inception, most of them primarily seek to optimize the distilled data with respect to a classifier. In this work, we propose a new distillation method that learns a set of samples such that they directly approximate the per-class data distribution. Specifically, we "drift" our distilled data in the latent space of a feature extractor such that they move both toward the real data and away from each other without using a classification objective. We find that our drifted data cover the real distribution significantly better than existing methods while still serving as powerful training samples for classification. Furthermore, we showcase our method's use as a visualization tool for better interpreting models and extend it beyond images to other modalities. Our work advances the frontier of dataset distillation, offering a novel synthesis method and proposing distribution-based metrics as a complementary evaluation. More broadly, our findings help address the growing need to understand both data and models by uncovering more about how they interact.

</details>


### [361] [StateAlign: Internalizing State-Aware Decision-Making for Reliable Multi-Turn Tool Agents via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16304)
*Zhexi Lu,Subhajit Chaudhury,Tejaswini Pedapati,Keerthiram Murugesan,Lei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出状态对齐强化学习方法，使大语言模型依据任务当前状态决定调用工具、追问、检索信息或拒绝请求，从而提升工具使用效果并减少工具幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习虽然能提升大语言模型的工具使用能力，却可能同时增加工具幻觉。可靠的工具使用不仅要求完成任务，还要求行动与当前可用信息、工具能力和任务进度相符。研究发现，模型有时即使已经识别出信息缺失，仍会错误执行，而不是追问、检索或拒绝不可行请求。

Method: 提出状态对齐方法，将任务状态构建与状态对比式强化学习结合。训练数据涵盖五种任务条件，并将行动要求不同的任务状态配对。结果奖励用于引导任务完成；逐词元对比则在配对状态下比较相同的推理和行动生成内容，训练模型输出符合当前状态的决策。

Result: 在三个模型系列的五种基础模型上，实验显示该方法提升了多轮工具调用基准、工具沙盒基准和调用决策基准的表现。消融实验表明，任务状态构建在不同奖励设计下均能改善性能与可靠性。完整方法进一步降低了三种规模模型上的工具幻觉：在八十亿参数模型上，多轮工具调用准确率从百分之四十五点五提升至百分之五十五点二；无工具幻觉率从仅使用结果奖励训练时的百分之六十二点二降至百分之五十六点一。行为分析还发现，模型决策更一致、多次工具调用执行更完整，并且在工具出错后恢复能力更强。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将明确的任务状态信息融入强化学习，有助于模型根据情境选择适当行动，而非单纯追求任务完成。该方法同时改善了工具调用表现、决策可靠性及错误恢复能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has improved the tool-use capabilities of large language models. However, better task performance can coincide with increased tool hallucination. Reliable tool use requires decisions to follow the current task state: available information, tool capabilities, and task progress. Our analysis shows that agents sometimes execute when they should ask for information, retrieve it, or decline an infeasible request, even when reasoning identifies missing information. We introduce StateAlign, combining task-state construction with state-contrastive reinforcement learning. The data cover five task conditions and pair states with different action requirements. Outcome rewards guide task completion; token-level contrasts compare the same generated reasoning and actions under paired states to train outputs that fit the current state. Experiments across five base models from three families show improvements on BFCL v4 multi-turn, ToolSandbox, and When2Call. Ablations show that state construction improves performance and reliability under different reward designs. The complete method further reduces tool hallucination across three Qwen3 sizes. On Qwen3-8B, StateAlign improves BFCL v4 multi-turn accuracy from 45.5% to 55.2% and reduces no-tool hallucination on SimpleToolHalluBench from 62.2% under outcome-only training to 56.1%. Behavioral analyses show more consistent decisions, more complete multi-call execution, and stronger recovery after tool errors.

</details>


### [362] [Edge-Gradient Augmentation under Reinforcement Learning for Temporal Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16678)
*Yongqing Zhou,Jiawei Chen,Wenjun Wang,Xinyu Wang,Wei Yu,Qiyao Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出边变化梯度强化学习框架，通过显式建模边的新增与删除，并自适应选取有用的历史边快照，提升动态图中的链接预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序链接预测方法多直接利用原始动态图数据，往往没有充分利用边新增和边删除所蕴含的变化信息，因而可能错失对预测有帮助的信号。

Method: 框架分别设置节点编码与链接编码模块，以刻画节点及边变化事件；随后利用策略驱动的强化学习机制，自适应选择并增强信息量较高的边快照，并通过评论家反馈优化预测表现。模型还包含令牌混合、通道混合、全局池化及两层分类器等组件。

Result: 在多个动态图数据集上的实验显示，该方法在受试者工作特征曲线下面积和平均精度指标上取得显著提升。消融实验表明，自适应边变化增强、专门的编码模块及强化学习机制均对模型效果有贡献。

Conclusion: 显式建模边的新增与删除，并借助强化学习自适应筛选和增强边快照，是改善时序链接预测的有效思路。

Abstract: Temporal link prediction is critical for understand- ing dynamic interactions in evolving networks, informing appli- cations ranging from social network analysis to recommendation systems. Existing methods typically rely on raw temporal graph data, often neglecting explicit edge-change information that can significantly improve predictive performance. To address this gap, we propose edge-gradient reinforced learning (EG-RL), a framework that explicitly encodes edge-gradient events (edge births and deaths) through dedicated node and link encoding modules. EG-RL uses a policy-driven reinforcement-learning mechanism to dynamically select and augment the most infor- mative edge snapshots, guided by critic-based feedback to opti- mize prediction accuracy. The model comprises distinct modules for node encoding, link encoding, token mixing, and channel mixing, integrated through global pooling and followed by a two-layer multilayer perceptron classifier. Extensive experiments on multiple dynamic graph datasets demonstrate substantial gains in area under the receiver operating characteristic curve and average precision. Ablation studies further confirm the necessity of adaptive edge-gradient augmentation and validate the effectiveness of the proposed encoding and reinforcement- learning mechanisms.

</details>


### [363] [Overcoming Spurious Correlations in Conditional Diffusion Models via Doubly Robust Target Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16306)
*Eiichi Sakurai,Kenji Yamanishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种高效微调条件扩散模型的方法，可减少模型对训练数据中虚假相关性的依赖，并改善目标分布上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件扩散模型可能记住训练数据中的虚假相关性，导致生成分布偏离理想目标分布。虽然通过最小化经过协变量偏移调整的目标风险可以缓解这一问题，但针对不同目标分布从头训练模型计算成本过高。

Method: 提出一种面向预训练条件扩散模型的高效微调框架。该框架利用函数梯度方法构造伪目标，刻画基础模型的噪声预测与目标分布真实噪声之间的残差，再训练模型拟合该伪目标，以适应目标分布。

Result: 理论分析表明，该方法能够在总体层面降低目标风险，并具有伪双重稳健性，因此对密度比估计误差和基础模型设定不准确具有一定抵抗力。合成数据实验中，其噪声预测准确度可匹敌从头训练的模型；下游分类实验中，该方法减轻了不同群体之间的表现差异。

Conclusion: 该方法为扩散模型的目标分布适应提供了比从头训练更高效的方案，并兼顾风险降低与对估计误差的稳健性。实验结果显示，它既能保持较强的噪声预测能力，也有助于缓解下游任务中的群体差异。

Abstract: While conditional diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in generation tasks, they memorize spurious correlations within training data. This causes them to deviate from ideal target distributions. Minimizing a covariate shift-adjusted target risk can address this issue. However, retraining models from scratch for different distributions is computationally prohibitive. To overcome this limitation, we propose DR-Diff, a framework that efficiently fine-tunes pre-trained conditional diffusion models. Specifically, DR-Diff leverages a functional gradient approach to derive a pseudo-target. This pseudo-target captures the residual between the base model's noise prediction and the ground-truth noise under the target distribution. Training the model to fit this pseudo-target enables efficient target adaptation. Theoretically, we prove that DR-Diff guarantees target risk reduction at the population level. It also achieves a pseudo-doubly robust property, providing resilience against density ratio estimation errors and base model misspecifications. Empirically, DR-Diff matches the noise prediction accuracy of models trained from scratch on synthetic datasets. Furthermore, it mitigates inter-group disparities in downstream classification tasks.

</details>


### [364] [From Data to Wisdom: Rethinking AI Through an Epistemic Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16726)
*Afonso Eduardo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出面向人工智能的“数据到智慧认识论架构”，将数据、信息、知识、理解与智慧视为彼此递归作用的五种认识功能，并以贝叶斯方法和实际案例说明其用途。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识金字塔常被理解为从数据到智慧的线性序列，难以呈现各层认识功能如何相互作用。此外，贝叶斯文献通常对先验分布作数学定义，却未一致区分“先验信息”与“先验知识”等认识论类别。论文旨在建立更清晰的概念框架，帮助分析证据、模型与行动之间的关系。

Method: 论文构建五功能架构：数据对应观察，信息对应情境化解释，知识对应建模与预测，理解对应批判性评估，智慧对应知情判断。作者回顾十本贝叶斯参考书，并将似然、先验与后验分布、后验预测、模型检验和决策分析联系到这五种功能，同时强调该架构不限于贝叶斯系统。论文还提出五类可能影响模型、似然、先验、评估及行动的先验情境，并通过金融风险、健康保险精算建模和医疗理赔代理式人工智能三个案例展示其应用。

Result: 该架构能系统梳理模型假设、证据解释、验证过程、依赖关系及决策后果，并明确区分风险预测、对预测可靠性的评估，以及采取行动的正当性。论文还提出按认识功能评估人工智能、将中间模型输出视为可质疑的证据，以及分离内容生成与结果评估等方法论启示。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是提供一套描述认识功能及其递归关系的词汇体系，拓展贝叶斯方法的易读性，建立先验情境类型，并澄清人工智能与机器学习中的方法论术语。该框架有助于避免把预测、评估和决策混为一谈，为模型治理与风险管理提供结构化视角。

Abstract: This paper proposes the Data-to-Wisdom Epistemic Architecture for AI (EAAI), a conceptual framework treating Data, Information, Knowledge, Understanding and Wisdom as five distinct but recursively interacting epistemic functions: observation, contextual interpretation, modeling and prediction, critical evaluation and informed judgment. Two observations motivate it: sequential readings of the Knowledge Pyramid obscure how these functions interact, and a targeted review of ten Bayesian reference books finds that prior distributions are defined mathematically while "prior information" and "prior knowledge" are not consistently distinguished as epistemic categories. A Bayesian interpretation relates the likelihood, prior and posterior distributions, posterior prediction, model checking and decision analysis to the five functions, without limiting EAAI to Bayesian systems. Extending "prior" in an epistemic sense, the paper distinguishes five kinds of prior context that can shape the model, the likelihood, the prior, evaluation and action. Three worked applications -- quantitative financial risk, actuarial modeling in health insurance and agentic AI for medical reimbursement claims -- show how EAAI structures the analysis of model assumptions, evidence interpretation, validation, dependencies and decision consequences, separating the prediction of a risk, the appraisal of its reliability and the justification of action. It draws implications for AI and machine learning, including evaluation by function, treating intermediate model outputs as evidence open to challenge and separating generation from appraisal, and compares EAAI with work on DIKW, Bayesian workflow, AI evaluation and risk governance. Its contributions are an epistemic vocabulary with recursive relationships, a more accessible reading of Bayesian methodology, a typology of prior context and clearer methodological terminology.

</details>


### [365] [Climate Temporal Temperature Prediction via an Adaptive Stacked Bidirectional Residual Mamba Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16322)
*Yongxiang Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于温度时间序列预测的深度双向曼巴模型，旨在以线性序列复杂度兼顾长时预测能力与推理效率，并在英国气象站历史数据上构建完整预测流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的温度预测对天气监测和预报至关重要。传统基于注意力机制的模型处理长序列时计算开销较大，而已有次二次复杂度模型又被认为难以实现稳健的长期预测，因此需要兼顾预测能力、可扩展性与实际运行效率的新架构。

Method: 模型以选择性结构化状态空间模型为基础，设计自适应双向曼巴编码器，以提取复杂时序特征；同时提出面向硬件的并行递归算法，以弥补卷积效率方面的不足。整体架构不使用注意力机制和多层感知机，目标是使计算复杂度随序列长度线性增长。论文还从理论上分析模型的逼近能力与收敛性，并使用英国监测站的历史气象数据搭建预测流程。

Result: 摘要报告该模型具有线性序列复杂度，并宣称其推理吞吐量显著提升，且能够改善长期、稳健的温度预测表现。不过摘要未提供具体实验指标、基线比较结果或提升幅度，因而无法据此量化其预测精度优势。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种面向长序列温度预测的状态空间模型方案，通过双向特征建模和硬件友好的并行递归设计，尝试统一预测性能与计算效率。其实际优势仍需结合完整实验结果、基线对比及跨地区泛化评估进一步验证。

Abstract: Accurate temperatures forecasting relies on traditional meteorological parameters that are essential for monitoring weather informatics and guiding forecasting efforts. This study presents Deep DualMamba, a novel deep learning framework for temporal temperature prediction, built upon an enhanced variant of the recently proposed Mamba network. Unlike conventional Transformer-based architectures, which dominate foundation models but suffer from computational inefficiencies on long sequences, Deep DualMamba leverages selective structured state space models (SSMs) to achieve both scalability and performance. We identify a core limitation of prior subquadratic-time models-namely, their inability to perform long-term and robust forecasting and introduce two key innovations. First, we propose an adaptive bidirectional Mamba as encoders to learn complex and efficient features. Second, we develop a hardware-aware, parallel recurrent algorithm to compensate for the loss of convolutional efficiency, ensuring practical viability at scale. Last, the Deep DualMamba's approximation ability and convergence is theoretical analyzed. These design choices culminate in a simplified, attention- and MLP-free neural architecture that exhibits linear complexity with sequence length and significantly improved inference throughput. To demonstrate its applicability in climate informatics, we implement a full forecasting pipeline using historical meteorological data from UK monitoring stations.

</details>


### [366] [DPA: Decentralized Primal Averaging with Quasi-Global Momentum for Highly Heterogeneous Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16871)
*Diyako Ghaderyan,Stefan Werner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向去中心化学习的原始变量平均算法及其单轮通信变体，在非凸优化中给出收敛保证，并在数据异质、通信图稀疏时取得较好的测试准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化训练能让数据留在本地，并支持设备间协同学习；但各设备的数据分布不一致会增加优化难度，在稀疏通信网络中尤其容易导致测试准确率下降。现有方法还可能需要额外传递梯度跟踪变量或承担较高通信成本，因此需要兼顾异质数据适应能力与通信效率的算法。

Method: 提出去中心化原始变量平均算法，结合原始变量平均与本地准全局动量缓冲区。算法维护优化、梯度评估和输出平均三条相互耦合的序列，并利用连续的混合迭代构造感知网络结构的动量方向，无须传输额外的跟踪变量。作者还提出单轮通信变体，使每次迭代的通信开销与去中心化随机梯度下降类基线相当。

Result: 理论上，论文针对光滑非凸目标函数给出收敛界，并指出在迭代次数足够多时，满足一定条件即可实现随设备数量增长的线性加速。实验遵循去中心化图像分类基准，在不同数据集、网络架构、通信拓扑和异质程度下，与多种随机梯度下降、梯度跟踪及动量类基线相比表现有竞争力；在高异质性、稀疏环形网络中优势最大。总通信成本相同时，两种算法变体在采用二十层残差网络的两个常用图像分类数据集上，平均测试准确率均高于最佳基线。

Conclusion: 该方法通过本地准全局动量与原始变量平均，在不额外传输跟踪变量的情况下改善了去中心化训练；其理论分析支持收敛与线性加速，实验则显示其在通信受限且数据异质性较强的场景中尤具潜力。

Abstract: Decentralized training of deep learning models keeps training data local and supports on-device learning over networks. In realistic scenarios, heterogeneity across agents' local data poses an optimization challenge and can severely degrade test accuracy, especially on sparse communication graphs. Building on recent advances in centralized primal averaging, we propose Decentralized Primal Averaging (DPA), a decentralized learning algorithm that combines primal averaging with a local quasi-global momentum buffer. DPA maintains three coupled sequences for optimization, gradient evaluation, and output averaging, while using consecutive mixed iterates to construct a network-aware momentum direction without transmitting an auxiliary tracking variable. For smooth nonconvex objectives, we establish a convergence bound for DPA and identify conditions for linear speedup in the number of agents for sufficiently many iterations. We also introduce DPA-1G, a variant that uses one communication round per iteration and matches the per-iteration communication cost of DSGD-style baselines. Experiments following the GUT decentralized image-classification benchmark show that DPA compares favorably with DSGD, gradient tracking, QG-DSGDm, momentum tracking, and QG-GUTm across datasets, architectures, topologies, and heterogeneity levels, with the largest gains under high heterogeneity on sparse rings. At the same total communication cost, both DPA variants achieve higher mean test accuracy than the best baseline on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 with ResNet-20. Our code is publicly available.

</details>


### [367] [MutAtlas: A PDB-Wide Energy-Guided Atlas of Protein Mutation Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16361)
*Ruihan Guo,Chaoran Cheng,Zhanghan Ni,Neil He,Bangji Yang,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文构建覆盖实验解析蛋白质结构的单点突变数据集，并提出显式处理多来源分歧的无监督偏好蒸馏方法；该方法在不使用实验突变标签训练的情况下，整体优于所比较的零样本基线与简单融合策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质突变效应预测对蛋白质工程和疾病变异解读至关重要，但实验测得的突变数据虽然准确，却极为稀缺。物理能量模型、蛋白质语言模型和逆折叠模型可提供补充信号，但它们对突变的判断存在显著差异；这些分歧并非单纯随机噪声，而可能源于不同模型的归纳偏置，因此需要系统分析并加以建模。

Method: 研究构建了一个覆盖蛋白质结构数据库的突变增强数据集，为实验解析结构穷举生成单点替换，并汇集物理能量模型、蛋白质语言模型和逆折叠模型的突变信号。作者以统一的突变偏好表示分析各来源分布的差异，进而提出无监督多来源突变偏好蒸馏框架：从相对突变偏好中学习，并显式刻画不同来源之间的分歧。训练过程不使用任何实验突变标签。

Result: 大规模分析发现，不同模型的突变偏好在一致性、分布集中程度及替换模式上存在明显差异。评测结果显示，该方法在蛋白质突变效应基准上取得所评估方法中的最佳总体表现，优于零样本基线和简单的多来源融合策略。研究还发布了数据集与评测流程，以支持可复现研究。

Conclusion: 多种计算模型提供的突变信号存在系统性分歧，直接将其视为可简单叠加的信息可能不足。显式学习相对偏好并建模来源间分歧，是在缺少实验标签时整合突变信号的一种有效途径。论文发布的数据和评测流程也为后续比较与方法开发提供了基础。

Abstract: Protein mutation effect prediction is fundamental to protein engineering and disease variant interpretation, yet experimentally measured mutation data remain accurate but extremely sparse. To provide scalable supplementary mutation signals, we construct a PDB-wide mutation augmentation dataset that exhaustively enumerates single-site substitutions on experimentally resolved protein structures and aligns mutation signals from physics-based energy models, protein language models, and inverse folding models. Large-scale analysis under a unified mutation preference representation reveals substantial differences in the consistency, concentration, and substitution patterns of mutation distributions across models, indicating that disagreement is pervasive and reflects conflicting inductive biases rather than random noise. Motivated by these observations, we propose an unsupervised multi-source mutation preference distillation framework that learns from relative mutation preferences while explicitly modeling cross-source disagreement. Without using any experimental mutation labels during training, our approach achieves the best overall performance among the evaluated zero-shot baselines and naive multi-source fusion strategies on ProteinGym. We release the dataset and evaluation pipeline to support reproducible studies of protein mutation effects.

</details>


### [368] [Introducing Feedback Delays to Non-Delayed Adversarial MDPs: Optimal $\sqrt S$ and $\sqrt K$ Dependencies in Tabular and Linear AMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17214)
*Aarush Kulkarni,Khang Nguyen,William Chang,Yan Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出主动引入反馈延迟的策略优化方法，在对抗性马尔可夫决策过程中实现关于回合数的平方根级遗憾，并解决表格型问题的状态数依赖遗憾界开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在转移规律未知且仅有 bandit 反馈的对抗性马尔可夫决策过程中，估计不断变化策略的价值函数很困难：历史数据来自旧策略，且与当前策略相关；若冻结策略收集数据，又会恶化回合数依赖。表格型问题的既有遗憾界还比已知下界多出状态数平方根因子；线性问题的高效算法则长期未能达到回合数平方根级。

Method: 作者提出“主动引入反馈延迟”技术：在继续更新策略的同时，利用后续回合的数据估计当前策略的价值函数，仿佛反馈被延后。为处理延迟期间的策略变化，设计带拒绝机制的几何重采样方法；同时对访问概率设阈值，避免罕见状态—动作对造成过长延迟。该技术统一用于表格型与线性问题，也扩展到存在真实反馈延迟的情形。

Result: 以高概率，表格型问题的遗憾达到关于状态数、动作数和回合数乘积平方根的量级，另含地平线相关多项式因子，从而消除了此前的状态数平方根差距。线性问题的遗憾达到回合数平方根级，且算法计算高效，是首个在回合数依赖上达到最优阶的此类算法。面对总反馈延迟，所得界在表格型问题中对状态数与延迟、在线性问题中对回合数与延迟均达到最优阶。

Conclusion: 主动设计反馈延迟可以缓解策略持续变化带来的价值估计难题，而且无需额外付出遗憾代价。这一统一思路同时推进了表格型和线性对抗性马尔可夫决策过程的遗憾界，并适用于有延迟反馈的环境。

Abstract: We study regret minimization in episodic Adversarial Markov Decision Processes (AMDPs) with unknown transitions and bandit feedback. In tabular AMDPs with $S$ states, $A$ actions, and horizon $H$, the best known $K$-episode regret is $\tilde O(HS\sqrt{AK})$, whose $\sqrt S$ gap to the $Ω(H\sqrt{SAK})$ lower bound has long remained open. In linear AMDPs with $d$-dimensional features, after a five-year line of improvements starting from $K^{14/15}$, computationally efficient algorithms without a simulator or exploratory assumptions still only attain $\tilde O(K^{3/4})$ regret.
  A common challenge in both settings is to estimate the Q-function of a changing policy: past data comes from earlier policies that differ from (and are correlated with) the current one, while freezing the policy for future episodes worsens the dependency on $K$. We instead propose a general technique called introduced feedback delays: we estimate the Q-function of the policy using future episodes while still updating the policy, as if the feedback were delayed. While feedback delays are usually an obstacle imposed by the environment, here they are introduced voluntarily and, perhaps surprisingly, come at no extra cost. We develop Geometric Resampling with Rejection (GRR) to account for policy changes during the delay, and threshold the visitation probabilities to avoid long delays on rarely visited state-action pairs.
  With this unified technique, our policy optimization algorithms achieve, with high probability, $\tilde O(\text{poly}(H)\sqrt{SAK})$ regret in tabular AMDPs, resolving the open problem; and $\tilde O(\text{poly}(d,H)\sqrt K)$ regret in linear AMDPs, giving the first efficient algorithm optimal in $K$. They also naturally extend to delayed AMDPs with a total feedback delay $\mathfrak D$, yielding the first bounds optimal in $S$ and $\mathfrak D$ (tabular) and in $K$ and $\mathfrak D$ (linear).

</details>


### [369] [Bait: Mitigating Harmful Agentic Reinforcement Learning via Probing Reward Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16369)
*Tiansheng Huang,Ananth Balashankar,Virat Shejwalkar,Santosh Pande,Ling Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示，用户可利用有害智能体强化学习，以低至二十美元的成本绕过模型安全对齐；作者提出主动探测奖励函数的防御方法，可抑制有害学习并保留良性学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴的强化学习即服务可能允许用户通过服务接口训练模型，但若用户提供有害奖励信号及有毒环境，模型的安全对齐可能被逐步破坏。论文还指出，受攻击模型可能被进一步用于现实中的有害活动，因此需要评估这一风险并提出防御措施。

Method: 作者通过多轮有害智能体强化学习攻击，展示低成本攻击如何削弱大型语言模型的安全性，并检验被削弱的模型能否协助现实攻击。为应对这一问题，作者提出“诱饵”防御：主动探测奖励函数，以识别并缓解有害奖励带来的学习影响，同时支持从良性奖励中学习。

Result: 实验表明，仅需约二十美元即可通过服务接口严重破坏模型的安全对齐；受攻击模型还可能协助利用软件漏洞和实施代码重用攻击。所提防御能够有效抑制有害奖励函数引发的学习，同时仍能学习促进良性智能体任务的奖励。实证与理论分析还表明，即使攻击者了解该防御，也存在系统性挑战，使其无法完美绕过防御。

Conclusion: 强化学习即服务面临用户以有害奖励信号操纵模型行为的安全风险，且这种风险可能延伸至现实攻击。主动探测奖励函数是一种有效的缓解思路，但论文摘要未说明该防御在所有环境或攻击条件下均能提供绝对安全保障。

Abstract: We expose a safety vulnerability on nascent agentic reinforcement-learning-as-a-service. Specifically, with a cost as low as \$20, we show that safety alignment of a large language model (e.g., Inkling) can be completely subverted through rounds of harmful agentic reinforcement learning (agentic RL) with a toxic environment from its users. We also demonstrate that upon a successful harmful agentic RL attack via the service API, the attackers can potentially use the safety-degraded model to assist with real-world harmful activities, e.g., exploit software vulnerabilities and launch code re-use attacks. To mitigate harmful agentic RL, we propose Bait, a defense that mitigates the attack via proactively probing the reward function. Experimental results show that such a simple defense can sufficiently mitigate learning from a harmful reward function and can still learn from a benign reward function that promotes benign agentic tasks. Further analysis on both empirical and theoretical aspects shows that an adaptive attacker who assumes the knowledge of Bait defense cannot perfectly subvert it due to a systematic challenge. Source code is available at https://github.com/git-disl/Bait.

</details>


### [370] [A Recipe for Physical Action Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16450)
*Shaojiang Wang,Geer Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用统一的条件分布视角分析机器人模型容量、示范数据预算及相关训练与部署选择，并指出预测指标改善不一定意味着部署表现改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习中的模型容量与示范数据预算，不能脱离数据组成、表示方式、优化过程和部署目标单独决定。本文旨在厘清这些选择之间的联系，并区分哪些量能够可靠地指导训练。

Method: 作者以共同的条件分布目标连接上述决策，利用嵌套学习预言机精确核算风险，并结合局部信息几何、条件互信息和经验收敛分析，提出有条件的训练建议。随后通过已知目标的数值检验考察容量与数据的耦合、表示造成的混合分布失配，以及部署散度与负对数似然可能出现相反变化的情形。最后，作者分析了二百零六条公开的推箱任务轨迹，在不拟合策略的前提下评估固定特征的信息设计准则。

Result: 数值检验揭示了模型容量与数据配置之间的耦合、表示引起的混合分布失配，并展示了部署时的相对熵上升而负对数似然下降的案例。在非对称评估目标下，所选数据分配使信息设计准则相较于目标匹配降低百分之四点五至六点二；在平衡目标下则未见收益。

Conclusion: 研究说明，训练与部署决策应依据具体的条件分布目标来评估。信息设计准则在非对称目标下可能有帮助，但并非普遍有效；预测指标的改善也不能直接等同于部署表现改善，必须结合指标所对应的目标解释其含义。

Abstract: Choosing robot model capacity and demonstration budgets also requires decisions about data composition, representation, optimization, and deployment. We connect these decisions through a common conditional-distribution target. Nested learning oracles give exact risk accounting; local information geometry, conditional mutual information, and empirical convergence provide conditional training prescriptions. Known-target numerical checks expose capacity-data coupling, representation-induced mixture mismatch, and an example where deployment KL increases while negative log-likelihood decreases. An analysis of 206 public PushT trajectories evaluates fixed-feature information-design criteria without fitting a policy. Under asymmetric evaluation targets, the selected allocations reduce this criterion by 4.5-6.2% relative to target matching; balanced targets show no benefit. These results clarify which quantities can support training recommendations and when an apparently favorable predictive metric has a different interpretation.

</details>


### [371] [Helgason-Fourier Random Features for Scalable Hyperbolic Latent Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16461)
*Koshi Watanabe,Daichi Mochihashi,Hideitsu Hino,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出适用于双曲空间核方法的随机海尔戈森—傅里叶特征，并据此构建高效的双曲潜变量模型，在树状数据的度量恢复和大规模处理方面表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树状与层级数据适合嵌入双曲空间，因为双曲几何能在低维中较好地保留树形结构。但现有双曲高斯过程潜变量模型往往依赖数值积分、变分界或辅助变量，限制了模型的简洁性与规模扩展能力。论文旨在构造可直接计算的有限维特征表示，并降低此类模型的计算负担。

Method: 论文提出随机海尔戈森—傅里叶特征，将双曲空间上的核近似为显式的有限维特征映射；同时刻画特征频率的谱密度，使马特恩核等核函数可以直接在频率域中指定，并证明该特征映射具有一致近似误差界。基于这一表示，论文进一步构建双曲潜变量模型，无须数值求积、变分界或辅助变量。

Result: 在合成树数据上，该模型比所有基线方法更准确地恢复度量；其平均排名最佳，优势主要来自二维嵌入下对树状图谱数据的显著改进。此外，模型在505秒内完成了50万点数据的拟合。

Conclusion: 随机海尔戈森—傅里叶特征为双曲空间核方法提供了有理论保证且便于计算的显式近似，并支持高效的双曲潜变量建模。实验结果表明，该方法能兼顾树状结构保真度与大规模数据处理能力，是首个达到这一数据规模的非参数双曲潜变量模型。

Abstract: Hyperbolic geometry is well suited to embedding hierarchical data, capturing tree-like structure faithfully even in the low dimensions where Euclidean dimensionality reduction is severely constrained. We introduce Random Helgason--Fourier Features (RHFF), an explicit finite-dimensional feature map for kernels on hyperbolic space. We characterize the spectral density from which RHFF frequencies are drawn, allowing kernels such as the Matérn family to be specified directly in the frequency domain. We also prove a uniform approximation error bound for RHFF. Building on this representation, we develop a hyperbolic latent variable model called RHFF-LVM, which requires no numerical quadrature, variational bounds or auxiliary variables unlike previous hyperbolic GP-LVMs. Our RHFF-LVM recovers the metric of a synthetic tree more faithfully than all baselines and attains the best mean rank, driven by large gains on tree-structured atlases at $Q\!=\!2$. Furthermore, it fits $5\times10^{5}$ points in $505$ seconds, making it the first non-parametric hyperbolic LVM to reach this scale.

</details>


### [372] [Learning Temporal Dynamics with Derivative Representations for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16514)
*Shujie Duan,Jia Hong Puah,Haocheng Wu,Yifei Mao,Hongting Zhou,Chenxuan Li,Zitao Fang,Yang Lu,Sim Kuan Goh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种与模型架构无关的时间序列预测训练框架，通过联合学习序列及其离散时间导数，提升预测精度、时间一致性与动态合理性，且不增加推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习预测方法，尤其是基于注意力机制的模型，仍难以准确刻画系统动态随时间的演化。研究通常侧重于改进模型架构，而较少利用时间序列自身的时间结构设计训练策略，因此预测结果可能缺乏动态一致性。

Method: 提出动态感知预测框架，在输入区间和预测区间内，联合学习时间序列及其离散时间导数，并在统一框架中耦合这些表示，以促使预测维持时间一致性。该方法无需显式计算导数，可适配不同模型架构，也不会增加推理阶段的计算开销。作者指出，这种训练方式可形成一种离散泰勒一致性。

Result: 在多种基准数据集和不同骨干架构上的实验表明，该框架能够持续提升预测性能，并使预测结果比强基线方法更具动态连贯性和物理合理性。摘要未提供具体数据集、指标或数值提升幅度。

Conclusion: 通过离散导数表示强化动态一致性，是一种可与架构设计并行互补的通用预测范式。实验结果支持其在改善预测效果与动态合理性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Time series forecasting aims to predict the future evolution of complex systems by learning their underlying dynamics from past observations. Despite recent advances in deep learning, Transformers in particular still struggle to capture the temporal evolution of system dynamics. Existing approaches have been largely driven by architectural innovation, with comparatively little attention paid to training strategies that explicitly exploit the temporal structure governing system dynamics. In this work, we introduce DeltaTSF, a dynamics-aware forecasting framework based on discrete derivative representations of time series. Specifically, DeltaTSF jointly learns the series and their discrete temporal derivatives across both input and forecast horizons within a unified, model-agnostic framework. By coupling these representations over time, the model is encouraged to maintain temporal consistency, a property we show to induce a form of discrete Taylor consistency during training. This formulation enables the model to better reflect the underlying system dynamics, resulting in more stable and physically coherent forecasts. Notably, DeltaTSF does not require explicit derivative computation and introduces no additional computational overhead at inference time. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks and backbone architectures demonstrate that DeltaTSF consistently improves forecasting performance and yields more dynamically coherent predictions than strong SOTA methods. These results highlight the effectiveness of enforcing dynamic coherence through derivative representations as a model-agnostic paradigm that operates alongside and complements architectural design. Source code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [373] [HALight: Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Traffic Signal Control under Lane-Level Missing Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16574)
*Chenkai Hu,Yingqian Zhang,Ming Xie,Yi Fang,Chengxiang Jin,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向异构路口和车道级观测缺失的多智能体交通信号控制框架，通过邻域通信和顺序策略更新提升交通效率与学习稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
